local function repeatsKey(key)
    return key == 8 or key == 127 or (key >= 17 and key <= 20)
        or (key >= 32 and key <= 126) or (key >= 128 and key <= 137)
end


local MAXWELLNET = {
    capabilities = {
        api = 2, subsites = true, buttons = true, pointer = true, textFields = true, actionResults = true,
        navigation = true, scrolling = true, sounds = true, storageHelper = true,
        maxButtons = 64, maxFields = 8, maxCommands = 384, maxHeight = 1023
    }
}
function MAXWELLNET.clean(value, limit)
    return type(value) == "string" and value:sub(1, limit):gsub("%c", " ") or ""
end
function MAXWELLNET.identifier(value)
    return type(value) == "string" and #value > 0 and #value <= 40
        and value:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$") ~= nil
end
function MAXWELLNET.route(value)
    if value == nil or value == "" then return "home" end
    if type(value) == "string" and #value <= 64 and value:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_/-]+$") then return value end
    return "home"
end
function MAXWELLNET.extensions(options, height)
    local result = {inputs = {}, links = {}, missing = {}}
    for _, kind in ipairs({"inputs", "links"}) do
        local entries = type(options[kind]) == "table" and options[kind] or {}
        local seen = {}
        for i = 1, math.min(#entries, kind == "inputs" and 8 or 16) do
            local item = entries[i]
            if type(item) == "table" then
                local x, y, w, h = item[1], item[2], item[3], item[4]
                if type(x) == "number" and type(y) == "number" and type(w) == "number" and type(h) == "number"
                    and x >= 0 and y >= 0 and w >= 1 and h >= 1 and x + w <= 512 and y + h <= height then
                    if kind == "inputs" and MAXWELLNET.identifier(item[6]) and not seen[item[6]] then
                        seen[item[6]] = true
                        result.inputs[#result.inputs + 1] = {
                            math.floor(x), math.floor(y), math.floor(w), math.floor(h),
                            MAXWELLNET.clean(item[5], 32), item[6],
                            math.max(1, math.min(160, math.floor(tonumber(item[7]) or 80))),
                            MAXWELLNET.clean(item[8], 160)
                        }
                    elseif kind == "links" and type(item[6]) == "string" then
                        result.links[#result.links + 1] = {
                            math.floor(x), math.floor(y), math.floor(w), math.floor(h),
                            MAXWELLNET.clean(item[5], 32), MAXWELLNET.clean(item[6], 32), MAXWELLNET.route(item[7])
                        }
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if type(options.requires) == "table" then
        for i = 1, math.min(#options.requires, 8) do
            local feature = options.requires[i]
            if type(feature) == "string" and not MAXWELLNET.capabilities[feature] then
                result.missing[#result.missing + 1] = MAXWELLNET.clean(feature, 32)
            end
        end
    end
    return result
end
function MAXWELLNET.validFrame(frame)
    if type(frame) ~= "table" or #frame < 1 or #frame > 384 then return false end
    for _, command in ipairs(frame) do
        if type(command) ~= "table" or (command[1] ~= "rect" and command[1] ~= "text") then return false end
        for i = 2, 8 do
            local value = command[i]
            local limit = i >= 6 and 255 or (i == 3 and 1023 or 512)
            if type(value) ~= "number" or not (value >= 0 and value <= limit) then return false end
        end
        if command[2] + command[4] > 512 or command[3] + command[5] > 1023 then return false end
        if command[1] == "text" and (type(command[9]) ~= "string" or #command[9] > 96) then return false end
    end
    return true
end

MAXWELLNET.announcedSites={}
hook.add("remote", "maxwellnet_capabilities", function(sender, _, protocol, id, name)
    if protocol == "starfall_website_capabilities_v1" then return MAXWELLNET.capabilities end
    if SERVER and protocol=="starfall_website_register_v1" and sender and sender:isValid()
        and sender:getClass()=="starfall_processor" and id==sender:entIndex()
        and type(name)=="string" and #name>0 and #name<=32 then
        MAXWELLNET.announcedSites[id]={chip=sender,name=name,expires=timer.curtime()+10}
    end
end)

if SERVER then

-- Spawn one computer per player. Match NETWORK to the server chip.
local NETWORK = "lobby"
local PROTOCOL = "sf_chatroom_v2"
local server
local room
local roomVersion = ""
local typingNames = ""
local channelName = "General"
local roomNotice = ""
local sequence = 0
local nextDiscovery = 0
local history = {}
local ready = false
local nextPost = 0
local historyDirty = true
local available = {}
local directorySignature
local nextDirectoryRequest = 0
local wiredServer
local watchingGames = false
local gameVisible, hasWatchedMatch = false, false
local watchedVariant = "tic"
local gameRevision = -1
local gameNotice = ""
local gamePollStatus = ""
local paymentQuote
local watchingPrivate = false
local privateSignature
local paymentSequence = 0
local nextGameAction = 0
local nextChessHint = 0

local function sendHistory()
    if not historyDirty or not ready or not owner():isValid() then return end
    local bytes = 32 + #(room or "") + #roomNotice + #channelName + #typingNames
    for _, entry in ipairs(history) do bytes = bytes + #entry.line + 2 end
    if net.getBytesLeft() < bytes then return end
    net.start("room_history")
    net.writeString(room or "")
    net.writeString(roomVersion)
    net.writeUInt(#history, 5)
    for _, entry in ipairs(history) do
        net.writeString(entry.line)
        net.writeBool(entry.alert)
    end
    net.writeString(roomNotice)
    net.writeUInt(sequence, 32)
    net.writeString(channelName)
    net.writeString(typingNames)
    net.send(owner())
    historyDirty = false
end

local function request(target, action, value, extra)
    if not target or not target:isValid() then return end
    -- A processor can still exist after its script stops or is reloaded.
    local ok, results = pcall(hook.runRemote, target, PROTOCOL, NETWORK, action, value, extra)
    if ok then return results[1] end
end

local function syncGames()
    if not watchingGames or not server or not room or net.getBytesLeft() < 1200 then return end
    local snapshot
    if watchedVariant == "trail" and json then
        local trailReply = request(server, "trail_snapshot", gameRevision)
        if trailReply and type(trailReply[1]) == "string" then
            local ok, decoded = pcall(json.decode, trailReply[1])
            if ok and type(decoded) == "table" then snapshot = decoded end
        end
    end
    if not snapshot then
        local reply = request(server, "game_snapshot", gameRevision, watchedVariant)
        snapshot = reply and reply[1]
    end
    local pollStatus = ""
    if type(snapshot) ~= "table" then
        local legacy = request(server, "game_state", gameRevision, watchedVariant)
        if legacy and type(legacy[1]) == "number"
            and (legacy[4] == watchedVariant or watchedVariant == "tic" or legacy[1] == gameRevision) then
            snapshot = {variant = watchedVariant, revision = legacy[1], matches = legacy[2],
                current = legacy[3], notice = legacy[5]}
        elseif legacy then
            pollStatus = "This server does not support this game."
        elseif watchedVariant == "trail" and tonumber(roomVersion) and tonumber(roomVersion) < 21.9 then
            pollStatus = "Update the room server to play Oregon Trail."
        else
            pollStatus = "No game response from the server. Retrying..."
        end
    end
    if type(snapshot) == "table" and (snapshot.supported == false or snapshot.variant ~= watchedVariant) then
        pollStatus = "This server does not support this game."
    end
    local revision = gameRevision
    if pollStatus == "" and type(snapshot.revision) == "number" then
        revision = snapshot.revision
        if revision ~= gameRevision and type(snapshot.matches) ~= "table" then
            revision = gameRevision
            pollStatus = "Incomplete game response from the server. Retrying..."
        end
    elseif pollStatus == "" then
        pollStatus = "Invalid game response from the server. Retrying..."
    end
    local changed = revision ~= gameRevision and type(snapshot.matches) == "table"
    if changed then hasWatchedMatch = type(snapshot.current) == "table" end
    if not gameVisible and not hasWatchedMatch then
        watchingGames = false
        timer.remove("chatroom_games_poll")
    end
    if not changed and gameNotice == "" and pollStatus == gamePollStatus then return end
    net.start("room_games_state")
    net.writeTable({
        changed = changed,
        matches = changed and snapshot.matches or {},
        current = changed and snapshot.current or nil,
        variant = watchedVariant,
        notice = changed and type(snapshot.notice) == "string" and snapshot.notice ~= "" and snapshot.notice
            or (gameNotice ~= "" and gameNotice or pollStatus),
        clearNotice = pollStatus == "" and gamePollStatus ~= ""
    })
    net.send(owner())
    if changed then gameRevision = revision end
    gameNotice, gamePollStatus = "", pollStatus
end

local function discover()
    local now = timer.curtime()
    if not ready or not owner():isValid() or now < nextDiscovery then return end
    nextDiscovery = now + 2
    local directory = {}
    local detail = "Check that the server processor is running."
    local function add(candidate, name, passwordRequired, version)
        if not candidate or not candidate:isValid() or candidate:getClass() ~= "starfall_processor"
            or type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then return end
        directory[candidate:entIndex()] = {
            chip = candidate,
            name = name:gsub("%c", " "):sub(1, 40),
            passwordRequired = passwordRequired ~= false,
            version = type(version) == "string" and version:match("^%d%d?%d?%.%d$") or ""
        }
    end

    local function probe(candidate)
        local reply = request(candidate, "probe")
        if reply and type(reply[2]) == "string" then
            if reply[2] == NETWORK then
                add(candidate, reply[1], reply[3], reply[4])
            else
                detail = "Server network differs from " .. NETWORK:sub(1, 24) .. "."
            end
        else
            reply = request(candidate, "discover_index") or request(candidate, "discover")
            if reply then add(candidate, reply[2], reply[3], reply[4]) end
        end
    end

    if wiredServer and wiredServer:isValid() then
        detail = "Wired processor did not answer the chatroom probe."
        if wiredServer:getClass() == "starfall_processor" then probe(wiredServer) end
    end

    -- Probe actual processors so discovery also works when a broadcast has no replies.
    if find then
        local ok, processors = pcall(find.byClass, "starfall_processor")
        if ok then
            local self = chip()
            for _, candidate in ipairs(processors) do
                if candidate ~= self and candidate ~= wiredServer then probe(candidate) end
            end
        elseif not (wiredServer and wiredServer:isValid()) then
            detail = "Auto-scan unavailable. Wire RoomServer to ServerChip."
        end
    end

    local function collect(results)
        for _, result in ipairs(results) do
            local candidate, name = result[1], result[2]
            if type(candidate) == "number" then
                if candidate >= 1 and candidate <= 65535 and candidate % 1 == 0 then
                    candidate = entity(candidate)
                else
                    candidate = nil
                end
            elseif type(candidate) ~= "Entity" then
                candidate = nil
            end
            local existing = candidate and candidate:isValid() and directory[candidate:entIndex()]
            if not existing then add(candidate, name, result[3], result[4]) end
        end
    end
    local ok, results = pcall(hook.runRemote, nil, PROTOCOL, NETWORK, "discover_index")
    if ok then collect(results) end
    available = directory
    local ids = {}
    for id in pairs(directory) do ids[#ids + 1] = id end
    table.sort(ids)
    local signature = {}
    local count = math.min(#ids, 128)
    if count > 0 then detail = "" end
    local bytes = 32 + #detail
    for i = 1, count do
        local id = ids[i]
        local entry = directory[id]
        signature[i] = id .. ":" .. entry.version .. ":" .. tostring(entry.passwordRequired) .. ":" .. #entry.name .. ":" .. entry.name
        bytes = bytes + #entry.name + #entry.version + 5
    end
    signature = detail .. "\n" .. table.concat(signature)
    if signature == directorySignature or net.getBytesLeft() < bytes then return end
    net.start("room_directory")
    net.writeString(detail)
    net.writeUInt(count, 8)
    for i = 1, count do
        net.writeUInt(ids[i], 16)
        net.writeString(directory[ids[i]].name)
        net.writeBool(directory[ids[i]].passwordRequired)
        net.writeString(directory[ids[i]].version)
    end
    net.send(owner())
    directorySignature = signature
end

local function clearRoom(notice)
    server, room, sequence = nil, nil, 0
    roomVersion = ""
    channelName, typingNames = "General", ""
    roomNotice = notice or ""
    history = {}
    paymentQuote = nil
    watchingPrivate, privateSignature = false, nil
    watchingGames, gameVisible, hasWatchedMatch, gameRevision, gameNotice = false, false, false, -1, ""
    timer.remove("chatroom_games_poll")
    historyDirty = true
    nextDiscovery = 0
    sendHistory()
end

local function update()
    if not server then
        sendHistory()
        discover()
        return
    end

    local reply = request(server, "read", sequence)
    if reply and reply[1] == "filter_kicked" and reply[2] == nil then
        clearRoom("your message was blocked. You were kicked after 3 blocked messages.")
        return
    end
    if reply and reply[2] == nil and (reply[1] == "kicked" or reply[1] == "banned") then
        clearRoom("You were " .. reply[1] .. " from the chatroom.")
        return
    end
    local restarted = false
    if reply and type(reply[2]) == "number" and (reply[2] < sequence
        or (type(reply[5]) == "string" and reply[5] ~= channelName)) then
        sequence = 0
        history = {}
        restarted = true
        reply = request(server, "read", sequence)
    end
    if not reply or type(reply[1]) ~= "string" or type(reply[2]) ~= "number"
        or type(reply[3]) ~= "table" then
        clearRoom()
        return
    end

    local typing = type(reply[7]) == "string" and reply[7]:sub(1, 40) or ""
    historyDirty = historyDirty or restarted or room ~= reply[1] or sequence ~= reply[2] or typingNames ~= typing
    typingNames = typing
    room = reply[1]
    channelName = type(reply[5]) == "string" and reply[5] or "General"
    sequence = reply[2]
    local alerts = type(reply[4]) == "table" and reply[4] or {}
    for index, line in ipairs(reply[3]) do
        if type(line) == "string" then
            history[#history + 1] = {line = line, alert = alerts[index] == true}
            if #history > 24 then table.remove(history, 1) end
        end
    end
    sendHistory()
end

hook.add("ClientInitialized", "chatroom_history", function(ply)
    if ply ~= owner() then return end
    ready = true
    historyDirty = true
    directorySignature = nil
    nextDiscovery = 0
    sendHistory()
end)

wire.adjustInputs({"KeyboardKeys", "KeyboardUser", "ServerChip", "Power", "DashboardValue1","DashboardValue2","DashboardValue3","DashboardValue4","DashboardLabel1","DashboardLabel2","DashboardLabel3","DashboardLabel4"}, {"array", "entity", "entity", "number", "number","number","number","number","string","string","string","string"})
wiredServer = wire.ports.ServerChip
local heldKeys = {}
local pendingKeys, firstPendingKey, lastPendingKey = {}, 1, 0
local keyRetryScheduled = false
local keyboardActive = false
local sentKeyboardActive, sentPower
local wirePowered = true

local dashboardOutputs={0,0,0,0}
wire.adjustOutputs({"Control1","Control2","Control3","Control4"},{"number","number","number","number"})
local nextDashboard=0
net.receive("mos_dashboard_request",function(_,ply)
    if ply~=owner() or timer.curtime()<nextDashboard then return end
    nextDashboard=timer.curtime()+0.1
    local changing=net.readBool()
    if changing then
        local index,value=net.readUInt(3),net.readFloat()
        if not wirePowered or index<1 or index>4 or value~=value or math.abs(value)>1000000 then return end
        dashboardOutputs[index]=value;wire.ports["Control"..index]=value
    end
    if net.getBytesLeft()<512 then return end
    net.start("mos_dashboard_state")
    for i=1,4 do
        local value=tonumber(wire.ports["DashboardValue"..i]) or 0
        if value~=value or math.abs(value)>1000000 then value=0 end
        net.writeFloat(value)
        net.writeString(tostring(wire.ports["DashboardLabel"..i] or ("Input "..i)):sub(1,24))
        net.writeFloat(dashboardOutputs[i])
    end
    net.send(ply)
end)

local function syncKeyboard()
    if not ready or net.getBytesLeft() < 32 then return end
    if keyboardActive == sentKeyboardActive and wirePowered == sentPower then return end
    net.start("room_keyboard")
    net.writeBool(keyboardActive)
    net.writeBool(wirePowered)
    net.send(owner())
    sentKeyboardActive, sentPower = keyboardActive, wirePowered
end

local function flushKeyboard()
    if not ready or not wirePowered or not keyboardActive or firstPendingKey > lastPendingKey then return end
    local count = math.min(lastPendingKey - firstPendingKey + 1, 32, math.floor(net.getBytesLeft()) - 32)
    if count > 0 then
        net.start("room_keys")
        net.writeUInt(count, 9)
        for i = firstPendingKey, firstPendingKey + count - 1 do
            net.writeUInt(pendingKeys[i], 8)
            pendingKeys[i] = nil
        end
        firstPendingKey = firstPendingKey + count
        if firstPendingKey > lastPendingKey then firstPendingKey, lastPendingKey = 1, 0 end
        net.send(owner())
    end
    if firstPendingKey <= lastPendingKey and not keyRetryScheduled then
        keyRetryScheduled = true
        timer.simple(0.025, function()
            keyRetryScheduled = false
            flushKeyboard()
        end)
    end
end

local function syncWirePower()
    local powered = not wire.isConnected(chip(), "Power") or wire.ports.Power ~= 0
    if powered ~= wirePowered then
        wirePowered = powered
        heldKeys = {}
        pendingKeys, firstPendingKey, lastPendingKey = {}, 1, 0
        if not powered then gameVisible = false end
    end
    syncKeyboard()
end

hook.add("input", "chatroom_keyboard", function(port, value)
    if port == "Power" then
        syncWirePower()
        return
    end
    if port == "ServerChip" then
        wiredServer = value
        nextDiscovery = 0
        return
    end
    if port == "KeyboardUser" then
        local active = value == owner()
        if active ~= keyboardActive then
            keyboardActive = active
            heldKeys = {}
            pendingKeys, firstPendingKey, lastPendingKey = {}, 1, 0
        end
        syncKeyboard()
        return
    end
    if port ~= "KeyboardKeys" or not wirePowered or not keyboardActive then return end

    local current = {}
    for _, key in ipairs(value) do
        if type(key) == "number" and key >= 0 and key <= 255 and key % 1 == 0
            and not current[key] then
            current[key] = heldKeys[key] or (repeatsKey(key) and timer.curtime() + 0.38 or true)
            if ready and not heldKeys[key] and lastPendingKey - firstPendingKey < 127 then
                lastPendingKey = lastPendingKey + 1
                pendingKeys[lastPendingKey] = key
            end
        end
    end
    heldKeys = current
    flushKeyboard()
end)

hook.add("ClientInitialized", "chatroom_keyboard", function(ply)
    if ply ~= owner() then return end
    sentKeyboardActive = nil
    syncWirePower()
end)
local wireArcade = 0
net.receive("room_arcade_repeat", function(_, ply)
    if ply == owner() then wireArcade = net.readUInt(2) end
end)

timer.create("maxwellos_wire_repeat", 0.07, 0, function()
    if not ready or not wirePowered or not keyboardActive then return end
    local now = timer.curtime()
    for key, due in pairs(heldKeys) do
        local immediate = (wireArcade == 1 and (key == 19 or key == 20))
            or (wireArcade == 2 and (key == 17 or key == 18))
            or (wireArcade == 3 and (key == 32 or (key >= 17 and key <= 20)
                or key == 87 or key == 65 or key == 83 or key == 68
                or key == 119 or key == 97 or key == 115 or key == 100))
        if (wireArcade ~= 3 or immediate) and type(due) == "number" and (now >= due or (immediate and due - now > 0.07))
            and lastPendingKey-firstPendingKey<127 then
            heldKeys[key] = now + 0.07
            lastPendingKey = lastPendingKey + 1
            pendingKeys[lastPendingKey] = key
        end
    end
    flushKeyboard()
end)

timer.create("maxwellos_wire_held",0.1,0,function()
    if not ready or not keyboardActive or not wirePowered then return end
    local keys={}
    for key in pairs(heldKeys) do keys[#keys+1]=key end
    if net.getBytesLeft()<#keys+64 then return end
    net.start("room_held_keys")
    net.writeUInt(#keys,9)
    for _,key in ipairs(keys) do net.writeUInt(key,8) end
    net.send(owner())
end)

timer.create("chatroom_keyboard_sync", 0.5, 0, syncWirePower)


local nextCompanionRequest=0
local companionReplies={}
local function flushCompanionReply()
    local reply=companionReplies[1]
    if not reply or net.getBytesLeft()<#reply.data+80 then return end
    table.remove(companionReplies,1)
    net.start("mos_companion_reply")
    net.writeUInt(reply.ticket,16)
    net.writeBool(reply.ok)
    net.writeString(reply.data)
    net.send(owner())
end
hook.add("think","maxwellos_companion_replies",flushCompanionReply)
net.receive("mos_companion_request",function(_,ply)
    if ply~=owner() then return end
    local ticket,operation=net.readUInt(16),net.readUInt(1)
    local appId,targetId=net.readString(),net.readUInt(16)
    local action,encoded=net.readString(),net.readString()
    local hostedRole=net.readString()
    local function respond(ok,value)
        local success,data=pcall(json.encode,value)
        if not success or type(data)~="string" or #data>6000 then
            ok,data=false,json.encode({error="Companion returned unsupported or oversized data."})
        end
        if #companionReplies<16 then
            companionReplies[#companionReplies+1]={ticket=ticket,ok=ok,data=data}
            flushCompanionReply()
        end
    end
    if timer.curtime()<nextCompanionRequest then respond(false,{error="Companion is busy. Press R to reload and retry."});return end
    nextCompanionRequest=timer.curtime()+0.15
    if not appId:match("^[a-z0-9_-]+$") or #appId>32 or #action>40 or #encoded>2048 then
        respond(false,{error="Invalid companion request."});return
    end
    if operation==0 then
        local ok,replies=pcall(hook.runRemote,nil,"maxwellos_app_companion_discover_v1",appId)
        local list,seen=MOS_SERVICES.find(appId,action),{}
        for _,entry in ipairs(ok and type(replies)=="table" and replies or {}) do
            local id,role,name=entry[1],entry[2],entry[3]
            local target=type(id)=="number" and id>=1 and id<=65535 and id%1==0 and entity(id)
            if target and target:isValid() and target:getClass()=="starfall_processor" and not seen[id]
                and type(role)=="string" and type(name)=="string" and (action=="" or role==action) and #list<32 then
                seen[id]=true
                list[#list+1]={id=id,role=role:sub(1,40),name=name:sub(1,48)}
            end
        end
        table.sort(list,function(a,b) return tostring(a.id)<tostring(b.id) end)
        respond(true,{companions=list})
        return
    end
    if hostedRole~="" then
        local decoded,payload=pcall(json.decode,encoded)
        if not decoded then respond(false,{error="Invalid request data."});return end
        respond(MOS_SERVICES.call(appId,hostedRole,action,payload))
        return
    end
    local target=entity(targetId)
    if not target or not target:isValid() or target:getClass()~="starfall_processor" then
        respond(false,{error="Companion chip is unavailable."});return
    end
    local discovered,replies=pcall(hook.runRemote,target,"maxwellos_app_companion_discover_v1",appId)
    if not discovered or type(replies)~="table" or type(replies[1])~="table" or replies[1][1]~=targetId then
        respond(false,{error="The target does not support this app."});return
    end
    local decoded,payload=pcall(json.decode,encoded)
    if not decoded then respond(false,{error="Invalid request data."});return end
    local called,result=pcall(hook.runRemote,target,"maxwellos_app_companion_call_v1",appId,action,payload)
    local value=called and type(result)=="table" and type(result[1])=="table" and result[1][1]
    if value==nil then respond(false,{error="No companion response."})
    else respond(true,value) end
end)

net.receive("room_directory_request", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() then return end
    local now = timer.curtime()
    if now < nextDirectoryRequest then return end
    nextDirectoryRequest = now + 1
    ready = true
    historyDirty = true
    sentKeyboardActive = nil
    directorySignature = nil
    nextDiscovery = 0
    syncWirePower()
    update()
end)

local requestedWebsiteButtons, requestedWebsiteInputs
local nextWebsiteRequest, requestedWebsiteId, requestedWebsiteRoute = 0, 0, "home"
function MAXWELLNET.result(ply, id, result)
    if net.getBytesLeft() < 1024 then return end
    local response = {id = id, route = requestedWebsiteRoute}
    if type(result) == "table" then
        response.message = MAXWELLNET.clean(result.message, 160)
        response.status = result.ok == false and "error" or "success"
        response.sound = MAXWELLNET.clean(result.sound, 16)
        response.navigate = MAXWELLNET.clean(result.navigate, 32)
        response.nextRoute = type(result.route) == "string" and MAXWELLNET.route(result.route) or nil
    elseif type(result) == "string" then
        response.sound = result
    end
    net.start("room_website_result")
    net.writeTable(response)
    net.send(ply)
    local sounds = {click=1, success=2, error=3, splash=4, chime=5}
    if sounds[response.sound] and net.getBytesLeft() >= 64 then
        net.start("room_website_sound")
        net.writeUInt(id,16)
        net.writeUInt(sounds[response.sound],3)
        net.send(ply)
    end
end
function MAXWELLNET.values(values)
    local result = {}
    if type(values) ~= "table" then return result end
    for _, field in ipairs(requestedWebsiteInputs or {}) do
        local value = values[field[6]]
        if type(value) == "string" then result[field[6]] = MAXWELLNET.clean(value, field[7]) end
    end
    return result
end
local function websiteRequest(ply,id,route)
    if ply ~= owner() then return end
    local now = timer.curtime()
    if now < nextWebsiteRequest then return end
    nextWebsiteRequest = now + 0.4
    local sites, seen = {}, {}
    local function clean(value, limit)
        return type(value) == "string" and value:sub(1, limit):gsub("[^ -~]", " ") or ""
    end
    local function collect(results, target)
        if type(results) ~= "table" then return end
        for _, result in ipairs(results) do
            if type(result) == "table" and type(result[1]) == "number" then
                local candidate = target or (result[1] >= 1 and result[1] <= 65535
                    and result[1] % 1 == 0 and entity(result[1]))
                local name = clean(result[2], 32)
                if candidate and candidate:isValid() and candidate:getClass() == "starfall_processor" and name ~= "" then
                    local index = candidate:entIndex()
                    if not seen[index] then
                        local site = {id = index, name = name, chip = candidate}
                        seen[index] = site
                        sites[#sites + 1] = site
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function discover(processor)
        local ok, results = pcall(hook.runRemote, processor, "starfall_website_discover_v1")
        if ok then collect(results, processor) end
    end
    for siteId,entry in pairs(MAXWELLNET.announcedSites) do
        if timer.curtime()<entry.expires and entry.chip:isValid() then
            collect({{siteId,entry.name}},entry.chip)
        else MAXWELLNET.announcedSites[siteId]=nil end
    end
    if wiredServer and wiredServer:isValid() then discover(wiredServer) end
    if find then
        local ok, processors = pcall(find.byClass, "starfall_processor")
        if ok and type(processors) == "table" then
            local self = chip()
            for _, processor in ipairs(processors) do
                if processor ~= self and processor ~= wiredServer then discover(processor) end
            end
        end
    end
    discover(nil)
    table.sort(sites, function(first, second) return first.id < second.id end)

    local selected = seen[id]
    local page
    if selected then
        local ok, results = pcall(hook.runRemote, selected.chip, "starfall_website_page_v1", MAXWELLNET.capabilities, route)
        local result = ok and type(results) == "table" and results[1]
        if type(result) == "table" and type(result[4]) == "table" then
            local function bounded(value, lower, upper, default)
                if type(value) == "number" and value >= lower and value <= upper then return math.floor(value) end
                return default
            end
            local style = type(result[6]) == "table" and result[6] or {}
            local amounts = type(result[7]) == "table" and result[7] or {}
            page = {
                title = clean(result[3], 26), lines = {}, amounts = {},
                mode = result[5] == "demo_timeline_v1" and 1 or (result[5] == "donation_buttons_v1" and 2 or 0),
                face = clean(style[1], 40), size = bounded(style[2], 12, 64, 25),
                weight = bounded(style[3], 100, 1000, 500),
                uptime = bounded(type(result[8]) == "number" and result[8] * 10, 0, 4294967295, 0)
            }
            local options = type(result[9]) == "table" and result[9] or {}
            page.height = bounded(options[1], 512, 1023, 512)
            page.extensions = MAXWELLNET.extensions(options, page.height)
            page.buttons = {}
            local layout = type(options.buttons) == "table" and options.buttons
                or {{options[2], options[3], options[4], options[5], options[6], options[7]}}
            for i = 1, math.min(#layout, 64) do
                local item = layout[i]
                if type(item) == "table" then
                    local button = {
                        bounded(item[1], 0, 511, 0), bounded(item[2], 0, 1022, 0),
                        bounded(item[3], 1, 512, 0), bounded(item[4], 1, 512, 0),
                        clean(item[5], 60), ""
                    }
                    local action = item[6]
                    if not options.buttons and (button[5] == "generate_haiku_v1" or button[5] == "plinko_drop_v1") then
                        action = button[5]
                    end
                    if type(action) == "string" and #action <= 40 and action:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$") then
                        button[6] = action
                    end
                    if button[3] > 0 and button[4] > 0 and button[1] + button[3] <= 512
                        and button[2] + button[4] <= page.height and button[5] ~= "" then
                        page.buttons[#page.buttons + 1] = button
                    end
                end
            end
            for i = 1, math.min(#result[4], 5) do page.lines[i] = clean(result[4][i], 42) end
            for i = 1, 3 do page.amounts[i] = bounded(amounts[i], 1, 2147483647, 0) end
        end
    end
    local count = math.min(#sites, 32)
    local bytes = 16
    for i = 1, count do bytes = bytes + 4 + #sites[i].name end
    if page then
        -- Bound the actual table encoding instead of reserving 9 KB for every page.
        local function tableBytes(value)
            if type(value)=="string" then return #value+5 end
            if type(value)~="table" then return 10 end
            local size=4
            for key,item in pairs(value) do size=size+tableBytes(key)+tableBytes(item) end
            return size
        end
        bytes = bytes + 48 + #page.title + #page.face + #route + tableBytes(page.extensions)
        for _, button in ipairs(page.buttons) do bytes = bytes + 8 + #button[5] + #button[6] end
        for _, line in ipairs(page.lines) do bytes = bytes + #line + 1 end
    end
    if net.getBytesLeft() < bytes then
        -- Return discovery even when the selected page must wait for more budget.
        if not page then return end
        page=nil
        local directoryBytes=32
        for i=1,count do directoryBytes=directoryBytes+4+#sites[i].name end
        if net.getBytesLeft()<directoryBytes then return end
    end
    requestedWebsiteId = selected and id or 0
    requestedWebsiteRoute = route
    requestedWebsiteButtons = page and page.buttons
    requestedWebsiteInputs = page and page.extensions.inputs
    net.start("room_website_pages")
    net.writeUInt(count, 6)
    for i = 1, count do
        net.writeUInt(sites[i].id, 16)
        net.writeString(sites[i].name)
    end
    net.writeBool(page ~= nil)
    if page then
        net.writeUInt(id, 16)
        net.writeString(page.title)
        net.writeString(route)
        net.writeUInt(page.mode, 2)
        net.writeString(page.face)
        net.writeUInt(page.size, 7)
        net.writeUInt(page.weight, 10)
        net.writeUInt(page.height, 10)
        net.writeUInt(#page.buttons, 7)
        for _, button in ipairs(page.buttons) do
            for i = 1, 4 do net.writeUInt(button[i], 10) end
            net.writeString(button[5])
            net.writeString(button[6])
        end
        if page.mode == 1 then net.writeUInt(page.uptime, 32) end
        if page.mode == 2 then
            for i = 1, 3 do net.writeUInt(page.amounts[i], 31) end
        end
        net.writeUInt(#page.lines, 3)
        for _, line in ipairs(page.lines) do net.writeString(line) end
        net.writeTable(page.extensions)
    end
    net.send(ply)
    return true
end

-- Queue discovery requests when the network is busy, then send their replies.
local pendingWebsiteRequest
net.receive("room_website_request",function(_,ply)
    if ply~=owner() then return end
    pendingWebsiteRequest={user=ply,id=net.readUInt(16),route=MAXWELLNET.route(net.readString())}
end)
hook.add("think","maxwellnet_discovery_reply",function()
    local request=pendingWebsiteRequest
    if not request or timer.curtime()<nextWebsiteRequest or net.getBytesLeft()<128 then return end
    local ok,sent=pcall(websiteRequest,request.user,request.id,request.route)
    if not ok then
        if net.getBytesLeft()<256 then return end
        net.start("room_website_error")
        net.writeString("Website discovery failed: "..tostring(sent):sub(1,140))
        net.send(request.user)
        pendingWebsiteRequest=nil
    elseif sent then pendingWebsiteRequest=nil end
end)

local nextWebsiteButton = 0
net.receive("room_website_button", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or timer.curtime() < nextWebsiteButton then return end
    local id = net.readUInt(16)
    if id == 0 or id ~= requestedWebsiteId or not requestedWebsiteButtons then return end
    local site = entity(id)
    if not site or not site:isValid() or site:getClass() ~= "starfall_processor" then return end
    nextWebsiteButton = timer.curtime() + 0.5
    local action = net.readString()
    local values = MAXWELLNET.values(net.readTable())
    if #action > 40 or action == "" then return end
    for _, button in ipairs(requestedWebsiteButtons) do
        if button[6] == action then
            local ok, replies = pcall(hook.runRemote, site, "starfall_website_button_v1", action, values, MAXWELLNET.capabilities, requestedWebsiteRoute)
            local returned = ok and type(replies) == "table" and type(replies[1]) == "table" and replies[1][1]
            MAXWELLNET.result(ply, id, not ok and {ok=false, message="Website action failed."} or returned)
            return
        end
    end
end)

net.receive("room_website_input", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or timer.curtime() < nextWebsiteButton then return end
    local id, fieldId, text = net.readUInt(16), net.readString(), net.readString()
    if id ~= requestedWebsiteId or #text > 160 then return end
    local site = entity(id)
    if not site or not site:isValid() or site:getClass() ~= "starfall_processor" then return end
    for _, field in ipairs(requestedWebsiteInputs or {}) do
        if field[6] == fieldId then
            nextWebsiteButton = timer.curtime() + 0.5
            local ok, replies = pcall(hook.runRemote, site, "starfall_website_input_v1",
                fieldId, MAXWELLNET.clean(text, field[7]), MAXWELLNET.capabilities, requestedWebsiteRoute)
            local result = ok and type(replies) == "table" and type(replies[1]) == "table" and replies[1][1]
            MAXWELLNET.result(ply, id, not ok and {ok=false, message="Website input failed."} or result)
            return
        end
    end
end)

local nextWebsiteDonation = 0
net.receive("room_website_donate", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or timer.curtime() < nextWebsiteDonation or net.getBytesLeft() < 192 then return end
    nextWebsiteDonation = timer.curtime() + 1
    local id, button = net.readUInt(16), net.readUInt(2)
    local site = entity(id)
    if not site or not site:isValid() or site:getClass() ~= "starfall_processor" or button == 0 then return end
    local ok, results = pcall(hook.runRemote, site, "starfall_website_donation_v1", button)
    local result = ok and type(results) == "table" and type(results[1]) == "table" and results[1][1]
    net.start("room_website_donate_result")
    net.writeUInt(id, 16)
    net.writeString(type(result) == "string" and result:sub(1, 96) or "Donation site unavailable.")
    net.send(ply)
end)

-- Pointer events are scoped to the owner and their currently selected website.
local nextWebsitePointer = 0
net.receive("room_website_pointer", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or timer.curtime() < nextWebsitePointer then return end
    local id = net.readUInt(16)
    local x, y = net.readUInt(10), net.readUInt(10)
    local held, right = net.readBool(), net.readBool()
    if id ~= requestedWebsiteId or x > 511 or y > 1023 then return end
    local site = entity(id)
    if not site or not site:isValid() or site:getClass() ~= "starfall_processor" then return end
    -- Raw mouse state comes from the authenticated owner: control locking
    -- suppresses game attack/use flags while the browser has focus.
    nextWebsitePointer = timer.curtime() + 0.08
    pcall(hook.runRemote, site, "starfall_website_pointer_v1", x, y, held, right, MAXWELLNET.capabilities, requestedWebsiteRoute)
end)

local nextWebsiteFrameRequest = 0
local websiteFrameSound = {}
net.receive("room_website_frame_request", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or timer.curtime() < nextWebsiteFrameRequest then return end
    nextWebsiteFrameRequest = timer.curtime() + 0.2
    local id = net.readUInt(16)
    if id ~= requestedWebsiteId then return end
    local processor = entity(id)
    if not processor or not processor:isValid() or processor:getClass() ~= "starfall_processor" then return end
    local ok, results = pcall(hook.runRemote, processor, "starfall_website_screen_v1", MAXWELLNET.capabilities, requestedWebsiteRoute)
    local frame = ok and type(results) == "table" and type(results[1]) == "table" and results[1][1]
    if not MAXWELLNET.validFrame(frame) then
        MAXWELLNET.result(ply, id, {ok=false, message="Site returned an invalid frame or exceeded drawing limits."})
        return
    end
    local commands, bytes = {}, 32
    for _, command in ipairs(frame) do
        if type(command) ~= "table" or (command[1] ~= "rect" and command[1] ~= "text") then return end
        local copy = {command[1]}
        for i = 2, 8 do
            local number = command[i]
            if type(number) ~= "number" or not (number >= 0 and number <= (i == 3 and 1023 or 512)) then return end
            copy[i] = number
        end
        if command[1] == "text" then
            if type(command[9]) ~= "string" or #command[9] > 96 then return end
            copy[9] = command[9]
        end
        commands[#commands + 1] = copy
        bytes = bytes + 16 + #(copy[9] or "")
    end
    if net.getBytesLeft() < bytes then return end
    net.start("room_website_frame")
    net.writeUInt(id, 16)
    net.writeString(requestedWebsiteRoute)
    net.writeUInt(#commands, 9)
    for _, command in ipairs(commands) do
        net.writeBool(command[1] == "text")
        for i = 2, 5 do net.writeUInt(math.floor(command[i] + 0.5), 10) end
        for i = 6, 8 do net.writeUInt(math.min(255, math.floor(command[i] + 0.5)), 8) end
        if command[1] == "text" then net.writeString(command[9]) end
    end
    net.send(ply)
    local cue, token = results[1][2], results[1][3]
    local soundId = cue == "drop" and 1 or (cue == "win" and 5 or nil)
    if soundId and type(token) == "string" and #token <= 48 and websiteFrameSound[id] ~= token
        and net.getBytesLeft() >= 32 then
        websiteFrameSound[id] = token
        net.start("room_website_sound")
        net.writeUInt(id, 16)
        net.writeUInt(soundId, 3)
        net.send(ply)
    end
end)

local nextJoin = 0
net.receive("room_join", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or not ready or net.getBytesLeft() < 128 then return end
    local id, password, name = net.readUInt(16), net.readString(), net.readString()
    local entry = available[id]
    local now = timer.curtime()
    local result = "Server unavailable. Choose another server."
    if now < nextJoin then
        result = "Wait a second before trying again."
    elseif not server and entry and #password <= 64 then
        nextJoin = now + 0.5
        local reply
        if #name <= 24 and name:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$") then
            reply = request(entry.chip, "join", password, name)
        else
            reply = {"Invalid computer name."}
        end
        if reply and type(reply[1]) == "string" then
            result = reply[1]:sub(1, 100)
            if result == "ok" then
                server = entry.chip
                roomVersion = entry.version
                roomNotice = ""
            end
        end
    end
    net.start("room_join_result")
    net.writeString(result)
    net.send(ply)
    if result == "ok" then update() end
end)

local nextRename = 0
net.receive("room_rename", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or not ready or not server or net.getBytesLeft() < 160 then return end
    local now = timer.curtime()
    if now < nextRename then return end
    nextRename = now + 0.5
    local name = net.readString()
    local result = "Invalid computer name."
    if #name <= 24 and name:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$") then
        local reply = request(server, "rename", name)
        result = reply and type(reply[1]) == "string" and reply[1]:sub(1, 96) or "This room could not rename your computer."
    end
    net.start("room_rename_result")
    net.writeString(result)
    net.writeString(result == "ok" and name or "")
    net.send(ply)
end)

net.receive("room_leave", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or not ready or not server then return end
    request(server, "leave")
    clearRoom()
end)

local nextTyping = 0
net.receive("room_typing", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or not server then return end
    local active = net.readBool()
    local now = timer.curtime()
    if active and now < nextTyping then return end
    nextTyping = active and now + 0.75 or 0
    request(server, "typing", active)
end)

do
    local nextPainting = 0
    net.receive("room_painting", function(_, ply)
        if ply ~= owner() or not ready or not server or timer.curtime() < nextPainting then return end
        nextPainting = timer.curtime() + 1
        local sending = net.readBool()
        if net.getBytesLeft() < (sending and 128 or 2600) then return end
        local reply = request(server, sending and "painting_post" or "painting_latest", sending and net.readString() or nil)
        local message = reply and type(reply[1]) == "string" and reply[1] or "Painting sharing requires an updated room."
        local pixels = reply and reply[2]
        net.start("room_painting_result")
        net.writeString(message:sub(1, 100))
        net.writeString(type(pixels) == "string" and #pixels == 2500 and pixels or "")
        net.writeString(channelName)
        net.send(ply)
    end)
end

net.receive("room_post", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or not ready then return end
    local now = timer.curtime()
    if now < nextPost then return end
    nextPost = now + 0.25

    local message = net.readString()
    if net.getBytesLeft() < 128 then return end
    local result
    if message:lower():match("^%s*/pm%f[%s%z]") then
        result = "Private messaging has been removed."
    else
        local reply = #message <= 160 and request(server, "post", message)
        result = reply and tostring(reply[1]) or "Room unavailable. Try again."
    end
    net.start("room_result")
    net.writeString(result)
    net.send(ply)
end)

local function requestPaymentFrom(payer, amount)
    local api = economy and economy.requestMoney and economy or (darkrp and darkrp.requestMoney and darkrp)
    if not api then return "The in-game payment API is unavailable." end
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        if api.canMakeMoneyRequest and not api.canMakeMoneyRequest(payer) then
            return "A payment request is pending or on cooldown."
        end
        if api.canAfford and not api.canAfford(payer, amount) then return "You cannot afford this payment." end
        api.requestMoney(payer, amount, "Chatroom payment", nil, nil, nil)
        return "ok"
    end)
    return ok and result or "Payment prompt failed. Check Starfall permissions."
end

hook.add("remote", "chatroom_payment_request", function(sender, _, protocol, network, action, payer, amount)
    if not room or sender ~= server or protocol ~= PROTOCOL or network ~= NETWORK
        or action ~= "payment_receive_request" then return end
    if type(payer) ~= "Player" or not payer:isValid() or payer == owner()
        or type(amount) ~= "number" or amount < 1 or amount > 999999999 or amount % 1 ~= 0 then
        return "Invalid payment request."
    end
    return requestPaymentFrom(payer, amount)
end)

local nextContractRequest=0
net.receive("room_contracts",function(_,ply)
    if ply~=owner() or not room or not server or net.getBytesLeft()<15000 or timer.curtime()<nextContractRequest then return end
    nextContractRequest=timer.curtime()+0.2
    local action,value=net.readString(),net.readTable()
    if action~="contracts_list" and action~="contracts_post" and action~="contracts_accept" and action~="contracts_cancel" then return end
    local reply=request(server,action,action=="contracts_post" and value or value.id)
    net.start("room_contracts_state")
    net.writeTable(reply and type(reply[1])=="table" and reply[1] or {notice="Contracts unavailable on this server."})
    net.send(ply)
end)

local function paymentResult(ply, data)
    net.start("room_payment_state")
    net.writeTable(data)
    net.send(ply)
end

net.receive("room_payment", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or not room or not server or net.getBytesLeft() < 256 then return end
    local action, id, amount = net.readUInt(2), net.readUInt(32), net.readUInt(32)
    if action == 3 then
        paymentQuote = nil
        return
    end
    if action == 0 then
        local reply = request(server, "payment_list")
        if not reply or type(reply[1]) ~= "table" then
            paymentResult(ply, {notice = "This room server did not provide a payment list."})
            return
        end
        local recipients = {}
        local bytes = 128
        for i = 1, math.min(#reply[1], 128) do
            local entry = reply[1][i]
            if type(entry) == "table" and type(entry.id) == "number"
                and type(entry.name) == "string" and type(entry.playerName) == "string" then
                recipients[#recipients + 1] = {
                    id = entry.id, name = entry.name:sub(1, 24), playerName = entry.playerName:gsub("%c", " "):sub(1, 48)
                }
                local recipient = recipients[#recipients]
                bytes = bytes + #recipient.name + #recipient.playerName + 64
            end
        end
        if net.getBytesLeft() < bytes then
            paymentResult(ply, {notice = "Connection busy. Press R to refresh."})
            return
        end
        paymentResult(ply, {recipients = recipients})
        return
    end
    if action == 1 then
        paymentQuote = nil
        if amount < 1 or amount > 999999999 then
            paymentResult(ply, {notice = "Enter a positive whole-dollar amount."})
            return
        end
        local reply = request(server, "payment_target", id)
        local recipient = reply and reply[1]
        if type(recipient) ~= "Player" or not recipient:isValid() or recipient == ply then
            paymentResult(ply, {notice = "Recipient unavailable. Press R to refresh."})
            return
        end
        paymentSequence = paymentSequence + 1
        local name = recipient:getName():gsub("%c", " "):sub(1, 48)
        paymentQuote = {token = paymentSequence, id = id, recipient = recipient, amount = amount,
            expires = timer.curtime() + 20}
        paymentResult(ply, {quote = {token = paymentSequence, name = name, amount = amount}})
        return
    end
    if action ~= 2 then return end
    local quote = paymentQuote
    if not quote or quote.token ~= id then return end
    paymentQuote = nil
    if timer.curtime() >= quote.expires then
        paymentResult(ply, {finished = true, notice = "Confirmation expired. Review the payment again."})
        return
    end
    local reply = request(server, "payment_target", quote.id)
    if not reply or reply[1] ~= quote.recipient or not quote.recipient:isValid() then
        paymentResult(ply, {finished = true, notice = "Recipient unavailable. No payment was sent."})
        return
    end
    local response = request(server, "payment_request", quote.id, quote.amount)
    local result = response and response[1]
    local notice = "The server did not respond to the payment request."
    if result == "ok" then notice = "Payment prompt opened. Accept it in-game to pay."
    elseif type(result) == "string" then notice = result end
    paymentResult(ply, {finished = true, notice = notice})
end)

local function decodePrivateSnapshot(payload)
    if type(payload) ~= "string" or payload:sub(1, 4) ~= "PM1\n" then return end
    local lines = {}
    local first = 5
    while first <= #payload do
        local stop = payload:find("\n", first, true)
        lines[#lines + 1] = payload:sub(first, stop and stop - 1 or #payload)
        if not stop then break end
        first = stop + 1
    end
    local revision, count = tonumber(lines[1]), tonumber(lines[2])
    if not revision or not count or count < 0 or count > #lines - 2 or count % 1 ~= 0 then return end
    local snapshot = {revision = revision, recipients = {}, messages = {}}
    for i = 3, count + 2 do
        local line = lines[i]
        local colon = line:find(":", 1, true)
        local id = colon and tonumber(line:sub(1, colon - 1))
        if not id then return end
        snapshot.recipients[#snapshot.recipients + 1] = {id = id, name = line:sub(colon + 1)}
    end
    for i = count + 3, #lines do snapshot.messages[#snapshot.messages + 1] = lines[i] end
    return snapshot
end

local function syncPrivate()
    if not watchingPrivate or not room or net.getBytesLeft() < 256 then return end
    local reply = request(server, "private_snapshot")
    local snapshot = reply and decodePrivateSnapshot(reply[1])
    if not snapshot then
        local legacy = request(server, "private_state")
        if legacy and type(legacy[1]) == "table" then snapshot = legacy[1] end
    end
    local signature = {"unavailable"}
    local bytes = 128
    if type(snapshot) == "table" then
        signature = {tostring(snapshot.revision)}
        for _, entry in ipairs(snapshot.recipients) do
            signature[#signature + 1] = tostring(entry.id)
            bytes = bytes + #entry.name + 32
        end
        for _, message in ipairs(snapshot.messages) do bytes = bytes + #message + 12 end
    else
        local version = tonumber(roomVersion)
        snapshot = {notice = version and version < 19.3 and "This server predates private messaging."
            or "No private inbox reply. Retrying..."}
    end
    signature = table.concat(signature, ",")
    if signature == privateSignature or net.getBytesLeft() < bytes then return end
    net.start("room_private_state")
    net.writeTable(snapshot)
    net.send(owner())
    privateSignature = signature
end

net.receive("room_private_action", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or not room then return end
    local command = net.readUInt(2)
    if command == 0 then
        watchingPrivate, privateSignature = false, nil
    elseif command == 1 then
        watchingPrivate, privateSignature = true, nil
        syncPrivate()
    elseif command == 2 and watchingPrivate and net.getBytesLeft() >= 256 then
        local recipient, message = net.readUInt(32), net.readString()
        local reply = #message <= 160 and request(server, "private_send", message, recipient)
        net.start("room_private_result")
        net.writeString(reply and tostring(reply[1]) or "Private message could not be sent.")
        net.send(ply)
        syncPrivate()
    end
end)
timer.create("chatroom_private_poll", 0.75, 0, syncPrivate)

net.receive("room_channel_action", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or not room or net.getBytesLeft() < 512 then return end
    local id = net.readUInt(32)
    if id == 0 then
        local reply = request(server, "channel_directory") or request(server, "channel_list")
        net.start("room_channel_list")
        net.writeString(reply and type(reply[1]) == "string" and reply[1]:sub(1, 480) or "")
        net.send(ply)
    else
        local reply = request(server, "channel_join", id)
        local result = reply and reply[1] or "The server did not respond."
        net.start("room_channel_result")
        net.writeString(tostring(result):sub(1, 100))
        net.send(ply)
        if result == "ok" then update() end
    end
end)

timer.create("chatroom_poll", 0.5, 0, update)

local gameActions = {[2] = "game_create", [3] = "game_join", [4] = "game_move", [5] = "game_leave", [6] = "game_rematch"}
net.receive("room_games_action", function(_, ply)
    if ply ~= owner() or not room then return end
    local command, value = net.readUInt(3), net.readUInt(32)
    if command == 0 then
        gameVisible = false
        syncGames()
    elseif command == 1 then
        local variant = ({[1] = "chess", [2] = "battleship", [3] = "trail", [4] = "pong"})[value] or "tic"
        if hasWatchedMatch and variant ~= watchedVariant then return end
        watchingGames, gameVisible, gameRevision, gameNotice = true, true, -1, ""
        gamePollStatus = ""
        watchedVariant = variant
        timer.create("chatroom_games_poll", variant == "pong" and 0.12 or 0.5, 0, syncGames)
        syncGames()
    elseif command == 7 and watchingGames and watchedVariant == "chess"
        and gameRevision >= 0 and timer.curtime() >= nextChessHint and net.getBytesLeft() >= 256 then
        nextChessHint = timer.curtime() + 0.1
        local reply = request(server, "game_hint", value, "chess")
        if not reply or type(reply[3]) ~= "table" then return end
        net.start("room_chess_hints")
        net.writeTable({id = reply[1], board = reply[2], from = value, hints = reply[3]})
        net.send(ply)
    elseif watchingGames and gameActions[command] and timer.curtime() >= nextGameAction then
        if watchedVariant ~= "tic" and (gameRevision < 0 or gamePollStatus ~= "") then
            syncGames()
            return
        end
        nextGameAction = timer.curtime() + (watchedVariant == "pong" and 0.05 or 0.15)
        local reply = request(server, gameActions[command], value, watchedVariant)
        if reply and reply[1] == "ok" and (command == 2 or command == 3) then hasWatchedMatch = true end
        if reply and type(reply[1]) == "string" and reply[1] ~= "ok" then
            gameNotice = reply[1]:sub(1, 100)
        end
        syncGames()
    end
end)
return
end

local isOwner = player() == owner()

local updates = {latest = nil}

function updates.check()
    if not isOwner or not http or not http.get then return end
    if http.canRequest and not http.canRequest() then return end
    local url = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Mooseyboi30/MaxwellOS/main/starfall_chatroom_computer.txt"
    pcall(http.get, url, function(body, _, _, code)
        if code ~= 200 or type(body) ~= "string" then return end
        local version = body:sub(1, 1024):match('CHIP_VERSION = "(%d%d?%d?%.%d)"')
        if version then
            updates.latest = tonumber(version) > tonumber(CHIP_VERSION) and version or nil
        end
    end)
end

if isOwner then
    timer.simple(2, updates.check)
    timer.create("maxwellos_update_check", 60, 0, updates.check)
    hook.add("permissionrequest", "maxwellos_update_check", updates.check)
end

local PHONE = {open = false, unread = 0, alertUntil = 0, lockNotice = "", lockNoticeUntil = 0}
local room = ""
local roomVersion = ""
local channelName = "General"
local showChannels = false
local calculator = {entry = "", result = false}
local CALCULATOR_KEYS = {
    "sin", "cos", "tan", "sqrt", "x^2", "log",
    "7", "8", "9", "/", "^", "ln",
    "4", "5", "6", "*", "%", "pi",
    "1", "2", "3", "-", "+/-", "e",
    "0", ".", "=", "+", "Angle", "1/x"
}
local channelNames, selectedChannel = {}, 1
local channelIds = {}
local channelStatus = ""
local channelWaiting
local lastSequence
local computerName = ""
local nameDraft = ""
local showChatHUD = false
local showPayments = false
local showPrivate = false
local privateRecipients, privateRows = {}, {}
local privateSelection, privateScroll = 0, 0
local privateDraft, privateStatus = "", ""
local privateWaiting
local paymentRecipients = {}
local paymentSelection = 1
local paymentAmount = ""
local paymentStatus = ""
local paymentReview
local paymentWaiting
local gameTab = "menu"
local poweredOn = true
local startupUntil = 0
local lastApp = false
local selectedGame = 1
local websitePages, selectedWebsite = {}, 1
local websiteLoaded = false
local websiteFrame, websiteFrameSite
local startWebsitePolling
local websiteControls = {message = ""}
websiteControls.history, websiteControls.bookmarks, websiteControls.drafts = {}, {}, {}
websiteControls.bookmarkSelection = 1

function websiteControls.values(page)
    local key = page.id .. ":" .. (page.route or "home")
    local values = websiteControls.drafts[key]
    if not values then
        local count = 0
        for _ in pairs(websiteControls.drafts) do count = count + 1 end
        if count >= 32 then websiteControls.drafts = {} end
        values = {}
        websiteControls.drafts[key] = values
    end
    for _, field in ipairs((page.extensions or {}).inputs or {}) do
        if values[field[6]] == nil then values[field[6]] = field[8]:sub(1, field[7]) end
    end
    return values
end

function websiteControls.loadBookmarks()
    if websiteControls.bookmarksLoaded or not isOwner or not file or not file.exists then return end
    if timer.curtime() < (websiteControls.nextBookmarkLoad or 0) then return end
    websiteControls.nextBookmarkLoad = timer.curtime() + 3
    local ok, exists = pcall(file.exists, "maxwellnet_bookmarks.json")
    if not ok then return end
    local bookmarks = {}
    if exists then
        local read, data = pcall(file.read, "maxwellnet_bookmarks.json")
        local decoded, value = false, nil
        if read and type(data) == "string" and #data <= 16000 then decoded, value = pcall(json.decode, data) end
        if not decoded or type(value) ~= "table" then
            websiteControls.message = "Bookmarks could not be loaded."
            return
        end
        for i = 1, math.min(#value, 32) do
            local entry = value[i]
            if type(entry) == "table" and type(entry.name) == "string" then
                bookmarks[#bookmarks+1] = {id=tonumber(entry.id), name=entry.name:sub(1,32), route=MAXWELLNET.route(entry.route)}
            end
        end
    end
    websiteControls.bookmarks, websiteControls.bookmarksLoaded = bookmarks, true
end

function websiteControls.go(name, route, remember, targetId)
    local current = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
    route = MAXWELLNET.route(route)
    local preferred = targetId or (current and current.name == name and current.id)
    local found = false
    for _, page in ipairs(websitePages) do
        if page.name == name and page.id == preferred then found = true; break end
    end
    if not found then preferred = nil end
    for index, page in ipairs(websitePages) do
        if page.name == name and (not preferred or page.id == preferred) then
            if remember ~= false and current and (current.id ~= page.id or (current.route or "home") ~= route) then
                local history = websiteControls.history
                history[#history+1] = {id=current.id, name=current.name, route=current.route or "home"}
                if #history > 32 then table.remove(history, 1) end
            end
            selectedWebsite = index
            page.route, page.title, page.extensions = route, nil, nil
            page.loadedAt, page.frameAt = timer.curtime(), nil
            websiteFrame, websiteFrameSite = nil, nil
            websiteControls.focus, websiteControls.showBookmarks, websiteControls.waitUntil = nil, false, nil
            websiteControls.message = ""
            websiteControls.resultStatus = nil
            return true
        end
    end
    websiteControls.message = "Linked site is unavailable. Refresh discovery and try again."
    return false
end

function websiteControls.retry()
    local page=websitePages[selectedWebsite]
    if page then websiteControls.go(page.name,page.route,false,page.id) end
    websiteControls.startedAt=timer.curtime()
    websiteControls.message=""
    websiteControls.discoveryError=false
    if net.getBytesLeft()<128 then websiteControls.message="Connection busy. Try Retry shortly."; return end
    net.start("room_website_request")
    net.writeUInt(page and page.id or 0,16)
    net.writeString(page and page.route or "home")
    net.send()
end

function websiteControls.back()
    local history = websiteControls.history
    local previous = history[#history]
    if previous and websiteControls.go(previous.name, previous.route, false, previous.id) then table.remove(history) end
end

function websiteControls.bookmark()
    websiteControls.loadBookmarks()
    if not websiteControls.bookmarksLoaded then websiteControls.message="Grant file access to save bookmarks."; return end
    local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
    if not page then return end
    local bookmarks, match = websiteControls.bookmarks, nil
    for i, entry in ipairs(bookmarks) do
        if entry.name == page.name and entry.route == (page.route or "home") then match=i; break end
    end
    local updated = {}
    for i, entry in ipairs(bookmarks) do if i ~= match then updated[#updated+1] = entry end end
    if not match then
        if #updated >= 32 then websiteControls.message="Bookmark limit reached (32)."; return end
        updated[#updated+1] = {id=page.id, name=page.name, route=page.route or "home"}
    end
    local ok = pcall(file.write, "maxwellnet_bookmarks.json", json.encode(updated))
    if ok then
        websiteControls.bookmarks = updated
        websiteControls.message = match and "Bookmark removed." or "Bookmark saved."
    else websiteControls.message = "Bookmark save failed. Check file permission." end
end

function websiteControls.submit(page, field)
    local value = websiteControls.values(page)[field[6]] or ""
    if net.getBytesLeft() < 256 then websiteControls.message="Connection busy. Try again."; return end
    net.start("room_website_input")
    net.writeUInt(page.id, 16)
    net.writeString(field[6])
    net.writeString(value)
    net.send()
    websiteControls.message = "Submitting..."
    websiteControls.waitUntil = timer.curtime() + 4
end

function websiteControls.key(key, enter, character)
    local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
    if websiteControls.showBookmarks then
        if key == 17 then websiteControls.bookmarkSelection=math.max(1,websiteControls.bookmarkSelection-1)
        elseif key == 18 then websiteControls.bookmarkSelection=math.min(#websiteControls.bookmarks,websiteControls.bookmarkSelection+1)
        elseif enter then
            local entry=websiteControls.bookmarks[websiteControls.bookmarkSelection]
            if entry then websiteControls.go(entry.name,entry.route,true,entry.id) end
        elseif key == 8 or key == 127 or key == 107 or key == 27 then websiteControls.showBookmarks=false end
        return true
    end
    if page and page.extensions then
        local fields = page.extensions.inputs
        if (key == 9 or (key == 105 and not websiteControls.focus)) and #fields > 0 then
            local selected = 0
            for i, field in ipairs(fields) do if field[6]==websiteControls.focus then selected=i end end
            local field=fields[selected % #fields + 1]
            websiteControls.focus=field[6]
            page.scroll=math.max(0,math.min(page.height-512,field[2]-24))
            return true
        end
        for _, field in ipairs(fields) do
            if field[6] == websiteControls.focus then
                local values = websiteControls.values(page)
                if key == 27 then websiteControls.focus=nil
                elseif enter then websiteControls.submit(page,field)
                elseif key == 8 or key == 127 then values[field[6]]=values[field[6]]:sub(1,-2)
                elseif character and #values[field[6]] + #character <= field[7] then
                    values[field[6]]=values[field[6]]..character
                end
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    if key==98 or key==66 then websiteControls.bookmark()
    elseif key==104 or key==72 then websiteControls.back()
    elseif key==107 or key==75 then
        websiteControls.loadBookmarks()
        websiteControls.showBookmarks=true
    elseif key==114 or key==82 then websiteControls.retry()
    else return false end
    return true
end

net.receive("room_website_result", function()
    local result = net.readTable()
    local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
    if (gameTab ~= "website" and not (PHONE.tools and PHONE.tools.webVisible())) or not page or page.id ~= result.id or (page.route or "home") ~= result.route then return end
    websiteControls.waitUntil = nil
    websiteControls.resultStatus = result.status
    websiteControls.message = result.message or ""
    if result.navigate and result.navigate ~= "" then
        websiteControls.go(result.navigate,result.nextRoute)
    elseif result.nextRoute then
        websiteControls.go(page.name,result.nextRoute)
    end
end)


function websiteControls.scroll(delta)
    local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
    if not page or not page.height then return end
    page.scroll = math.max(0, math.min(page.height - 512, (page.scroll or 0) + delta))
    websiteControls.message = ""
end

function websiteControls.activateButton(x, y)
    local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
    if not page or not page.buttons or not websiteFrame or websiteFrameSite ~= page.id then return end
    if x and y then
        local extensions = page.extensions or {inputs={}, links={}}
        for _, kind in ipairs({"inputs", "links"}) do
            for _, item in ipairs(extensions[kind]) do
                local top = item[2] - (page.scroll or 0)
                if x >= item[1] and x < item[1]+item[3] and y >= top and y < top+item[4] then
                    if kind == "inputs" then websiteControls.focus=item[6]
                    else websiteControls.go(item[6] ~= "" and item[6] or page.name,item[7]) end
                    return
                end
            end
        end
        websiteControls.focus = nil
    end
    for _, button in ipairs(page.buttons) do
        local top = button[2] - (page.scroll or 0)
        local inside = x and x >= button[1] and x < button[1] + button[3]
            and y >= 0 and y < 512 and y >= top and y < top + button[4]
        if inside or (not x and top >= 0 and top + button[4] <= 512) then
            if button[6] == "" then
                websiteControls.message = button[5]
            elseif net.getBytesLeft() >= 2400 then
                net.start("room_website_button")
                net.writeUInt(page.id, 16)
                net.writeString(button[6])
                net.writeTable(websiteControls.values(page))
                net.send()
                websiteControls.waitUntil = timer.curtime() + 4
                websiteControls.message = "Waiting for website..."
            end
            return
        end
    end
end

local function requestWebsiteDonation(slot)
    local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
    if not page or not page.donation or (page.amounts[slot] or 0) == 0 then return end
    if net.getBytesLeft() < 32 then
        websiteControls.message = "Connection busy. Try again shortly."
        return
    end
    net.start("room_website_donate")
    net.writeUInt(page.id, 16)
    net.writeUInt(slot, 2)
    net.send()
end

local function stepWebsite(delta)
    if #websitePages < 2 then return end
    local index = (selectedWebsite - 1 + delta) % #websitePages + 1
    local page = websitePages[index]
    websiteControls.go(page.name, page.route or "home", true, page.id)
end

function PHONE.desktopScroll(delta, follow)

    local maxTop=math.max(0,math.ceil(#menuOptions/rows)-columns)
    if follow then
        local column=math.floor((math.max(1,selectedGame)-1)/rows)
        top=math.max(column-columns+1,math.min(top,column))
    else top=top+delta end
    PHONE.desktopTop=math.max(0,math.min(maxTop,top))
end

local function playChatSound(cue)
    if not isOwner then return end
    if PHONE.tools and PHONE.tools.dnd() and cue=="mention" then return end
    local audio = AUDIO[cue]
    pcall(function()
        if sound and sound.emitSound then
            sound.emitSound(audio[1], eyePos(), 65, 100, audio[2])
        else
            player():emitSound(audio[1], 65, 100, audio[2])
        end
    end)
end

local function isMentionNameByte(byte)
    return byte and ((byte >= 97 and byte <= 122) or (byte >= 48 and byte <= 57) or byte == 45 or byte == 95)
end

local function messageMentions(message, name)
    if name == "" then return false end
    local colon = message:find(": ", 1, true)
    if not colon then return false end
    local body = message:sub(colon + 2):lower()
    local wanted = name:lower()
    local from = 1
    while true do
        local at = body:find("@", from, true)
        if not at then return false end
        if (at == 1 or not isMentionNameByte(body:byte(at - 1)))
            and body:sub(at + 1, at + #wanted) == wanted
            and not isMentionNameByte(body:byte(at + #wanted + 1)) then
            return true
        end
        from = at + 1
    end
end

local settings = {theme = 1, themeTop = 1, page = "color", name = "", message = ""}
local themeConfigured = false
local THEME_TOP = 100
local THEME_STEP = 34
local THEME_HEIGHT = 30

function settings.saveProfile()
    if not isOwner or not file or not file.write then return end
    local ok = pcall(file.write, "maxwellos_profile.txt", settings.theme .. "\n" .. computerName)
    settings.storageError = not ok
end

function themes.move(delta)
    settings.theme = math.max(1, math.min(#themes, settings.theme + delta))
    settings.themeTop = math.max(settings.theme - 8, math.min(settings.themeTop, settings.theme))
end

local function applyTheme()
    local theme = themes[settings.theme]
    background, panel, accent = theme.background, theme.panel, theme.accent
    ink, muted, alertColor = theme.ink or themes.defaultInk, theme.muted or themes.defaultMuted, theme.alert or themes.defaultAlert
    themeConfigured = true
    settings.saveProfile()
end

if isOwner and file and file.read then
    local ok, data = pcall(file.read, "maxwellos_profile.txt")
    if ok and type(data) == "string" and #data <= 28 then
        local theme, name = data:match("^(%d%d?)\n([A-Za-z0-9_-]*)$")
        theme = tonumber(theme)
        if theme and themes[theme] and #name <= 24 then
            settings.theme, computerName = theme, name
            themes.move(0)
            applyTheme()
        end
    end
end
function themes.selectAt(y, x)
    if x and x >= 444 and y >= 419 and y < 466 then
        themes.move(9)
    elseif x and x >= 392 and y >= 419 and y < 466 then
        themes.move(-9)
    elseif y >= THEME_TOP and y < THEME_TOP + 9 * THEME_STEP then
        local offset = y - THEME_TOP
        if offset % THEME_STEP < THEME_HEIGHT then
            settings.theme = math.min(#themes, settings.themeTop + math.floor(offset / THEME_STEP))
        end
    elseif y >= 419 and y < 466 then
        applyTheme()
    end
end

function settings.selectPage(page)
    if settings.page == page then return end
    settings.page = page
    if page == "name" then settings.name = computerName end
    if page == "wallpaper" then PHONE.wallpaper.scan() end
end

function settings.saveName()
    if settings.waiting then return end
    local name = settings.name
    if #name < 1 or #name > 24 or not name:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$") then
        settings.message = "Use 1-24 letters, numbers, hyphens or underscores."
    elseif name == computerName then
        settings.message = "Name saved."
    elseif room == "" then
        computerName, settings.message = name, "Name saved."
        settings.saveProfile()
    elseif net.getBytesLeft() < 64 then
        settings.message = "Connection busy. Try again shortly."
    else
        settings.waiting = timer.curtime() + 5
        settings.message = "Saving name..."
        net.start("room_rename")
        net.writeString(name)
        net.send()
    end
end

local function wrapText(value, width, face)
    render.setFont(face)
    local lines, starts = {}, {}
    for offset in value:gmatch("()[^\128-\191][\128-\191]*") do
        starts[#starts + 1] = offset
    end
    local count = #starts
    starts[count + 1] = #value + 1
    local first = 1
    while first <= count do
        local low, high = first, count
        while low <= high do
            local middle = math.floor((low + high) / 2)
            local candidate = value:sub(starts[first], starts[middle + 1] - 1)
            if render.getTextSize(candidate) <= width then
                low = middle + 1
            else
                high = middle - 1
            end
        end
        local last = math.max(first, high)
        if last < count then
            for i = last, first + 1, -1 do
                if value:sub(starts[i], starts[i]) == " " then
                    last = i
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        lines[#lines + 1] = value:sub(starts[first], starts[last + 1] - 1)
        first = last + 1
    end
    return lines
end

local function label(x, y, value, color, face)
    render.setFont(face or font)
    render.setColor(color)
    render.drawText(x, y, value)
end


PHONE.wallpaper = {files={{name="Default",path=""}},selected=1,path="",notice="",restored=false,nextRestore=0}

function PHONE.wallpaper.allowed(path)
    if path=="" then return true end
    if type(path)~="string" or path:sub(1,14)~="moswallpapers/" then return false end
    local name=path:sub(15)
    if name=="" or name:find("/",1,true) or name:find(string.char(92),1,true) then return false end
    local ext=name:lower():sub(-4)
    return ext==".png" or ext==".jpg"
end

function PHONE.wallpaper.scan()
    local app=PHONE.wallpaper
    local ok,names=pcall(function()
        file.createDir("moswallpapers")
        return file.find("moswallpapers/*","nameasc")
    end)
    if not ok or type(names)~="table" then app.notice="Grant file access, then press R to rescan."; return end
    app.files={{name="Default",path=""}}
    app.selected=1
    for _,name in ipairs(names) do
        local path="moswallpapers/"..name
        if app.allowed(path) and #app.files<65 then
            app.files[#app.files+1]={name=name,path=path}
            if path==app.path then app.selected=#app.files end
        end
    end
    app.notice=#app.files==1 and "Add PNG or JPG images to data/sf_filedata/moswallpapers." or "Select an image, then press Enter to apply."
end

function PHONE.wallpaper.load(path)
    local app=PHONE.wallpaper
    if not app.allowed(path) then app.notice="Invalid wallpaper filename."; return false end
    if path=="" then app.image,app.path,app.notice=nil,"","Default wallpaper applied."; return true end
    if not material or not material.createFromImage then app.notice="Local image loading is unavailable in this runtime."; return false end
    local ok,image=pcall(material.createFromImage,"data/sf_filedata/"..path,"smooth noclamp")
    if not ok or not image then app.notice="Image failed to load. Check the file and image permission."; return false end
    local measured,width,height=pcall(function() return image:getWidth(),image:getHeight() end)
    if not measured or not width or not height or width<=0 or height<=0 then
        app.notice="Wallpaper dimensions could not be read."
        return false
    end
    app.image,app.width,app.height,app.path=image,width,height,path
    app.notice="Wallpaper applied."
    return true
end

function PHONE.wallpaper.restore()
    local app=PHONE.wallpaper
    if app.restored or not isOwner or timer.curtime()<app.nextRestore then return end
    app.nextRestore=timer.curtime()+5
    local ok,path=pcall(function()
        if file.exists("maxwellos_wallpaper.txt") then return file.read("maxwellos_wallpaper.txt") end
        return ""
    end)
    if not ok or type(path)~="string" then return end
    if not app.allowed(path) then app.restored=true; app.notice="Saved wallpaper path is invalid."; return end
    if app.load(path) then app.restored=true end
end

function PHONE.wallpaper.apply()
    local app=PHONE.wallpaper
    local entry=app.files[app.selected]
    if not entry or not app.load(entry.path) then return end
    app.restored=true
    local ok=pcall(file.write,"maxwellos_wallpaper.txt",entry.path)
    if not ok then app.notice="Applied for this session. File access is needed to save the selection." end
end

function PHONE.wallpaper.drawImage(x,y,w,h)
    local app=PHONE.wallpaper
    if not app.image then return false end
    local u0,v0,u1,v1=0,0,1,1
    local imageRatio,boxRatio=app.width/app.height,w/h
    if imageRatio>boxRatio then
