        local portion=boxRatio/imageRatio
        u0,u1=(1-portion)/2,(1+portion)/2
    else
        local portion=imageRatio/boxRatio
        v0,v1=(1-portion)/2,(1+portion)/2
    end
    render.setColor(Color(255,255,255))
    render.setMaterial(app.image)
    if render.drawTexturedRectUV then render.drawTexturedRectUV(x,y,w,h,u0,v0,u1,v1)
    else render.drawTexturedRect(x,y,w,h) end
    render.setMaterial()
    return true
end

function PHONE.wallpaper.key(key,enter)
    local app=PHONE.wallpaper
    if key==17 then app.selected=math.max(1,app.selected-1)
    elseif key==18 then app.selected=math.min(#app.files,app.selected+1)
    elseif key==151 then app.selected=math.max(1,app.selected-8)
    elseif key==152 then app.selected=math.min(#app.files,app.selected+8)
    elseif key==114 or key==82 then app.scan()
    elseif enter then app.apply() end
end

function PHONE.wallpaper.click(x,y)
    local app=PHONE.wallpaper
    if y>=402 and y<440 then
        if x<250 then app.apply() else app.scan() end
    elseif x>=22 and x<250 and y>=135 and y<375 then
        local first=math.max(1,app.selected-9)
        local index=first+math.floor((y-135)/24)
        if app.files[index] then app.selected=index end
    end
end

function PHONE.wallpaper.drawSettings()
    local app=PHONE.wallpaper
    label(22,108,"Wallpapers",accent,smallFont)
    local first=math.max(1,app.selected-9)
    for index=first,math.min(#app.files,first+9) do
        local text=app.files[index].name
        label(22,139+(index-first)*24,(index==app.selected and "> " or "")..text:sub(1,25),
            index==app.selected and accent or ink,smallFont)
    end
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(268,135,220,220)
    app.drawImage(268,135,220,220)
    label(270,365,"Current wallpaper",muted,smallFont)
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(22,402,216,36)
    render.drawRect(266,402,222,36)
    label(38,411,"Apply",accent,smallFont)
    label(282,411,"Rescan",accent,smallFont)
    local lines=wrapText(app.notice,464,smallFont)
    for i=1,math.min(2,#lines) do label(22,449+(i-1)*19,lines[i],muted,smallFont) end
    label(22,489,"Up/Down selects | Enter applies | R rescans",muted,smallFont)
end

PHONE.appHost = {
    folders = {"data/sf_filedata/mosapps", "data/starfall/MaxwellOS/mosapps", "data/starfall/mosapps"},
    installed = {}, active = nil, nextScan = 0, notice = "", startOpen = false, scanned = false
}

function PHONE.appHost.list(path)
    local prefix="data/sf_filedata/"
    if path:sub(1,#prefix)~=prefix then
        assert(file.findInGame,"Game-file scanning is unavailable")
        return file.findInGame(path,"nameasc")
    end
    local ok,files,folders=pcall(file.find,path:sub(#prefix+1),"nameasc")
    local alternate,otherFiles,otherFolders=false,nil,nil
    if file.findInGame then alternate,otherFiles,otherFolders=pcall(file.findInGame,path,"nameasc") end
    if not ok and not alternate then error(tostring(files)) end
    local function merge(first,second)
        local result,seen={},{}
        for _,list in ipairs({type(first)=="table" and first or {},type(second)=="table" and second or {}}) do
            for _,value in ipairs(list) do
                if type(value)=="string" and not seen[value] then seen[value]=true;result[#result+1]=value end
            end
        end
        table.sort(result)
        return result
    end
    return merge(ok and files,alternate and otherFiles),merge(ok and folders,alternate and otherFolders)
end

function PHONE.appHost.read(path)
    local prefix="data/sf_filedata/"
    if path:sub(1,#prefix)==prefix then return file.read(path:sub(#prefix+1)) end
    assert(file.readInGame,"Game-file reading is unavailable")
    return file.readInGame(path)
end

function PHONE.appHost.directory(path)
    local last,start=0,1
    while true do
        local index=path:find("/",start,true)
        if not index then break end
        last,start=index,index+1
    end
    return path:sub(1,last-1)
end

function PHONE.appHost.samePackage(first,second)
    if first.source~=second.source then return false end
    local roots={PHONE.appHost.directory(first.path),PHONE.appHost.directory(second.path)}
    local dependencies={{},{}}
    for index,instance in ipairs({first,second}) do
        for path,code in pairs(instance.dependencies or {}) do
            dependencies[index][path:sub(#roots[index]+2)]=code
        end
    end
    for path,code in pairs(dependencies[1]) do if dependencies[2][path]~=code then return false end end
    for path,code in pairs(dependencies[2]) do if dependencies[1][path]~=code then return false end end
    return true
end

function PHONE.appHost.call(instance, callback, ...)
    if not instance or type(instance.module[callback]) ~= "function" then return true end
    if instance.error or (callback=="close" and instance.cleanupDone) then return false end
    local ok, result = pcall(instance.module[callback], instance.api, ...)
    if not ok then
        instance.error = tostring(result):sub(1,160)
        PHONE.appHost.notice = instance.name .. ": " .. instance.error
        if callback~="close" and type(instance.module.close)=="function" then
            pcall(instance.module.close,instance.api)
        end
        instance.cleanupDone=true
    end
    return ok, result
end

function PHONE.appHost.storageName(instance, key)
    if type(key) ~= "string" or #key<1 or #key>40 or not key:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$") then
        error("Use a storage key with 1-40 letters, numbers, underscores or hyphens.")
    end
    return "mosapp_" .. instance.id .. "_" .. key .. ".txt"
end

function PHONE.appHost.errorMessage(value)
    if type(value)=="table" then value=value.message or value.msg or "Starfall rejected the app module." end
    return tostring(value)
end

function PHONE.appHost.create(source, filename)
    local host = PHONE.appHost
    local instance = {path=filename, source=source, state={}}
    local api = {state=instance.state, width=480, height=380}
    instance.api = api
    api.companions={}
    api.findCompanions=function(role) return host.request(instance,0,0,role or "",{}) end
    api.callCompanion=function(target,action,payload) return host.request(instance,1,target,action,payload) end
    api.label = function(x,y,text,color,face)
        label(x+16,y+80,tostring(text),color or ink,face or smallFont)
    end
    api.theme = function() return {background=background,panel=panel,accent=accent,text=ink,muted=muted} end
    api.home = function() instance.goHome=true end
    api.reload = function() instance.reloadRequested=true end
    api.room = function() return room end
    api.wrap = function(text,width,face) return wrapText(tostring(text),width,face or smallFont) end
    api.read = function(key, default)
        local value=file.read(host.storageName(instance,key))
        return value == nil and default or value
    end
    api.write = function(key, value)
        assert(type(value)=="string" and #value<=32768,"App saves support strings up to 32768 bytes.")
        file.write(host.storageName(instance,key),value)
    end
    api.isKeyDown = function(key)
        if host.active~=instance or not poweredOn then return false end
        if PHONE.controlsLocked and hasPermission("input") then return input.isKeyDown(key) end
        local code=PHONE.keys and PHONE.keys[key]
        if code and code>=65 and code<=90 then code=code+32 end
        return keyboardActive and timer.curtime()<(PHONE.wireHeldUntil or 0)
            and PHONE.wireHeld and PHONE.wireHeld[code]==true or false
    end
    local appRender=setmetatable({}, {__index=render})
    for _,method in ipairs({"drawRect","drawRectOutline","drawText","drawCircle","drawCircleOutline"}) do
        local draw=render[method]
        if draw then appRender[method]=function(x,y,...) return draw(x+16,y+80,...) end end
    end
    appRender.drawLine=function(x1,y1,x2,y2,...)
        return render.drawLine(x1+16,y1+80,x2+16,y2+80,...)
    end
    local environment
    instance.dependencies,instance.modules,instance.loading={},{},{}
    local packageFolder=host.directory(filename)
    api.require=function(relative)
        assert(type(relative)=="string" and #relative<=120 and relative:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_ ./-]+$"),"Invalid package module path")
        assert(relative:sub(1,1)~="/" and not relative:find("..",1,true),"Module path must stay inside the package")
        assert(relative:sub(-4)==".txt" or relative:sub(-4)==".lua","Modules must be .txt or .lua files")
        local fullPath=packageFolder.."/"..relative
        if instance.modules[fullPath]~=nil then return instance.modules[fullPath] end
        assert(not instance.loading[fullPath],"Circular module dependency")
        local count=0
        for _ in pairs(instance.dependencies) do count=count+1 end
        assert(count<16,"Package module limit reached")
        local code=host.read(fullPath)
        assert(type(code)=="string" and #code<=65536,"Unable to read package module")
        instance.loading[fullPath]=true
        local module,err=loadstring(code,fullPath,environment)
        if not module then instance.loading[fullPath]=nil; error(err) end
        local ok,value=pcall(module,api)
        instance.loading[fullPath]=nil
        if not ok then error(host.errorMessage(value)) end
        instance.dependencies[fullPath]=code
        instance.modules[fullPath]=value==nil and true or value
        return instance.modules[fullPath]
    end
    environment = {
        assert=assert,error=error,pcall=pcall,pairs=pairs,ipairs=ipairs,next=next,
        type=type,tonumber=tonumber,tostring=tostring,select=select,unpack=unpack,
        setmetatable=setmetatable,getmetatable=getmetatable,
        math=math,string=string,table=table,json=json,
        Color=Color,Vector=Vector,Angle=Angle,
        render=appRender,sound=sound,KEY=KEY,MOUSE=MOUSE,
        timer={curtime=timer.curtime},print=print
    }
    environment._G=environment
    environment.require=api.require
    local chunk, err = loadstring(source, filename, environment)
    if not chunk then return nil,tostring(err) end
    local ok, descriptor = pcall(chunk,api)
    if not ok then return nil,host.errorMessage(descriptor) end
    if type(descriptor)~="table" or type(descriptor.id)~="string" or #descriptor.id<1 or #descriptor.id>32
        or not descriptor.id:match("^[a-z0-9_-]+$") or type(descriptor.name)~="string"
        or type(descriptor.draw)~="function" then
        return nil,"Return an app table with id, name, and a draw function."
    end
    instance.id=descriptor.id
    instance.name=descriptor.name:gsub("%c"," "):sub(1,32)
    if instance.name=="" then return nil,"App name is empty." end
    instance.module=descriptor
    api.id=instance.id
    instance.autoCompanions={}
    local declared=descriptor.companions
    if type(declared)~="table" then declared=type(descriptor.companion)=="string" and {descriptor.companion} or {} end
    if #declared>4 then instance.companionError="An app can declare up to four companion modules." end
    for index=1,math.min(4,#declared) do
        local relative=declared[index]
        if type(relative)~="string" or #relative>120 or relative:sub(1,1)=="/" or relative:find("..",1,true) or not relative:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_ ./-]+$")
            or (relative:sub(-4)~=".txt" and relative:sub(-4)~=".lua") then
            instance.companionError="Invalid companion module path."
        else
            local fullPath=packageFolder.."/"..relative
            local ok,code=pcall(host.read,fullPath)
            if not ok or type(code)~="string" or #code<1 or #code>65536 then
                instance.companionError="Unable to read companion module "..relative
            else
                instance.autoCompanions[relative]=code
                instance.dependencies[fullPath]=code
            end
        end
    end
    return instance
end

function PHONE.appHost.rebuild()
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    local selected=menuOptions[selectedGame] and menuOptions[selectedGame].tab
    local options, settingsEntries = {},{}
    for _,entry in ipairs(PHONE.tools and PHONE.tools.all or menuOptions) do
        if not entry.external then
            if entry.tab=="settings" or entry.tab=="credits" then settingsEntries[#settingsEntries+1]=entry
            else options[#options+1]=entry end
        end
    end
    local apps={}
    for _,instance in pairs(host.installed) do apps[#apps+1]=instance end
    table.sort(apps,function(a,b)
        if a.name:lower()==b.name:lower() then return a.id<b.id end
        return a.name:lower()<b.name:lower()
    end)
    for _,instance in ipairs(apps) do
        options[#options+1]={
            tab="mos:"..instance.id,name=instance.name,
            detail=MAXWELLNET.clean(instance.module.description,64),
            external=true,needsRoom=instance.module.needsRoom==true
        }
    end
    for _,entry in ipairs(settingsEntries) do options[#options+1]=entry end
    menuOptions=options
    if PHONE.tools then PHONE.tools.capture(options) end
    selectedGame=math.max(1,math.min(selectedGame,#options))
    for index,entry in ipairs(options) do if entry.tab==selected then selectedGame=index; break end end
    if type(lastApp)=="string" and lastApp:sub(1,4)=="mos:" and not host.installed[lastApp:sub(5)] then
        lastApp=false
    end
    PHONE.desktopScroll(0,true)
end

function PHONE.appHost.trace(message)
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    host.scanTrace=host.scanTrace or {}
    if #host.scanTrace<220 then host.scanTrace[#host.scanTrace+1]=tostring(message):sub(1,220) end
end

function PHONE.appHost.saveTrace()
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    local lines={"MaxwellOS app scan"}
    for _,line in ipairs(host.scanTrace or {}) do lines[#lines+1]=line end
    for id,app in pairs(host.installed) do lines[#lines+1]="INSTALLED "..id.." | "..app.path end
    for _,message in ipairs(host.scanErrors or {}) do lines[#lines+1]="ERROR "..message end
    if file and file.write then pcall(file.write,"maxwellos_app_scan.txt",table.concat(lines,"\n")) end
end

function PHONE.appHost.packageEntry(folder)
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    local listed,names=pcall(host.list,folder.."/*")
    local available={}
    if listed and type(names)=="table" then
        for _,name in ipairs(names) do
            if type(name)=="string" then available[name:lower()]=name end
        end
    end
    for _,main in ipairs({"app.txt","app.lua","main.txt","main.lua"}) do
        local actual=available[main]
        if actual then return folder.."/"..actual end
        -- A readable entry remains installable when a directory listing is incomplete.
        local ok,source=pcall(host.read,folder.."/"..main)
        if ok and type(source)=="string" then return folder.."/"..main,source end
    end
end

function PHONE.appHost.scan(force)
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    if not isOwner or not file or not file.find or not file.read then return end
    if host.scanQueue or (host.scanned and not force) then return end
    host.scanned=true
    host.scanTrace={}
    local queue,errors,succeeded={},{},false
    if file.createDir then pcall(file.createDir,"mosapps") end
    for _,folder in ipairs(host.folders) do
        local ok,names,folders=pcall(host.list,folder.."/*")
        host.trace(folder.." | files="..(type(names)=="table" and #names or "failed").." folders="..(type(folders)=="table" and #folders or "failed"))
        if not ok or type(names)~="table" then
            errors[#errors+1]="Grant game-file listing permission to scan mosapps."
        else
            succeeded=true
            for _,name in ipairs(names) do
                local extension=name:lower():sub(-4)
                if (extension==".txt" or extension==".lua") and name:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_ .-]+$") and #queue<96 then
                    queue[#queue+1]={path=folder.."/"..name,name=name}
                end
            end
            for _,name in ipairs(type(folders)=="table" and folders or {}) do
                if type(name)=="string" and name:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_ .-]+$") and name~="." and name~=".." and #queue<96 then
                    queue[#queue+1]={path=folder.."/"..name,name=name,package=true}
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if not succeeded then host.notice=errors[1] or "mosapps scan failed."; return end
    host.scanQueue,host.scanIndex,host.scanResult,host.scanErrors=queue,1,{},errors
    host.notice="Scanning mosapps..."
end

function PHONE.appHost.processScan()
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    if not host.scanQueue then return end
    local entry=host.scanQueue[host.scanIndex]
    if not entry then
        local count=#host.scanQueue
        host.installed=host.scanResult
        host.scanQueue,host.scanResult=nil,nil
        host.rebuild()
        local installed=0
        for _ in pairs(host.installed) do installed=installed+1 end
        host.notice=host.scanErrors[1] and host.scanErrors[1]:sub(1,100) or ("Apps: "..installed.." installed. R rescans.")
        host.saveTrace()
        return
    end
    host.scanIndex=host.scanIndex+1
    local source
    if entry.package then
        local path,code=host.packageEntry(entry.path)
        if not path then host.trace("NO ENTRY "..entry.path);return end
        host.trace("PACKAGE "..path)
        entry.path,source=path,code
    end
    local read=true
    if source==nil then read,source=pcall(host.read,entry.path) end
    if not read or type(source)~="string" then host.scanErrors[#host.scanErrors+1]=entry.name..": read failed"; return end
    if #source>65536 then host.scanErrors[#host.scanErrors+1]=entry.name..": app exceeds 65536 bytes"; return end
    host.trace("READ "..entry.path.." | "..#source.." bytes")
    local previous
    for _,instance in pairs(host.installed) do if instance.path==entry.path then previous=instance; break end end
    local instance,err
    local unchanged=previous and previous.source==source and not previous.error
    if unchanged then
        for dependency,code in pairs(previous.dependencies or {}) do
            local ok,current=pcall(host.read,dependency)
            if not ok or current~=code then unchanged=false; break end
        end
    end
    if unchanged then instance=previous
    else
        local ok
        ok,instance,err=pcall(host.create,source,entry.path)
        if not ok then err,instance=instance,nil end
    end
    if instance then
        host.trace("LOADED "..instance.id.." | "..entry.path)
        local existing=host.scanResult[instance.id]
        if not existing or (existing.module.fallback==true and instance.module.fallback~=true) then
            host.scanResult[instance.id]=instance
        elseif instance.module.fallback==true then
            -- A flat launcher provides an alternate entry for the same package.
        elseif not host.samePackage(existing,instance) then
            host.scanErrors[#host.scanErrors+1]=entry.name..": duplicate app id "..instance.id
        end
    else
        local message=host.errorMessage(err or "load failed")
        host.scanErrors[#host.scanErrors+1]=entry.name..": "..message
        if entry.package then
            local hash=0
            for i=1,#entry.path do hash=(hash*31+entry.path:byte(i))%2147483647 end
            local id="package_error_"..hash
            host.scanResult[id]={id=id,path=entry.path,source=source,name=entry.name:sub(1,24).." (error)",
                module={description="Open to see why this package could not load."},api={},error=message}
        end
    end
end


PHONE.appHost.autoCache,PHONE.appHost.autoPending,PHONE.appHost.autoTicket={}, {}, 0
function PHONE.appHost.prepareCompanions(instance)
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    if instance.companionError then instance.error=instance.companionError;return false end
    instance.waitingCompanions=false
    for slot,source in pairs(instance.autoCompanions or {}) do
        if host.autoCache[instance.id.."/"..slot]~=source then
            local pending=false
            for _,request in pairs(host.autoPending) do
                if request.instance==instance and request.slot==slot then pending=true;break end
            end
            if not pending then
                host.autoTicket=host.autoTicket%4294967295+1
                host.autoPending[host.autoTicket]={instance=instance,slot=slot,source=source,expires=timer.curtime()+45}
            end
            instance.waitingCompanions=true
        end
    end
    return not instance.waitingCompanions
end

MOS_SERVICES.onInstalled=function(ticket,appId,slot,ok,role,message)
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    local request=host.autoPending[ticket]
    if not request or request.instance.id~=appId or request.slot~=slot then return end
    host.autoPending[ticket]=nil
    local instance=request.instance
    if not ok then
        instance.waitingCompanions=false
        instance.error="Companion: "..message
        return
    end
    host.autoCache[appId.."/"..slot]=request.source
    local waiting=false
    for _,pending in pairs(host.autoPending) do if pending.instance==instance then waiting=true;break end end
    if not waiting and instance.waitingCompanions then
        instance.waitingCompanions=false
        if host.active==instance and not instance.error then
            instance.opened=host.call(instance,"open")
        end
    end
end

function PHONE.appHost.pumpCompanions()
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    if timer.curtime()<(host.nextAutoUpload or 0) then return end
    for ticket,request in pairs(host.autoPending) do
        if timer.curtime()>=request.expires then
            host.autoPending[ticket]=nil
            request.instance.waitingCompanions=false
            request.instance.error="Companion loading timed out. Return to Desktop and rescan."
        elseif not request.sent then
            if MOS_SERVICES.upload(request.instance.id,request.slot,request.source,ticket) then
                request.sent=true
                host.nextAutoUpload=timer.curtime()+0.3
            end
            return
        end
    end
end

function PHONE.appHost.switch(tab)
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    if host.active then
        if host.active.opened then host.call(host.active,"close") end
        host.active.opened=false
        host.active=nil
    end
    if type(tab)=="string" and tab:sub(1,4)=="mos:" then
        local instance=host.installed[tab:sub(5)]
        if instance then
            host.active=instance
            instance.lastUpdate=nil
            if host.prepareCompanions(instance) then instance.opened=host.call(instance,"open") end
        end
    end
end

net.receive("room_held_keys",function()
    local keys={}
    for _=1,net.readUInt(9) do
        local key=net.readUInt(8)
        if key>=65 and key<=90 then key=key+32 end
        keys[key]=true
    end
    PHONE.wireHeld,PHONE.wireHeldUntil=keys,timer.curtime()+0.35
end)


PHONE.appHost.pending,PHONE.appHost.nextTicket,PHONE.appHost.nextRequestAt={},0,0

function PHONE.appHost.request(instance,operation,target,action,payload)
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    if not instance.id then return nil,"Initialize companion requests in the open callback." end
    if timer.curtime()<host.nextRequestAt then return nil,"Please wait briefly before sending another request." end
    local count=0
    for _ in pairs(host.pending) do count=count+1 end
    if count>=16 then return nil,"Too many pending companion requests." end
    local hostedRole=""
    if type(target)=="table" then target=target.id end
    if type(target)=="string" and target:sub(1,6)=="local:" then hostedRole=target:sub(7);target=0 end
    if #hostedRole>40 then return nil,"Invalid hosted companion role." end
    if type(action)~="string" or #action>40 or type(target)~="number" or target<0 or target>65535 or target%1~=0 then
        return nil,"Invalid companion target or action."
    end
    local ok,data=pcall(json.encode,payload==nil and {} or payload)
    if not ok or type(data)~="string" or #data>2048 then return nil,"Request data must fit in 2048 JSON bytes." end
    if net.getBytesLeft()<#data+160 then return nil,"Connection busy." end
    host.nextRequestAt=timer.curtime()+0.2
    host.nextTicket=host.nextTicket%65535+1
    while host.pending[host.nextTicket] do host.nextTicket=host.nextTicket%65535+1 end
    local ticket=host.nextTicket
    host.pending[ticket]={instance=instance,kind=operation==0 and "discover" or "call",expires=timer.curtime()+6}
    net.start("mos_companion_request")
    net.writeUInt(ticket,16)
    net.writeUInt(operation,1)
    net.writeString(instance.id)
    net.writeUInt(target,16)
    net.writeString(action)
    net.writeString(data)
    net.writeString(hostedRole)
    net.send()
    return ticket
end

net.receive("mos_companion_reply",function()
    local ticket,ok,data=net.readUInt(16),net.readBool(),net.readString()
    local pending=PHONE.appHost.pending[ticket]
    if not pending then return end
    PHONE.appHost.pending[ticket]=nil
    local decoded,value=pcall(json.decode,data)
    if #data>6000 or not decoded then ok,value=false,{error="Invalid companion reply."} end
    local instance=pending.instance
    instance.replies=instance.replies or {}
    instance.replies[#instance.replies+1]={ticket=ticket,ok=ok,value=value,kind=pending.kind}
end)

function PHONE.appHost.processReplies(instance)
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    for ticket,pending in pairs(host.pending) do
        if timer.curtime()>=pending.expires then
            host.pending[ticket]=nil
            local app=pending.instance
            app.replies=app.replies or {}
            app.replies[#app.replies+1]={ticket=ticket,ok=false,value={error="Companion request timed out."},kind=pending.kind}
        end
    end
    local replies=instance and instance.replies or {}
    if instance then instance.replies={} end
    for _,reply in ipairs(replies) do
        if reply.kind=="discover" and reply.ok and type(reply.value)=="table" then
            instance.api.companions=reply.value.companions or {}
        end
        instance.api.lastReply=reply
        local err=not reply.ok and type(reply.value)=="table" and reply.value.error or nil
        host.call(instance,"reply",reply.ticket,reply.ok,reply.value,err)
    end
end

function PHONE.appHost.reloadActive()
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    local old=host.active
    if not old then return end
    local read,source=pcall(host.read,old.path)
    local ok,fresh,err=false,nil,nil
    if read and type(source)=="string" and #source<=65536 then
        ok,fresh,err=pcall(host.create,source,old.path)
        if not ok then err,fresh=fresh,nil end
    else err="Unable to read app source for reload." end
    if not fresh or fresh.id~=old.id then
        old.error=host.errorMessage(err or "Reload must keep the same app ID.")
        old.waitingCompanions=false
        return
    end
    for ticket,request in pairs(host.autoPending) do
        if request.instance==old then host.autoPending[ticket]=nil end
    end
    for ticket,request in pairs(host.pending) do
        if request.instance==old then host.pending[ticket]=nil end
    end
    for key in pairs(host.autoCache) do
        if key:sub(1,#old.id+1)==old.id.."/" then host.autoCache[key]=nil end
    end
    host.installed[old.id]=fresh
    host.switch("mos:"..old.id)
end

function PHONE.appHost.key(key,enter,character)
    local active=PHONE.appHost.active
    if active and (key==114 or key==82) and (active.id=="sprite_demo" or active.error or active.waitingCompanions) then
        PHONE.appHost.reloadActive();return
    end
    if PHONE.appHost.active and PHONE.appHost.active.waitingCompanions then return end
    return PHONE.appHost.call(PHONE.appHost.active,"key",key,enter,character)
end
function PHONE.appHost.click(x,y,right)
    if PHONE.appHost.active and PHONE.appHost.active.waitingCompanions then return end
    if x>=16 and x<496 and y>=80 and y<460 then
        PHONE.appHost.call(PHONE.appHost.active,"click",x-16,y-80,right==true)
    end
end
function PHONE.appHost.draw()
    local instance=PHONE.appHost.active
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(16,80,480,380)
    if not instance then
        label(30,120,"This app is unavailable. Return to Desktop and rescan.",alertColor,smallFont)
        return
    end
    if instance.waitingCompanions then
        label(30,110,"Loading companion modules...",ink,titleFont)
        label(30,165,"You can return to Desktop with Ctrl.",muted,smallFont)
        return
    end
    if instance.error then
        label(30,110,"Application stopped",alertColor,titleFont)
        local lines=wrapText(instance.error,448,smallFont)
        for i=1,math.min(#lines,12) do label(30,160+(i-1)*20,lines[i],ink,smallFont) end
        label(30,430,"Press R to reload, or Ctrl for Desktop.",muted,smallFont)
        return
    end
    -- The render adapter maps local content coordinates into the app window.
    PHONE.appHost.call(instance,"draw")
end

hook.add("think","maxwellos_installed_apps",function()
    if not isOwner then return end
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    local active=host.active
    if not poweredOn or timer.curtime()<startupUntil then
        if active and not active.suspended then
            host.call(active,"pause")
            active.suspended=true
        end
        return
    end
    if active and active.suspended then
        active.suspended=false
        active.lastUpdate=nil
        host.call(active,"resume")
    end
    host.pumpCompanions()
    if gameTab=="menu" then
        PHONE.wallpaper.restore()
        host.scan(false)
        host.processScan()
    end
    local instance=host.active
    if not instance or gameTab~="mos:"..instance.id then host.processReplies(nil); return end
    host.processReplies(instance)
    if instance.reloadRequested then host.reloadActive();return end
    if instance.waitingCompanions or instance.error then return end
    if instance.module.needsRoom and room=="" then
        if host.home then host.home() end
        return
    end
    if instance.goHome then
        instance.goHome=nil
        if host.home then host.home() end
        return
    end
    local now=timer.curtime()
    if instance.lastUpdate and now-instance.lastUpdate<1/60 then return end
    local dt=math.min(0.05,math.max(0,now-(instance.lastUpdate or now)))
    instance.lastUpdate=now
    host.call(instance,"update",dt,now)
end)

PHONE.squadron = {}

function PHONE.squadron.wave()
    PHONE.squadron.enemies, PHONE.squadron.shots, PHONE.squadron.bolts = {}, {}, {}
    PHONE.squadron.time, PHONE.squadron.nextBolt, PHONE.squadron.nextDive = 0, 3.5, 4
    for row = 1, 3 do
        for column = 1, 8 do
            local x, y = 95 + (column - 1) * 46, 105 + row * 35
            PHONE.squadron.enemies[#PHONE.squadron.enemies + 1] = {
                x = -32, y = 84, baseX = x, baseY = y, kind = row,
                hp = row == 1 and 2 or 1,
                arrive = (row - 1) * 0.65 + (column - 1) * 0.08
            }
        end
    end
end

function PHONE.squadron.start()
    PHONE.squadron.x, PHONE.squadron.score, PHONE.squadron.lives, PHONE.squadron.level = 256, 0, 3, 1
    PHONE.squadron.targetX = PHONE.squadron.x
    PHONE.squadron.wave()
end

function PHONE.squadron.key(key, enter)
    if not PHONE.squadron.lives or PHONE.squadron.lives == 0 then
        if enter then PHONE.squadron.start() end
    elseif key == 19 or key == 20 then
        PHONE.squadron.targetX = math.max(35, math.min(477, PHONE.squadron.targetX + (key == 19 and -14 or 14)))
    elseif (key == 32 or enter) and #PHONE.squadron.shots < 6 then
        PHONE.squadron.shots[#PHONE.squadron.shots + 1] = {x = PHONE.squadron.x, y = 411}
    end
end

function PHONE.squadron.update(now)
    local dt = math.min(0.05, math.max(0, now - (PHONE.squadron.lastTick or now)))
    PHONE.squadron.lastTick = now
    if not PHONE.squadron.lives or PHONE.squadron.lives == 0 then return end
    local ship = PHONE.squadron
    local distance = ship.targetX - ship.x
    ship.x = ship.x + math.max(-240 * dt, math.min(240 * dt, distance))
    PHONE.squadron.time = PHONE.squadron.time + dt
    local enemies, shots, bolts = PHONE.squadron.enemies, PHONE.squadron.shots, PHONE.squadron.bolts
    for _, enemy in ipairs(enemies) do
        local t = PHONE.squadron.time
        local age = t - enemy.arrive
        local x = enemy.baseX + 44 * math.sin(t * 1.8)
        local y = enemy.baseY + 10 * math.sin(t * 2 + enemy.baseX / 55)
        enemy.active = age >= 0
        if age < 2 then
            local u = math.max(0, age / 2)
            local side = enemy.kind % 2 == 0 and 1 or -1
            local edge = 256 + side * 288
            enemy.x = edge + (x - edge) * u - side * 78 * math.sin(math.pi * u)
            enemy.y = 85 + (y - 85) * u + 125 * math.sin(math.pi * u)
        elseif enemy.dive then
            local u = (t - enemy.dive) / 2
            if u >= 1 then
                enemy.dive = nil
                enemy.x, enemy.y = x, y
            else
                enemy.x = x + 66 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * u)
                enemy.y = y + (393 - y) * math.sin(math.pi * u)
            end
        else
            enemy.x, enemy.y = x, y
        end
    end
    for i = #shots, 1, -1 do
        local shot = shots[i]
        shot.y = shot.y - 450 * dt
        for j = #enemies, 1, -1 do
            local enemy = enemies[j]
            if enemy.active and math.abs(shot.x - enemy.x) < 15 and math.abs(shot.y - enemy.y) < 15 then
                enemy.hp = enemy.hp - 1
                if enemy.hp == 0 then
                    table.remove(enemies, j)
                    PHONE.squadron.score = PHONE.squadron.score + (4 - enemy.kind) * 75
                end
                shot.y = 0
                break
            end
        end
        if shot.y < 90 then table.remove(shots, i) end
    end
    if #enemies == 0 then
        PHONE.squadron.level = PHONE.squadron.level + 1
        PHONE.squadron.wave()
        return
    end
    if PHONE.squadron.time >= PHONE.squadron.nextDive then
        local divers = {}
        for _, enemy in ipairs(enemies) do
            if enemy.kind ~= 1 and not enemy.dive then divers[#divers + 1] = enemy end
        end
        if #divers > 0 then divers[math.random(#divers)].dive = PHONE.squadron.time end
        PHONE.squadron.nextDive = PHONE.squadron.time + math.max(0.8, 2.8 - PHONE.squadron.level * 0.18)
    end
    if PHONE.squadron.time >= PHONE.squadron.nextBolt then
        local enemy = enemies[math.random(#enemies)]
        bolts[#bolts + 1] = {x = enemy.x, y = enemy.y + 10}
        PHONE.squadron.nextBolt = PHONE.squadron.time + math.max(0.28, 1.1 - PHONE.squadron.level * 0.09)
    end
    for i = #bolts, 1, -1 do
        local bolt = bolts[i]
        bolt.y = bolt.y + 200 * dt
        if math.abs(bolt.x - PHONE.squadron.x) < 15 and math.abs(bolt.y - 425) < 13 then
            PHONE.squadron.lives = PHONE.squadron.lives - 1
            PHONE.squadron.bolts = {}
            return
        end
        if bolt.y > 453 then table.remove(bolts, i) end
    end
end

function PHONE.squadron.draw()
    if not PHONE.squadron.lives then
        label(22, 100, "Press Enter to start", accent)
        return
    end
    label(22, 83, "Score " .. PHONE.squadron.score .. "    Wave " .. PHONE.squadron.level .. "    Lives " .. PHONE.squadron.lives, ink)
    if PHONE.squadron.lives == 0 then
        label(22, 155, "Game over. Press Enter to restart.", alertColor)
        return
    end
    render.setColor(accent)
    render.drawRect(PHONE.squadron.x - 14, 426, 28, 9)
    render.drawRect(PHONE.squadron.x - 4, 416, 8, 12)
    for _, shot in ipairs(PHONE.squadron.shots) do render.drawRect(shot.x - 2, shot.y, 4, 10) end
    render.setColor(alertColor)
    for _, enemy in ipairs(PHONE.squadron.enemies) do
        render.setColor(enemy.kind == 1 and alertColor or (enemy.kind == 2 and accent or ink))
        render.drawRect(enemy.x - 12, enemy.y - 5, 24, 10)
        if enemy.hp == 2 then render.drawRect(enemy.x - 16, enemy.y - 8, 32, 3) end
        render.drawRect(enemy.x - 7, enemy.y - 10, 14, 20)
    end
    for _, bolt in ipairs(PHONE.squadron.bolts) do render.drawRect(bolt.x - 2, bolt.y, 4, 8) end
    label(22, 480, "Left/Right moves  |  Space or Enter fires  |  Ctrl Desktop", muted, smallFont)
end

PHONE.paint = {
    x = 25, y = 25, color = 1, slot = 1, paletteTop = 1, rows = {}, runs = {},
    palette = {Color(20, 20, 28), Color(220, 55, 68), Color(245, 145, 45),
        Color(249, 212, 65), Color(49, 164, 102), Color(55, 113, 220),
        Color(153, 79, 213), Color(255, 255, 255),
        Color(124, 34, 49), Color(191, 67, 100), Color(245, 115, 144), Color(255, 177, 188),
        Color(173, 79, 38), Color(222, 106, 61), Color(247, 181, 98), Color(255, 218, 174),
        Color(176, 142, 47), Color(212, 197, 66), Color(184, 216, 80), Color(233, 244, 161),
        Color(31, 95, 70), Color(84, 194, 111), Color(42, 177, 152), Color(154, 229, 204),
        Color(42, 68, 131), Color(42, 139, 181), Color(66, 196, 223), Color(166, 218, 255),
        Color(87, 49, 145), Color(124, 108, 214), Color(203, 114, 218), Color(232, 182, 244),
        Color(110, 73, 53), Color(151, 151, 163)}
}

function PHONE.paint.rebuildRow(y)
    local paint = PHONE.paint
    local row = paint.rows[y] or {}
    local runs = {}
    local previous
    for column = 1, 50 do
        local color = row[column]
        if color and color ~= previous then
            runs[#runs + 1] = {column, 1, color}
        elseif color then
            runs[#runs][2] = runs[#runs][2] + 1
        end
        previous = color
    end
    paint.runs[y] = runs
end

function PHONE.paint.pixel(x, y)
    local paint = PHONE.paint
    local row = paint.rows[y] or {}
    paint.rows[y] = row
    row[x] = paint.palette[paint.color] and paint.color or nil
    paint.rebuildRow(y)
end

function PHONE.paint.fill(x, y)
    local paint = PHONE.paint
    local target = (paint.rows[y] or {})[x]
    local replacement = paint.palette[paint.color] and paint.color or nil
    if target == replacement then return end

    local queue, changed = {}, {}
    local function visit(column, rowIndex)
        local row = paint.rows[rowIndex]
        if (row and row[column] or nil) ~= target then return end
        if not row then
            row = {}
            paint.rows[rowIndex] = row
        end
        row[column] = replacement
        changed[rowIndex] = true
        queue[#queue + 1] = (rowIndex - 1) * 50 + column - 1
    end

    visit(x, y)
    local head = 1
    while head <= #queue do
        local index = queue[head]
        local column, rowIndex = index % 50 + 1, math.floor(index / 50) + 1
        if column > 1 then visit(column - 1, rowIndex) end
        if column < 50 then visit(column + 1, rowIndex) end
        if rowIndex > 1 then visit(column, rowIndex - 1) end
        if rowIndex < 50 then visit(column, rowIndex + 1) end
        head = head + 1
    end
    for rowIndex in pairs(changed) do paint.rebuildRow(rowIndex) end
end

function PHONE.paint.apply()
    local paint = PHONE.paint
    if PHONE.tools then
        if PHONE.tools.paintPoint(paint.x,paint.y) then return end
        PHONE.tools.paintBegin()
    end
    if paint.bucket then paint.fill(paint.x, paint.y)
    else paint.pixel(paint.x, paint.y) end
end

function PHONE.paint.colorIndex(byte)
    if byte >= 65 and byte <= 90 then return byte - 56 end
    if byte >= 48 and byte <= 56 then return byte - 48 end
    return -1
end

function PHONE.paint.scrollPalette(delta)
    local paint = PHONE.paint
    paint.paletteTop = math.max(1, math.min(#paint.palette - 6, paint.paletteTop + delta))
end

function PHONE.paint.encode()
    local cells = {}
    for y = 1, 50 do
        local row = PHONE.paint.rows[y] or {}
        for x = 1, 50 do
            local index = row[x] or 0
            cells[#cells + 1] = string.char(index <= 8 and index + 48 or index + 56)
        end
    end
    return table.concat(cells)
end

function PHONE.paint.decode(data)
    if type(data) ~= "string" or #data ~= 2500 then return false end
    local rows, runs = {}, {}
    for y = 1, 50 do
        local row, spans, previous = {}, {}, 0
        for x = 1, 50 do
            local color = PHONE.paint.colorIndex(data:byte((y - 1) * 50 + x))
            if color < 0 or color > #PHONE.paint.palette then return false end
            if color > 0 then
                row[x] = color
                if color == previous then spans[#spans][2] = spans[#spans][2] + 1
                else spans[#spans + 1] = {x, 1, color} end
            end
            previous = color
        end
        rows[y], runs[y] = row, spans
    end
    return rows, runs
end

function PHONE.paint.action(action)
    local paint = PHONE.paint
    local path = paint.slot == 1 and "maxwellos_painting.txt" or ("maxwellos_painting_" .. paint.slot .. ".txt")
    if action == "slot" then
        paint.slot = paint.slot % 5 + 1
        paint.message = "Selected save slot " .. paint.slot .. "."
        return
    end
    if action == "save" then
        local ok = file and file.write and pcall(file.write, path, paint.encode())
        paint.message = ok and "Saved to slot " .. paint.slot .. "." or "Could not save painting."
    elseif action == "load" then
        local ok, data
        if file and file.read then ok, data = pcall(file.read, path) end
        local rows, runs = paint.decode(ok and data)
        if rows then if PHONE.tools then PHONE.tools.paintBegin() end;paint.rows, paint.runs = rows, runs end
        paint.message = rows and "Loaded slot " .. paint.slot .. "." or ("No readable painting in slot " .. paint.slot .. ".")
    elseif room == "" then
        paint.message = "Join a chatroom first."
    elseif action == "sendSlot" then
        if net.getBytesLeft() < 2560 then
            paint.message = "Connection busy. Try again."
            return
        end
        local ok, data
        if file and file.read then ok, data = pcall(file.read, path) end
        if not paint.decode(ok and data) then
            paint.message = "No readable painting in slot " .. paint.slot .. "."
            return
        end
        net.start("room_painting")
        net.writeBool(true)
        net.writeString(data)
        net.send()
    elseif net.getBytesLeft() < (action == "send" and 2560 or 32) then
        paint.message = "Connection busy. Try again."
    else
        net.start("room_painting")
        net.writeBool(action == "send")
        if action == "send" then net.writeString(paint.encode()) end
        net.send()
    end
end

function PHONE.paint.openMenu(mode)
    local paint = PHONE.paint
    paint.menu, paint.slots = mode, {}
    for i = 1, 5 do
        local path = i == 1 and "maxwellos_painting.txt" or ("maxwellos_painting_" .. i .. ".txt")
        local ok, data
        if file and file.read then ok, data = pcall(file.read, path) end
        paint.slots[i] = not ok and "Unavailable" or (not data and "Empty"
            or (paint.decode(data) and "Saved painting" or "Unreadable"))
    end
end

function PHONE.paint.confirm()
    local paint = PHONE.paint
    local mode = paint.menu
    paint.menu = nil
    paint.action(mode)
end

-- A stroke starts on the canvas; movement over controls never clicks them.
function PHONE.paint.drag(x, y, held)
    local paint=PHONE.paint
    if not held or paint.menu or paint.bucket then
        paint.dragging,paint.dragX,paint.dragY=false,nil,nil
        return
    end
    if not paint.dragging then return end
    if not x or not y or x<56 or x>=456 or y<78 or y>=478 then
        paint.dragX,paint.dragY=nil,nil
        return
    end
    local targetX,targetY=math.floor((x-56)/8)+1,math.floor((y-78)/8)+1
    local fromX,fromY=paint.dragX or targetX,paint.dragY or targetY
    if paint.dragX==targetX and paint.dragY==targetY then return end
    local dx,dy=math.abs(targetX-fromX),-math.abs(targetY-fromY)
    local sx,sy=fromX<targetX and 1 or -1,fromY<targetY and 1 or -1
    local err=dx+dy
    while true do
        paint.pixel(fromX,fromY)
        if fromX==targetX and fromY==targetY then break end
        local twice=2*err
        if twice>=dy then err=err+dy;fromX=fromX+sx end
        if twice<=dx then err=err+dx;fromY=fromY+sy end
    end
    paint.x,paint.y=targetX,targetY
    paint.dragX,paint.dragY=targetX,targetY
end

function PHONE.paint.click(x, y)
    local paint = PHONE.paint
    if PHONE.tools and PHONE.tools.paintClick(x,y) then return end
    paint.dragging,paint.dragX,paint.dragY=false,nil,nil
    if paint.menu then
        if x >= 62 and x < 450 and y >= 137 and y < 382 then
            paint.slot = math.floor((y - 137) / 49) + 1
        elseif x >= 62 and x < 245 and y >= 402 and y < 447 then paint.confirm()
        elseif x >= 264 and x < 450 and y >= 402 and y < 447 then paint.menu = nil end
        return
    end
    if x >= 16 and x < 50 and y >= 340 and y < 384 then
        paint.bucket, paint.pen, paint.message = not paint.bucket, false, nil
    elseif x >= 16 and x < 50 and y >= 395 and y < 455 then
        paint.action("sendSlot")
    elseif x >= 56 and x < 456 and y >= 78 and y < 478 then
        paint.x, paint.y = math.floor((x - 56) / 8) + 1, math.floor((y - 78) / 8) + 1
        paint.apply()
        paint.dragging=not paint.bucket and (not PHONE.tools or (PHONE.tools.paintTool~="line" and PHONE.tools.paintTool~="rect"))
        paint.dragX,paint.dragY=paint.x,paint.y
    elseif x >= 466 and x < 496 and y >= 78 and y < 365 then
        if y < 101 then paint.scrollPalette(-1)
        elseif y >= 343 then paint.scrollPalette(1)
        elseif y >= 108 and y < 108 + 7 * 32 then
            paint.color = paint.paletteTop + math.floor((y - 108) / 32)
        elseif y >= 332 then paint.color = 0 end
    elseif x >= 466 and x < 496 and y >= 365 and y < 477 then
        local action = ({"slot", "save", "load", "send"})[math.floor((y - 365) / 28) + 1]
        if action == "save" or action == "load" then paint.openMenu(action)
        else paint.action(action) end
    end
end

function PHONE.paint.key(key, enter)
    local paint = PHONE.paint
    if PHONE.tools and PHONE.tools.paintKey(key) then return end
    if PHONE.tools and paint.pen and key>=17 and key<=20 then PHONE.tools.paintBegin() end
    if paint.menu then
        if key == 17 then paint.slot = math.max(1, paint.slot - 1)
        elseif key == 18 then paint.slot = math.min(5, paint.slot + 1)
        elseif key >= 49 and key <= 53 then paint.slot = key - 48
        elseif enter then paint.confirm()
        elseif key == 8 or key == 127 then paint.menu = nil end
        return
    end
    if key == 71 or key == 103 then paint.action("sendSlot")
    elseif key == 86 or key == 118 then paint.action("slot")
    elseif key == 83 or key == 115 then paint.openMenu("save")
    elseif key == 76 or key == 108 then paint.openMenu("load")
    elseif key == 84 or key == 116 then paint.action("send")
    elseif key == 151 then paint.scrollPalette(-7)
    elseif key == 152 then paint.scrollPalette(7)
    elseif key >= 49 and key <= 55 then paint.color = paint.paletteTop + key - 49
    elseif key == 57 then paint.color = 0
    elseif enter or key == 32 then paint.apply()
    elseif key == 70 or key == 102 then
        paint.bucket, paint.pen, paint.message = not paint.bucket, false, nil
    elseif key == 17 or key == 18 or key == 19 or key == 20 then
        if key == 17 then paint.y = math.max(1, paint.y - 1)
        elseif key == 18 then paint.y = math.min(50, paint.y + 1)
        elseif key == 19 then paint.x = math.max(1, paint.x - 1)
        else paint.x = math.min(50, paint.x + 1) end
        if paint.pen then paint.pixel(paint.x, paint.y) end
    elseif key == 66 or key == 98 then
        paint.bucket = false
        paint.pen = not paint.pen
        if paint.pen then if PHONE.tools then PHONE.tools.paintBegin() end;paint.pixel(paint.x, paint.y) end
    end
end

function PHONE.paint.draw()
    local paint = PHONE.paint
    if paint.menu then
        local saving = paint.menu == "save"
        label(62, 88, saving and "Save painting" or "Load painting", ink, titleFont)
        for i = 1, 5 do
            local y = 137 + (i - 1) * 49
            render.setColor(panel)
            render.drawRect(62, y, 388, 43)
            label(75, y + 11, "Slot " .. i, i == paint.slot and accent or ink)
            label(275, y + 12, paint.slots[i], muted, smallFont)
        end
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(62, 402, 183, 45)
        render.drawRect(264, 402, 186, 45)
        label(74, 415, saving and "Save to slot" or "Load slot", accent)
        label(276, 415, "Cancel", ink)
        label(22, 483, "Up/Down or 1-5 selects | Enter confirms | Backspace cancels", muted, smallFont)
        return
    end
    render.setColor(paint.palette[8])
    render.drawRect(56, 78, 400, 400)
    for y, runs in pairs(paint.runs) do
        for _, run in ipairs(runs) do
            render.setColor(paint.palette[run[3]])
            render.drawRect(56 + (run[1] - 1) * 8, 78 + (y - 1) * 8, run[2] * 8, 8)
        end
    end
    render.setColor(Color(255, 90, 130))
    render.drawRectOutline(55 + (paint.x - 1) * 8, 77 + (paint.y - 1) * 8, 10, 10)
    label(474, 80, "^", accent, smallFont)
    for i = 1, 7 do
        local index = paint.paletteTop + i - 1
        local y = 108 + (i - 1) * 32
        render.setColor(paint.palette[index])
        render.drawRect(466, y, 30, 26)
        if index == paint.color then
            render.setColor(accent)
            render.drawRectOutline(465, y - 1, 32, 28)
        end
    end
    label(468, 328, paint.color == 0 and "E*" or "E", accent, smallFont)
    label(474, 344, "v", accent, smallFont)
    for i, text in ipairs({tostring(paint.slot), "S", "L", "T"}) do
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(466, 365 + (i - 1) * 28, 30, 24)
        label(475, 369 + (i - 1) * 28, text, accent, smallFont)
    end
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(16, 395, 34, 60)
    render.setColor(paint.bucket and accent or panel)
    render.drawRect(16, 340, 34, 44)
    label(21, 352, "Fill", paint.bucket and background or ink, smallFont)
    label(20, 400, "Send", accent, smallFont)
    label(20, 418, "slot", accent, smallFont)
    label(20, 436, "G", muted, smallFont)
    if PHONE.tools then PHONE.tools.drawPaintTools() end
    label(22, 486, paint.message or "Z undo | Y redo | I line | Q box | P pen | F fill", muted, smallFont)
end

calculator.functions = {
    sqrt = math.sqrt, ["x^2"] = function(x) return x * x end,
    log = function(x) return math.log(x) / math.log(10) end, ln = math.log,
    ["%"] = function(x) return x / 100 end, ["+/-"] = function(x) return -x end,
    ["1/x"] = function(x) return 1 / x end,
    sin = math.sin, cos = math.cos, tan = math.tan
}

local function calculatorInput(key)
    local state = calculator
    if state.error and key == "=" then return end
    if key == "C" then
        state.entry, state.left, state.operator, state.error, state.result = "", nil, nil, nil, false
    elseif key == "Backspace" then
        state.entry, state.error, state.result = state.entry:sub(1, -2), nil, false
    elseif key == "." or (#key == 1 and key >= "0" and key <= "9") then
        if state.result or state.error then
            state.entry, state.error, state.result = "", nil, false
        end
        if #state.entry < 12 and (key ~= "." or not state.entry:find(".", 1, true)) then
            if key == "." and (state.entry == "" or state.entry == "-") then state.entry = state.entry .. "0" end
            state.entry = state.entry .. key
        end
    elseif key == "Angle" then
        state.radians = not state.radians
    elseif key == "pi" or key == "e" then
        state.entry = string.format("%.15g", key == "pi" and math.pi or math.exp(1))
        state.error, state.result = nil, not state.operator
    elseif state.functions[key] then
        local number = tonumber(state.entry)
        if not number then return end
        if key == "sin" or key == "cos" or key == "tan" then
            if not state.radians then number = number * math.pi / 180 end
            if key == "tan" and math.abs(math.cos(number)) < 1e-12 then
                state.error = "Tangent is undefined."
                return
            end
        end
        local result = state.functions[key](number)
        if result ~= result or math.abs(result) == math.huge then
            state.error = "Undefined or out of range."
        else
            state.entry = string.format("%.15g", result)
            state.error, state.result = nil, true
        end
    elseif key == "+" or key == "-" or key == "*" or key == "/" or key == "^" or key == "=" then
        local number = tonumber(state.entry)
        if not number then
            if key == "-" and state.entry == "" and not state.error then state.entry = "-"
            elseif state.operator and key ~= "=" then state.operator = key end
            return
        end
        if state.operator then
            if state.operator == "/" and number == 0 then
                state.entry, state.left, state.operator, state.error, state.result = "", nil, nil, nil, false
                gameTab = "menu"
                startupUntil = timer.curtime() + 2.5 + themes.startupDuration
                return
            end
            if state.operator == "+" then number = state.left + number
            elseif state.operator == "-" then number = state.left - number
            elseif state.operator == "*" then number = state.left * number
            elseif state.operator == "^" then number = state.left ^ number
            else number = state.left / number end
        end
        if number ~= number or math.abs(number) == math.huge then
            state.entry, state.left, state.operator, state.error = "", nil, nil, "Result is out of range."
        elseif key == "=" then
            state.entry, state.left, state.operator, state.result = string.format("%.10g", number), nil, nil, true
        else
            state.left, state.operator, state.entry, state.result = number, key, "", false
        end
    end
end

local function calculatorKey(key, enter)
    if key == 172 then gameTab = "menu"; return end
    if enter then calculatorInput("=")
    elseif key == 127 or key == 8 then calculatorInput("Backspace")
    elseif key == 67 or key == 99 then calculatorInput("C")
    elseif key >= 128 and key <= 137 then calculatorInput(tostring(key - 128))
    elseif key >= 138 and key <= 141 then calculatorInput(({"/", "*", "-", "+"})[key - 137])
    elseif key == 143 then calculatorInput(".")
    elseif key >= 32 and key <= 126 then calculatorInput(string.char(key)) end
    return true
end

local function calculatorClick(x, y)
    if x >= 369 and y >= 426 and y < 470 then
        gameTab = "menu"
        return true
    end
    if y >= 146 and y < 177 and x >= 369 then
        calculatorInput("C")
    elseif x >= 16 and x < 496 and y >= 180 and y < 415 then
        local col, row = math.floor((x - 16) / 80), math.floor((y - 180) / 47)
        if (x - 16) % 80 < 74 and (y - 180) % 47 < 41 then calculatorInput(CALCULATOR_KEYS[row * 6 + col + 1]) end
    end
    return true
end

function PHONE.reportTyping(active)
    if room == "" or net.getBytesLeft() < 32 then return end
    local now = timer.curtime()
    if active and now < (PHONE.nextTyping or 0) then return end
    PHONE.nextTyping = active and now + 1 or 0
    net.start("room_typing")
    net.writeBool(active)
    net.send()
end

function PHONE.geometry()
    local sw, sh = render.getResolution()
    -- Reserve a separate button row for the control lock.
    local scale = math.min(0.90, math.max(0.38, (sh - 150) / 512))
    local face = 512 * scale
    local fw, fh = face + 28, face + 128
    local x = math.max(12, sw - fw - 24)
    local y = math.max(12, (sh - fh) * 0.5)
    return x, y, scale, face, fw, fh
end

local function versionStatus(version)
    local serverVersion = tonumber(version)
    if not serverVersion then return "Server version unknown", false end
    local computerVersion = tonumber(CHIP_VERSION)
    local text = "Server v" .. version
    if serverVersion < computerVersion then return text .. " (outdated)", true end
    if serverVersion > computerVersion then return text .. " (ahead)", true end
    return text, false
end

local function sendGameAction(command, value)
    if net.getBytesLeft() < 48 then return false end
    net.start("room_games_action")
    net.writeUInt(command, 3)
    net.writeUInt(value or 0, 32)
    net.send()
    return true
end

local function sendPrivateAction(command, recipient, message)
    if net.getBytesLeft() < #(message or "") + 48 then
        privateStatus = "Connection busy. Try again shortly."
        return false
    end
    net.start("room_private_action")
    net.writeUInt(command, 2)
    if command == 2 then
        net.writeUInt(recipient, 32)
        net.writeString(message)
    end
    net.send()
    return true
end

local function sendChannelAction(id)
    if net.getBytesLeft() < 48 then return end
    channelWaiting = timer.curtime() + 5
    net.start("room_channel_action")
    net.writeUInt(id, 32)
    net.send()
end

local function toggleChannels()
    if channelWaiting then return end
    showChannels = not showChannels
    if showChannels then
        channelStatus = "Loading channels..."
        sendChannelAction(0)
    end
end

local function handlePrivate(key, enter, character)
    if privateWaiting then return end
    if (key == 17 or key == 18) and #privateRecipients > 0 then
        privateSelection = math.max(1, math.min(#privateRecipients, privateSelection + (key == 17 and -1 or 1)))
    elseif key == 151 then
        privateScroll = math.min(privateScroll + 5, math.max(0, #privateRows - 6))
    elseif key == 152 then
        privateScroll = math.max(0, privateScroll - 5)
    elseif key == 127 or key == 8 then
        privateDraft = privateDraft:sub(1, -2)
    elseif enter and privateDraft:find("%S") then
        local recipient = privateRecipients[privateSelection]
        if not recipient then
            privateStatus = "Select a connected computer first."
        else
            privateWaiting = timer.curtime() + 5
            privateStatus = "Sending..."
            if not sendPrivateAction(2, recipient.id, privateDraft) then privateWaiting = nil end
        end
    elseif character and #privateDraft < 160 then
        privateDraft = privateDraft .. character
    end
end


PHONE.contracts={entries={},players={},target=1,selected=1,amount="100",notice="Press R to refresh."}
function PHONE.contracts.send(action,value)
    if net.getBytesLeft()<128 then return end
    net.start("room_contracts")
    net.writeString(action)
    net.writeTable(value or {})
    net.send()
end
function PHONE.contracts.key(key,enter)
    local a=PHONE.contracts
    if key==114 or key==82 then a.send("contracts_list")
    elseif key==19 then a.target=math.max(1,a.target-1)
    elseif key==20 then a.target=math.min(#a.players,a.target+1)
    elseif key==17 then a.selected=math.max(1,a.selected-1)
    elseif key==18 then a.selected=math.min(#a.entries,a.selected+1)
    elseif key==8 or key==127 then a.amount=a.amount:sub(1,-2)
    elseif key>=48 and key<=57 and #a.amount<7 then a.amount=a.amount..string.char(key)
    elseif key==112 or key==80 then
        local p=a.players[a.target]
        if p then a.send("contracts_post",{target=p.id,amount=tonumber(a.amount) or 0}) end
    elseif key==99 or key==67 or enter then
        local e=a.entries[a.selected]
        if e then a.send(enter and "contracts_accept" or "contracts_cancel",{id=e.id}) end
    end
end
net.receive("room_contracts_state",function()
    local data,a=net.readTable(),PHONE.contracts
    if data.entries then a.entries=data.entries end
    if data.players then a.players=data.players end
    a.target=math.max(1,math.min(a.target,#a.players))
    a.selected=math.max(1,math.min(a.selected,#a.entries))
    a.notice=data.notice or ""
end)
function PHONE.contracts.draw()
    local a=PHONE.contracts
    if timer.curtime() >= (a.nextPoll or 0) then
        a.nextPoll = timer.curtime() + 3
        a.send("contracts_list")
    end
    label(22,85,"Contracts",ink,titleFont)
    local p=a.players[a.target]
    label(22,130,"Target: "..(p and p.name or "Refresh to select"),accent,smallFont)
    label(22,158,"Reward: $"..a.amount.."   P posts",ink,smallFont)
    label(22,190,"Left/Right target | Type amount | R refresh",muted,smallFont)
    local first=math.max(1,a.selected-4)
    for i=first,math.min(#a.entries,first+4) do
        local e=a.entries[i]
        label(22,230+(i-first)*36,(i==a.selected and "> " or "")..e.target.." $"..e.amount.." - "..e.state,
            i==a.selected and accent or ink,smallFont)
    end
    label(22,423,"Up/Down selects | Enter accepts | C refunds yours",muted,smallFont)
    label(22,452,a.notice,ink,smallFont)
    label(22,481,"Owner holds funds. Keep room chip running to settle.",muted,smallFont)
end


PHONE.files = {
    names = {}, selected = 1, mode = "list", name = "", text = "", original = "",
    cursor = 0, firstLine = 1, notice = "Refresh to list local .txt files."
}

function PHONE.files.refresh()
    local app = PHONE.files
    local ok, names = pcall(file.find, "*.txt", "nameasc")
    if not ok or type(names) ~= "table" then app.notice="Grant file access, then refresh."; return end
    app.names = {}
    for _, name in ipairs(names) do
        if type(name)=="string" and name:lower():sub(-4)==".txt" then app.names[#app.names+1]=name end
    end
    app.selected=math.max(1,math.min(app.selected,#app.names))
    app.notice="Local Starfall files (data/sf_filedata)"
end

function PHONE.files.filename(name)
    if type(name)~="string" or #name<1 or #name>80 or not name:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_ .-]+$") then return end
    if name:sub(1,1)=="." or name:find("..",1,true) then return end
    if name:lower():sub(-4)~=".txt" then name=name..".txt" end
    return name
end

function PHONE.files.discard(action)
    local app=PHONE.files
    if app.text~=app.original and app.confirm~=action then
        app.confirm=action
        app.notice="Unsaved edits. Repeat this action to discard, or Save first."
        return false
    end
    app.confirm=nil
    return true
end

function PHONE.files.open(name)
    local app=PHONE.files
    if not app.discard("open:"..name) then return end
    local ok, text=pcall(file.read,name)
    if not ok or type(text)~="string" then app.notice="Could not read file."; return end
    if #text>32768 then app.notice="Editor limit is 32768 bytes."; return end
    app.name,app.text,app.original,app.cursor,app.firstLine,app.mode=name,text,text,0,1,"edit"
    app.loadedName=name
    app.overwrite=nil
    app.notice="Editing "..name
end

function PHONE.files.new()
    local app=PHONE.files
    if not app.discard("new") then return end
    app.name,app.text,app.original,app.cursor,app.mode="", "", "", 0,"name"
    app.loadedName,app.overwrite=nil,nil
    app.notice="Enter a new filename, then press Enter."
end

function PHONE.files.save()
    local app=PHONE.files
    local name=app.filename(app.name)
    if not name then app.notice="Use letters, numbers, spaces, dots, underscores or hyphens."; return end
    local ok, current=pcall(function()
        if file.exists(name) then return file.read(name) end
    end)
    if not ok then app.notice="Could not check file. Grant file access."; return end
    if current~=nil and (name~=app.loadedName or current~=app.original) and app.overwrite~=name then
        app.overwrite=name
        app.notice="File exists or changed elsewhere. Press Save again to overwrite."
        return
    end
    local written=pcall(function()
        file.write(name,app.text)
        assert(file.read(name)==app.text)
    end)
    if not written then app.notice="Save failed. Your edits are still in memory."; return end
    app.name,app.loadedName,app.original,app.overwrite=name,name,app.text,nil
    app.mode,app.notice="edit","Saved "..name
end

function PHONE.files.lines()
    local app=PHONE.files
    if app.layoutText==app.text then return app.layout end
    local lines, start={},1
    while true do
        local last=app.text:find("\n",start,true)
        lines[#lines+1]={text=app.text:sub(start,last and last-1 or #app.text),start=start-1}
        if not last then break end
        start=last+1
    end
    app.layoutText,app.layout=app.text,lines
    return lines
end

function PHONE.files.position()
    local app=PHONE.files
    local lines=app.lines()
    for row=#lines,1,-1 do
        if app.cursor>=lines[row].start then return row,app.cursor-lines[row].start,lines end
    end
    return 1,0,lines
end

function PHONE.files.boundary(position, direction)
    local text=PHONE.files.text
    position=math.max(0,math.min(#text,position))
    while position>0 and position<#text do
        local byte=text:byte(position+1)
        if byte<128 or byte>=192 then break end
        position=position+direction
    end
    return position
end

function PHONE.files.insert(value)
    local app=PHONE.files
    if #app.text+#value>32768 then app.notice="Editor limit is 32768 bytes."; return end
    app.text=app.text:sub(1,app.cursor)..value..app.text:sub(app.cursor+1)
    app.cursor=app.cursor+#value
    app.confirm,app.overwrite=nil,nil
end

function PHONE.files.key(key,enter,character)
    local app=PHONE.files
    if key==164 then app.save(); return end
    if key==9 then app.mode=app.mode=="edit" and "name" or "edit"; return end
    if app.mode=="list" then
        if key==17 then app.selected=math.max(1,app.selected-1)
        elseif key==18 then app.selected=math.min(#app.names,app.selected+1)
        elseif enter and app.names[app.selected] then
            app.open(app.names[app.selected])
        elseif key==110 or key==78 then app.new()
        elseif key==114 or key==82 then app.refresh() end
    elseif app.mode=="name" then
        if enter then app.mode="edit"
        elseif key==8 or key==127 then app.name=app.name:sub(1,-2)
        elseif character and #app.name<80 then app.name=app.name..character end
    elseif app.mode=="edit" then
        local row,column,lines=app.position()
        if key==19 then app.cursor=app.boundary(app.cursor-1,-1)
        elseif key==20 then app.cursor=app.boundary(app.cursor+1,1)
        elseif key==17 or key==18 then
            local nextRow=math.max(1,math.min(#lines,row+(key==17 and -1 or 1)))
            app.cursor=app.boundary(lines[nextRow].start+math.min(column,#lines[nextRow].text),-1)
        elseif key==151 or key==152 then
            local nextRow=math.max(1,math.min(#lines,row+(key==151 and -12 or 12)))
            app.cursor=app.boundary(lines[nextRow].start+math.min(column,#lines[nextRow].text),-1)
        elseif key==8 or key==127 then
            local previous=app.boundary(app.cursor-1,-1)
            app.text=app.text:sub(1,previous)..app.text:sub(app.cursor+1)
            app.cursor=previous
            app.confirm,app.overwrite=nil,nil
        elseif enter then app.insert("\n")
        elseif character then app.insert(character) end
    end
end

function PHONE.files.click(x,y)
    local app=PHONE.files
    if y>=84 and y<118 then
        if x<130 then
            if app.discard("list") then app.mode="list"; app.refresh() end
        elseif x<250 then app.new()
        elseif x<370 then app.save()
        else app.refresh() end
        return
    end
    if y>=123 and y<154 then app.mode="name"; return end
    if app.mode=="list" and y>=164 and y<434 then
        local first=math.max(1,app.selected-13)
        local name=app.names[first+math.floor((y-164)/18)]
        if name then app.open(name) end
    elseif app.mode~="list" and y>=164 and y<434 then
        local lines=app.lines()
        local row=math.min(#lines,app.firstLine+math.floor((y-164)/18))
        render.setFont(font)
        local column=math.max(0,math.floor((x-54)/render.getTextSize("M"))+(app.horizontal or 0))
        app.cursor=app.boundary(lines[row].start+math.min(column,#lines[row].text),-1)
        app.mode="edit"
    end
end

function PHONE.files.draw()
    local app=PHONE.files
    for i,text in ipairs({"Files","New","Save F2","Refresh"}) do
        local x=16+(i-1)*120
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(x,84,114,32)
        label(x+9,92,text,accent,smallFont)
    end
    label(22,130,"Name: "..app.name..(app.text~=app.original and " *" or ""),app.mode=="name" and accent or ink,smallFont)
    if app.mode=="list" then
        local first=math.max(1,app.selected-13)
        for i=first,math.min(#app.names,first+14) do
            label(22,166+(i-first)*18,(i==app.selected and "> " or "")..app.names[i],
                i==app.selected and accent or ink,smallFont)
        end
        if #app.names==0 then label(22,180,"No .txt files found. Choose New.",muted,smallFont) end
    else
        local row,column,lines=app.position()
        if row<app.firstLine then app.firstLine=row end
        if row>=app.firstLine+15 then app.firstLine=row-14 end
        app.horizontal=math.max(0,column-40)
        render.setFont(font)
        local charWidth=render.getTextSize("M")
        local available=math.max(1,math.floor(440/charWidth))
        for line=app.firstLine,math.min(#lines,app.firstLine+14) do
            local y=166+(line-app.firstLine)*18
            label(18,y,tostring(line),muted,smallFont)
            local content=lines[line].text:sub(app.horizontal+1,app.horizontal+available)
            label(54,y,content,ink,font)
        end
        if app.mode=="edit" and math.floor(timer.curtime()*2)%2==0 then
            render.setColor(accent)
            render.drawRect(54+(column-app.horizontal)*charWidth,166+(row-app.firstLine)*18,1,17)
        end
        label(22,444,"Line "..row.."  Column "..(column+1).."  "..#app.text.."/32768 bytes",muted,smallFont)
    end
    label(22,465,app.notice,ink,smallFont)
    label(22,485,"F2 Save | Tab filename/editor | Ctrl Desktop",muted,smallFont)
end


PHONE.maker = {
    name="My Website", filename="my_website.txt", pages={{route="home",items={}}},
    page=1, selected=0, property=1, notice="Add an element, then edit its properties."
}
PHONE.maker.properties = {"Site","File","Subsite","Text","X","Y","Width","Height","Message","Go to"}
PHONE.maker.keys = {"name","filename","route","text","x","y","w","h","message","target"}

function PHONE.maker.current()
    local app=PHONE.maker
    local page=app.pages[app.page]
    return page,page.items[app.selected]
end

function PHONE.maker.value(index)
    local app=PHONE.maker
    local page,item=app.current()
    if index<=2 then return app[app.keys[index]] end
    if index==3 then return page.route end
    return item and tostring(item[app.keys[index]] or "") or ""
end

function PHONE.maker.set(index,value)
    local app=PHONE.maker
    local page,item=app.current()
    if index<=2 then app[app.keys[index]]=value
    elseif index==3 then page.route=value
    elseif item then item[app.keys[index]]=value end
    app.overwrite=nil
end

function PHONE.maker.add(kind)
    local app=PHONE.maker
    local page=app.current()
    if #page.items>=24 then app.notice="Limit is 24 elements per subsite."; return end
    page.items[#page.items+1]={
        type=kind,text=kind=="text" and "New text" or (kind=="button" and "Click me" or "Type here"),
        x="28",y=tostring(110+(#page.items%5)*65),w="216",h="44",
        message="Button clicked.",target=""
    }
    app.selected,app.property=#page.items,4
end

function PHONE.maker.document()
    local app=PHONE.maker
    local result,seen={},{}
    for _,page in ipairs(app.pages) do
        if page.route=="" or MAXWELLNET.route(page.route)~=page.route or seen[page.route] then
            return nil,"Use unique subsite names with letters, numbers, slashes, underscores or hyphens."
        end
        seen[page.route]=true
        result[page.route]={}
        for index,item in ipairs(page.items) do
            local copy={type=item.type,text=item.text:sub(1,80),message=item.message:sub(1,120),target=item.target}
            for _,key in ipairs({"x","y","w","h"}) do
                local value=tonumber(item[key])
                if not value or value~=value or value%1~=0 or value<0 or value>1023 then return nil,"Positions and sizes must be whole positive numbers." end
                copy[key]=value
            end
            if copy.w<1 or copy.h<1 or copy.h>512 or copy.w>512 or copy.x+copy.w>512 or copy.y+copy.h>1023 then
                return nil,"Elements must fit inside the 512 x 1023 page."
            end
            copy.id="element_"..index
            result[page.route][#result[page.route]+1]=copy
        end
    end
    if not seen.home then return nil,"Keep a home subsite as the entry page." end
    for _,items in pairs(result) do
        for _,item in ipairs(items) do
            if item.type=="button" and item.target~="" and not seen[item.target] then return nil,"A button links to a subsite that does not exist." end
        end
    end
    return result
end


function PHONE.maker.luaLiteral(value)
    local kind=type(value)
    if kind=="string" then return string.format("%q",value) end
    if kind=="number" then
        assert(value==value and value~=math.huge and value~=-math.huge,"Invalid number")
        return tostring(value)
    end
    if kind=="boolean" then return tostring(value) end
    assert(kind=="table","Unsupported website data")
    local keys,parts={},{}
    for key in pairs(value) do keys[#keys+1]=key end
    table.sort(keys,function(a,b) return tostring(a)<tostring(b) end)
    for _,key in ipairs(keys) do
        parts[#parts+1]="["..PHONE.maker.luaLiteral(key).."]="..PHONE.maker.luaLiteral(value[key])
    end
    return "{"..table.concat(parts,",").."}"
end

function PHONE.maker.export()
    local app=PHONE.maker
    local pages,err=app.document()
    if not pages then app.notice=err; return end
    local filename=PHONE.files.filename(app.filename)
    if not filename or app.name=="" then app.notice="Enter a site name and valid .txt filename."; return end
    local source="--".."@name MaxwellNET Website\n".."--".."@shared\n\nlocal NAME = "..string.format("%q",app.name:sub(1,32))..
        "\n".."\
-- Publish discovery metadata independently of page requests.\
hook.add(\"remote\",\"made_site_discovery\",function(_,_,protocol)\
    if protocol==\"starfall_website_discover_v1\" then return chip():entIndex(),NAME end\
end)\
if SERVER then\
    local function announce()\
        pcall(hook.runRemote,nil,\"starfall_website_register_v1\",chip():entIndex(),NAME)\
    end\
    timer.simple(0.5,announce)\
    timer.create(\"made_site_announce\",3,0,announce)\
end\
\
".."local PAGES = "..app.luaLiteral(pages).."\n"
    source=source..[=[
local inputs = setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
if SERVER then
    hook.add("remote","made_website",function(sender,user,protocol,first,second,third,fourth)
        if protocol=="starfall_website_discover_v1" then return chip():entIndex(),NAME end
        if protocol=="starfall_website_page_v1" then
            local route=second or "home"
            local items=PAGES[route] or PAGES.home
            local options={512,buttons={},inputs={}}
            for _,item in ipairs(items) do
                options[1]=math.min(1023,math.max(options[1],item.y+item.h))
                if item.type=="button" then
                    options.buttons[#options.buttons+1]={item.x,item.y,item.w,item.h,item.text,item.id}
                elseif item.type=="input" then
                    options.inputs[#options.inputs+1]={item.x,item.y,item.w,item.h,item.text,item.id,80,""}
                end
            end
            return chip():entIndex(),NAME,NAME,{"Made with MaxwellOS Website Maker"},
                false,{"Roboto",22,500},false,false,options
        end
        if not user or not user:isValid() or not user:isPlayer() then return end
        if protocol=="starfall_website_screen_v1" then
            local frame={{"rect",0,0,512,512,21,32,48},{"text",24,24,464,36,245,250,255,NAME}}
            for _,item in ipairs(PAGES[second or "home"] or PAGES.home) do
                if item.type=="text" then
                    frame[#frame+1]={"text",item.x,item.y,item.w,item.h,245,250,255,item.text}
                elseif item.type=="button" then
                    frame[#frame+1]={"rect",item.x,item.y,item.w,item.h,45,115,108}
                    local inset=math.min(4,item.w/4,item.h/4)
                    frame[#frame+1]={"text",item.x+inset,item.y+inset,item.w-2*inset,item.h-2*inset,245,250,255,item.text}
                end
            end
            return frame
        elseif protocol=="starfall_website_button_v1" then
            for _,item in ipairs(PAGES[fourth or "home"] or PAGES.home) do
                if item.type=="button" and item.id==first then
                    local values=type(second)=="table" and second or {}
                    local text=item.message
                    for _,field in ipairs(PAGES[fourth or "home"] or PAGES.home) do
                        if field.type=="input" and type(values[field.id])=="string" then
                            text=text:gsub("{input}",function() return values[field.id]:sub(1,80) end)
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    return {ok=true,message=text,sound="click",route=item.target~="" and item.target or nil}
                end
            end
        elseif protocol=="starfall_website_input_v1" then
            inputs[user]=inputs[user] or {}
            inputs[user][first]=tostring(second):sub(1,80)
            return {ok=true,message="Entered: "..inputs[user][first],sound="success"}
        end
    end)
end
]=]
    if #source>32768 then app.notice="Export exceeds the 32768-byte editor limit."; return end
    local ok,exists=pcall(file.exists,filename)
    if not ok then app.notice="Grant file access before exporting."; return end
    if exists and app.overwrite~=filename then
        app.overwrite=filename
        app.notice="File exists. Export again to replace it."
        return
    end
    local saved=pcall(function()
        file.write(filename,source)
        assert(file.read(filename)==source)
    end)
    if not saved then app.notice="Export failed. Check file permissions."; return end
    app.filename,app.overwrite=filename,nil
    app.notice="Exported "..filename.." to data/sf_filedata."
end

function PHONE.maker.key(key,enter,character)
    local app=PHONE.maker
    if key==164 then app.export()
    elseif key==9 then app.property=app.property%#app.properties+1
    elseif key==17 then app.property=math.max(1,app.property-1)
    elseif key==18 then app.property=math.min(#app.properties,app.property+1)
    elseif key==151 or key==152 then
        local page=app.current()
        app.selected=math.max(0,math.min(#page.items,app.selected+(key==151 and -1 or 1)))
    elseif key==8 or key==127 then app.set(app.property,app.value(app.property):sub(1,-2))
    elseif character then
        local value=app.value(app.property)
        local limit=app.property==1 and 32 or (app.property==10 and 64 or 120)
        if #value+#character<=limit then app.set(app.property,value..character) end
    end
end

function PHONE.maker.click(x,y)
    local app=PHONE.maker
    if y>=84 and y<116 then
        if x<120 then
            if #app.pages>=16 then app.notice="Limit is 16 subsites."; return end
            app.pages[#app.pages+1]={route="page_"..(#app.pages+1),items={}}
            app.page,app.selected,app.property=#app.pages,0,3
        elseif x<200 then app.page=math.max(1,app.page-1); app.selected=0
        elseif x<280 then app.page=math.min(#app.pages,app.page+1); app.selected=0
        elseif x<396 then
            local page=app.current()
