            if app.selected>0 then table.remove(page.items,app.selected); app.selected=math.min(app.selected,#page.items) end
        else app.export() end
    elseif y>=120 and y<152 then
        if x<176 then app.add("text")
        elseif x<336 then app.add("button")
        else
            local count=0
            for _,item in ipairs((app.current()).items) do if item.type=="input" then count=count+1 end end
            if count>=8 then app.notice="Limit is 8 inputs per subsite." else app.add("input") end
        end
    elseif x<264 and y>=164 and y<444 then
        app.property=math.min(#app.properties,math.floor((y-164)/28)+1)
    elseif x>=272 and y>=164 and y<248 then
        local page=app.current()
        local first=math.max(1,app.selected-3)
        app.selected=math.min(#page.items,first+math.floor((y-164)/21))
    end
end

function PHONE.maker.draw()
    local app=PHONE.maker
    local page,item=app.current()
    local toolbar={{16,100,"+ Subsite"},{120,76,"< Page"},{200,76,"Page >"},{280,112,"Remove"},{396,100,"Export F2"}}
    for _,button in ipairs(toolbar) do
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(button[1],84,button[2],32)
        label(button[1]+5,92,button[3],accent,smallFont)
    end
    for i,name in ipairs({"+ Text","+ Button","+ Input"}) do
        local x=16+(i-1)*160
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(x,120,152,32)
        label(x+8,128,name,accent,smallFont)
    end
    for i,name in ipairs(app.properties) do
        local value=app.value(i)
        local y=164+(i-1)*28
        render.setColor(i==app.property and accent or panel)
        render.drawRect(16,y,244,25)
        label(22,y+4,name..": "..value:sub(-22),i==app.property and background or ink,smallFont)
    end
    local first=math.max(1,app.selected-3)
    for i=first,math.min(#page.items,first+3) do
        label(272,168+(i-first)*21,(i==app.selected and "> " or "")..i.." "..page.items[i].type,ink,smallFont)
    end
    render.setColor(Color(21,32,48))
    render.drawRect(272,254,220,194)
    label(280,258,"/"..page.route,accent,smallFont)
    local scale=0.35
    for _,element in ipairs(page.items) do
        local x,y=tonumber(element.x) or 0,tonumber(element.y) or 0
        local w,h=tonumber(element.w) or 1,tonumber(element.h) or 1
        if x>=0 and y>=0 and x+w<=512 and y+h<=512 then
            local px,py=278+x*scale,265+y*scale
            if element.type~="text" then
                render.setColor(element.type=="button" and Color(45,115,108) or panel)
                render.drawRect(px,py,math.max(1,w*scale),math.max(1,h*scale))
            end
            label(px,py,element.text:sub(1,14),ink,smallFont)
        end
    end
    label(22,463,app.notice,ink,smallFont)
    label(22,485,"Tab/Up/Down property | PgUp/PgDn element | F2 Export",muted,smallFont)
end

local function sendPaymentAction(action, id, amount)
    if net.getBytesLeft() < 64 then
        paymentStatus = "Connection busy. Try again shortly."
        return false
    end
    net.start("room_payment")
    net.writeUInt(action, 2)
    net.writeUInt(id or 0, 32)
    net.writeUInt(amount or 0, 32)
    net.send()
    return true
end

local function refreshPayments()
    paymentStatus = "Loading recipients..."
    sendPaymentAction(0)
end

local function handlePayment(key, enter)
    if paymentWaiting then return end
    if paymentReview then
        if key == 127 or key == 8 then
            sendPaymentAction(3)
            paymentReview, paymentStatus = nil, ""
        elseif enter then
            local token = paymentReview.token
            paymentWaiting = timer.curtime() + 5
            paymentStatus = "Opening payment prompt..."
            if not sendPaymentAction(2, token) then paymentWaiting = nil end
        end
        return
    end
    if key == 17 then
        paymentSelection = math.max(1, paymentSelection - 1)
    elseif key == 18 then
        paymentSelection = math.max(1, math.min(#paymentRecipients, paymentSelection + 1))
    elseif key >= 48 and key <= 57 and #paymentAmount < 9 then
        paymentAmount = paymentAmount .. string.char(key)
    elseif key == 127 or key == 8 then
        paymentAmount = paymentAmount:sub(1, -2)
    elseif key == 82 or key == 114 then
        refreshPayments()
    elseif enter then
        local recipient = paymentRecipients[paymentSelection]
        local amount = tonumber(paymentAmount)
        if not recipient then
            paymentStatus = "Select a recipient first."
        elseif not amount or amount < 1 then
            paymentStatus = "Enter a positive whole-dollar amount."
        else
            paymentWaiting = timer.curtime() + 5
            paymentStatus = "Checking recipient..."
            if not sendPaymentAction(1, recipient.id, amount) then paymentWaiting = nil end
        end
    end
end

function PHONE.extras.stop()
    local extras = PHONE.extras
    if extras.sound then pcall(function() extras.sound:destroy() end) end
    extras.sound = nil
    extras.message, extras.nowPlaying = nil, nil
end

function PHONE.extras.activate(tab)
    local extras = PHONE.extras
    local entry = extras[tab][extras.selected[tab]]
    if tab == "credits" then
        if entry[3] then
            setClipboardText(entry[3])
            extras.message = "Link copied: " .. entry[3]
        end
        return
    end
    extras.stop()
    local ok = pcall(function()
        extras.sound = sound.create(player(), "music/" .. entry[2])
        extras.sound:setSoundLevel(0)
        extras.sound:play()
        extras.sound:setVolume(0.5)
    end)
    if ok then
        extras.nowPlaying = "Playing " .. entry[1]
        extras.message = extras.nowPlaying
    else
        extras.stop()
        extras.message = "Playback unavailable. Check sound permission."
    end
end

function PHONE.extras.moveMusic(delta)
    local extras = PHONE.extras
    local index = math.max(1, math.min(#extras.music, extras.selected.music + delta))
    extras.selected.music = index
    extras.musicTop = math.max(index - 3, math.min(index, extras.musicTop))
end

function PHONE.extras.key(tab, key, enter)
    local extras = PHONE.extras
    if enter then extras.activate(tab)
    elseif tab == "music" then
        if key == 17 then extras.moveMusic(-1)
        elseif key == 18 then extras.moveMusic(1)
        elseif key == 151 then extras.moveMusic(-4)
        elseif key == 152 then extras.moveMusic(4)
        elseif key == 32 then extras.stop() end
    elseif key == 17 then extras.selected[tab] = math.max(1, extras.selected[tab] - 1)
    elseif key == 18 then extras.selected[tab] = math.min(#extras[tab], extras.selected[tab] + 1) end
end

function PHONE.extras.click(tab, x, y)
    local extras = PHONE.extras
    if tab == "music" and y >= 400 and y < 446 then
        if x < 170 then extras.stop()
        elseif x < 333 then extras.moveMusic(-4)
        else extras.moveMusic(4) end
        return
    end
    if y < 94 or y >= 394 or (y - 94) % 75 >= 64 then return end
    local first = tab == "music" and extras.musicTop or 1
    local index = first + math.floor((y - 94) / 75)
    if extras[tab][index] then
        extras.selected[tab] = index
        extras.activate(tab)
    end
end

function PHONE.extras.draw(tab)
    local extras = PHONE.extras
    local first = tab == "music" and extras.musicTop or 1
    for i = first, math.min(first + 3, #extras[tab]) do
        local entry = extras[tab][i]
        local y = 94 + (i - first) * 75
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(16, y, 480, 64)
        label(27, y + 9, entry[1], extras.selected[tab] == i and accent or ink)
        if tab == "music" then
            label(27, y + 34, entry[3] or "Half-Life 2", muted, smallFont)
        elseif tab == "credits" then
            label(27, y + 34, entry[2], muted, smallFont)
            if entry[3] then label(402, y + 34, "Copy link", accent, smallFont) end
        end
    end
    if tab == "music" then
        label(27, 412, "Stop", accent)
        label(181, 412, "< Prev page", accent)
        label(345, 412, "Next page >", accent)
    end
    if extras.message then
        for i, line in ipairs(wrapText(extras.message, 460, smallFont)) do
            label(22, 451 + (i - 1) * 16, line, muted, smallFont)
        end
    end
    label(22, 487, tab == "credits" and "Up/Down selects | Enter copies link" or "Arrows select | PgUp/PgDn scrolls | Enter plays | Space stops", muted, smallFont)
end

function PHONE.syncSoloGame()
    if not isOwner then return end
    if PHONE.runningSolo then hook.remove("think", "maxwellos_solo_game") end
    PHONE.squadron.lastTick = nil
    PHONE.runningSolo = nil
    if not poweredOn then return end
    local update
    if gameTab == "squadron" then update = PHONE.squadron.update
    elseif gameTab == "mines" then
        update = function()
            if mineSearch and not mineSearch() then mineSearch = nil end
        end
    end
    if not update then return end
    PHONE.runningSolo = gameTab
    hook.add("think", "maxwellos_solo_game", function()
        local now = timer.curtime()
        if now >= startupUntil then update(now) end
    end)
end

local function changeGameTab(tab)
    if gameTab=="windows" and tab~="windows" then
        timer.remove("room_website_refresh");timer.remove("room_website_screen_refresh")
    end
    if room == "" and (MULTIPLAYER_TABS[tab] or tab == "payments" or tab == "contracts") then return end
    if gameTab == "payments" and paymentWaiting then return end
    if MULTIPLAYER_TABS[tab] and tab ~= gameWatchTab
        and (currentGame or (gameEntryUntil and timer.curtime() < gameEntryUntil)) then return end
    if MULTIPLAYER_TABS[gameTab] and tab ~= gameTab and not sendGameAction(0) then return end
    if MULTIPLAYER_TABS[tab] then
        if not sendGameAction(1, MULTIPLAYER_TABS[tab]) then return end
        if tab ~= gameWatchTab then
            matches, currentGame, selectedMatch, gameStatus = {}, nil, 1, ""
            chessFrom, chessPromotion = nil, false
            chessHints, chessMoveScroll = {}, 0
            battleshipCursor, battleshipVertical = 1, false
        end
        gameWatchTab = tab
    end
    if showPayments then
        sendPaymentAction(3)
        showPayments, paymentReview = false, nil
    end
    if gameTab == "website" and tab ~= "website" then
        timer.remove("room_website_refresh")
        timer.remove("room_website_screen_refresh")
        websiteFrame, websiteFrameSite = nil, nil
    end
    if tab == "calculator" and gameTab ~= tab then calculator.streamGreeting = math.random(10) == 1 end
    if gameTab == "credits" then PHONE.extras.message = nil end
    if tab == "music" and PHONE.extras.sound then PHONE.extras.message = PHONE.extras.nowPlaying end
    if gameTab ~= tab then PHONE.appHost.switch(tab) end
    gameTab = tab
    PHONE.syncSoloGame()
    local arcade = tab == "squadron" and 1 or (tab == "pong" and 2 or 0)
    if arcade ~= (PHONE.wireArcade or 0) and net.getBytesLeft() >= 32 then
        PHONE.wireArcade = arcade
        net.start("room_arcade_repeat")
        net.writeUInt(arcade, 2)
        net.send()
    end
    if tab == "website" then websiteControls.startedAt=timer.curtime(); startWebsitePolling() end
    if tab=="windows" and PHONE.tools and PHONE.tools.windows[PHONE.tools.focus] then PHONE.tools.focusWindow(PHONE.tools.focus) end
    if tab ~= "menu" then lastApp = tab end
    if tab == "files" then PHONE.files.refresh() end
    if tab == "contracts" then PHONE.contracts.send("contracts_list") end
    if tab == "payments" then
        showPayments = true
        refreshPayments()
    end
end

PHONE.toolsFont=render.createFont("Roboto",14,500)
PHONE.tools=require("mos_desktop_tools.txt")({
    host=PHONE.appHost,paint=PHONE.paint,
    theme=function() return {background=background,panel=panel,accent=accent,text=ink,muted=muted} end,
    label=function(x,y,text,color) label(x,y,text,color,PHONE.toolsFont) end,
    wrap=function(text,width) return wrapText(text,width,PHONE.toolsFont) end,
    entries=function() return menuOptions end,
    setEntries=function(entries) menuOptions=entries;selectedGame=math.max(1,math.min(selectedGame,#entries));PHONE.desktopScroll(0,true) end,
    selection=function() return selectedGame end,select=function(index) selectedGame=index end,
    desktopTop=function() return PHONE.desktopTop end,
    scrollDesktop=function(delta) PHONE.desktopScroll(delta,false) end,
    tab=function() return gameTab end,room=function() return room end,
    icon=function(entry,x,y) PHONE.desktopIcon(entry,x,y,false) end,
    backgroundApps=function()
        local apps={}
        if room~="" then apps[#apps+1]="chatrooms" end
        if PHONE.extras.sound then apps[#apps+1]="music" end
        if gameWatchTab and (currentGame or (gameEntryUntil and timer.curtime()<gameEntryUntil)) then apps[#apps+1]=gameWatchTab end
        return apps
    end,
    running=function() return isOwner and poweredOn and timer.curtime()>=startupUntil end,
    open=function(tab) if tab==false or tab=="chatrooms" then tab=nil end;changeGameTab(tab) end,
    rebuild=function() PHONE.appHost.rebuild() end,
    unloadApp=function(id)
        for ticket,request in pairs(PHONE.appHost.pending) do if request.instance.id==id then PHONE.appHost.pending[ticket]=nil end end
        for ticket,request in pairs(PHONE.appHost.autoPending) do if request.instance.id==id then PHONE.appHost.autoPending[ticket]=nil end end
        for key in pairs(PHONE.appHost.autoCache) do if key:sub(1,#id+1)==id.."/" then PHONE.appHost.autoCache[key]=nil end end
        MOS_SERVICES.remove(id)
    end,
    mouseHeld=function() return hasPermission("input") and input.isMouseDown(MOUSE.LEFT) end,
    pointer=function()
        if not hasPermission("input") then return nil,nil,false end
        local held=input.isMouseDown(MOUSE.LEFT)
        if PHONE.open and hasPermission("input.enablecursor") then
            local mx,my=input.getCursorPos();local x,y,scale=PHONE.geometry()
            return (mx-x-14)/scale,(my-y-46)/scale,held
        elseif PHONE.screenMouseActive and PHONE.screenMouseActive() then
            local user,screen=player(),PHONE.mouseScreen
            if user:getEyeTrace().Entity==screen then local x,y=render.cursorPos(user,screen);return x,y,held end
        end
        return nil,nil,held
    end,
    profile=function() return {theme=settings.theme,name=computerName} end,
    validateProfile=function(profile)
        return type(profile)=="table" and type(profile.theme)=="number" and themes[profile.theme]~=nil
            and type(profile.name)=="string" and #profile.name>=1 and #profile.name<=24 and profile.name:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$")~=nil
    end,
    applyProfile=function(profile)
        settings.theme,computerName,settings.name=profile.theme,profile.name,profile.name
        applyTheme();PHONE.wallpaper.restored=false;PHONE.wallpaper.restore()
    end,
    activateWindow=function(tab)
        if tab==false then tab=nil end
        changeGameTab(tab);gameTab="windows"
        if PHONE.tools.webVisible() then startWebsitePolling() end
    end,
    renderWindow=function(tab)
        if tab==false then tab=nil end
        local previous,active=gameTab,PHONE.appHost.active
        gameTab=tab
        if type(tab)=="string" and tab:sub(1,4)=="mos:" then PHONE.appHost.active=PHONE.appHost.installed[tab:sub(5)] end
        PHONE.tools.inWindowRender=true
        local ok,err=pcall(PHONE.drawComputerScreen)
        PHONE.tools.inWindowRender=false
        gameTab,PHONE.appHost.active=previous,active
        if not ok then error(err) end
    end,
    clickWindow=function(win,x,y,right)
        local previous=gameTab;gameTab=win.tab or nil
        local ok,err=pcall(PHONE.computerClickAt,x,y,right)
        if gameTab=="menu" then win.closeRequested=true elseif gameTab~=win.tab and gameTab~="windows" then win.tab=gameTab or false end
        gameTab=previous;if not ok then error(err) end
    end,
    stopWebsite=function() timer.remove("room_website_refresh");timer.remove("room_website_screen_refresh") end,
    keyWindow=function(win,key) if PHONE.tools.dispatchKey then PHONE.tools.dispatchKey(win,key) end end
})
PHONE.tools.capture(menuOptions)

local function toggleLastApp()
    if joinUntil then return end
    if gameTab == "menu" then
        if room == "" and (MULTIPLAYER_TABS[lastApp] or lastApp == "payments" or lastApp == "contracts") then lastApp = false end
        if lastApp ~= false then changeGameTab(lastApp) end
        return
    end
    joining, versionWarning, password = nil, nil, ""
    showPrivate, showChannels = false, false
    changeGameTab("menu")
end

local function newMinefield()
    return {
        cursor = 1, mines = {}, open = {}, flags = {}, opened = 0, flagCount = 0,
        count = MINE_DIFFICULTIES[mineDifficulty].count, noGuess = mineNoGuess
    }
end

local mineNeighbors = {}
local function neighbors(cell)
    if mineNeighbors[cell] then return mineNeighbors[cell] end
    local x = (cell - 1) % MINE_SIDE
    local y = math.floor((cell - 1) / MINE_SIDE)
    local cells = {}
    for row = math.max(0, y - 1), math.min(MINE_SIDE - 1, y + 1) do
        for column = math.max(0, x - 1), math.min(MINE_SIDE - 1, x + 1) do
            local nextCell = row * MINE_SIDE + column + 1
            if nextCell ~= cell then cells[#cells + 1] = nextCell end
        end
    end
    mineNeighbors[cell] = cells
    return cells
end

local function generateMines(first, count)
    local safe = {[first] = true}
    for _, neighbor in ipairs(neighbors(first)) do safe[neighbor] = true end
    local choices, mines = {}, {}
    for i = 1, MINE_CELLS do
        if not safe[i] then choices[#choices + 1] = i end
    end
    for i = 1, count do
        local index = math.random(i, #choices)
        choices[i], choices[index] = choices[index], choices[i]
        mines[choices[i]] = true
    end
    return mines
end

local function revealMineArea(field, cell)
    local queue, queued = {cell}, {[cell] = true}
    local index = 1
    while index <= #queue do
        local current = queue[index]
        local adjacent = neighbors(current)
        local count = 0
        for _, nextCell in ipairs(adjacent) do
            if field.mines[nextCell] then count = count + 1 end
        end
        field.open[current] = count
        field.opened = field.opened + 1
        if count == 0 then
            for _, nextCell in ipairs(adjacent) do
                if not queued[nextCell] and not field.open[nextCell] and not field.flags[nextCell] then
                    queued[nextCell] = true
                    queue[#queue + 1] = nextCell
                end
            end
        end
        index = index + 1
    end
end

local function mineConstraints(field)
    local constraints = {}
    for cell, count in pairs(field.open) do
        local cells, set, remaining = {}, {}, count
        for _, neighbor in ipairs(neighbors(cell)) do
            if field.flags[neighbor] then remaining = remaining - 1
            elseif not field.open[neighbor] then
                cells[#cells + 1] = neighbor
                set[neighbor] = true
            end
        end
        if #cells > 0 then constraints[#constraints + 1] = {cells = cells, set = set, remaining = remaining} end
    end
    return constraints
end

local function applyMineDeduction(field, cells, remaining)
    if remaining ~= 0 and remaining ~= #cells then return false end
    local changed = false
    for _, cell in ipairs(cells) do
        if not field.open[cell] and not field.flags[cell] then
            if remaining == 0 then
                revealMineArea(field, cell)
            else
                field.flags[cell] = true
            end
            changed = true
        end
    end
    return changed
end

local function solvesWithoutGuessing(mines, first, count, checkpoint)
    local simulation = {mines = mines, open = {}, flags = {}, opened = 0}
    revealMineArea(simulation, first)
    while simulation.opened < MINE_CELLS - count do
        if not checkpoint() then return false end
        local constraints = mineConstraints(simulation)
        local changed = false
        for _, rule in ipairs(constraints) do
            if applyMineDeduction(simulation, rule.cells, rule.remaining) then changed = true end
        end
        if not changed then
            local comparisons = 0
            for _, small in ipairs(constraints) do
                for _, large in ipairs(constraints) do
                    comparisons = comparisons + 1
                    if comparisons % 48 == 0 and not checkpoint() then return false end
                    if #small.cells < #large.cells then
                        local subset = true
                        for _, cell in ipairs(small.cells) do
                            if not large.set[cell] then subset = false; break end
                        end
                        if subset then
                            local difference = {}
                            for _, cell in ipairs(large.cells) do
                                if not small.set[cell] then difference[#difference + 1] = cell end
                            end
                            if applyMineDeduction(simulation, difference, large.remaining - small.remaining) then
                                changed = true
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if changed then break end
            end
        end
        if not changed then return false end
    end
    return true
end

local function playMineCell(field, cell)
    if field.mines[cell] then
        playChatSound("explosion")
        field.result = "Mine hit. Press R to try again."
        return
    end
    revealMineArea(field, cell)
    if field.opened == MINE_CELLS - field.count then
        field.result = "You cleared the minefield! Press R to play again."
    end
end

local function revealMineCell()
    local field = minefield
    local cell = field.cursor
    if field.result or field.flags[cell] or field.open[cell] or field.generating then return end
    if field.started then
        playMineCell(field, cell)
    elseif not field.noGuess then
        field.mines, field.started = generateMines(cell, field.count), true
        playMineCell(field, cell)
    else
        if mineSearch then mineSearch() end
        field.generating, field.searchFailed = true, false
        mineSearch = coroutine.wrap(function()
            local function checkpoint()
                coroutine.yield(true)
                return minefield == field
            end
            for _ = 1, 120 do
                if minefield ~= field then return end
                local candidate = generateMines(cell, field.count)
                if solvesWithoutGuessing(candidate, cell, field.count, checkpoint) then
                    field.mines, field.started, field.generating = candidate, true, false
                    playMineCell(field, cell)
                    return
                end
                if not checkpoint() then return end
            end
            field.generating, field.searchFailed = false, true
        end)
    end
end

PHONE.appHost.home=function() changeGameTab("menu") end

local function enterSelectedGame()
    local entry = menuOptions[selectedGame]
    if not entry then return end
    if entry.needsRoom and room == "" then PHONE.appHost.notice="Join a room to use this app."; return end
    PHONE.appHost.startOpen=false
    local tab = entry.tab
    if tab == "chatrooms" then changeGameTab(nil)
    else changeGameTab(tab) end
end

local function handleBattleship(key, enter)
    if key == 17 and battleshipCursor > 10 then battleshipCursor = battleshipCursor - 10
    elseif key == 18 and battleshipCursor <= 90 then battleshipCursor = battleshipCursor + 10
    elseif key == 19 and (battleshipCursor - 1) % 10 > 0 then battleshipCursor = battleshipCursor - 1
    elseif key == 20 and battleshipCursor % 10 > 0 then battleshipCursor = battleshipCursor + 1
    elseif currentGame.ready == false then
        if key == 82 or key == 114 then battleshipVertical = not battleshipVertical
        elseif key == 85 or key == 117 then sendGameAction(4, 301)
        elseif enter and currentGame.nextShip > 0 then
            sendGameAction(4, battleshipCursor + (battleshipVertical and 200 or 100))
        end
    elseif enter and currentGame.guest ~= "" and currentGame.winner == ""
        and currentGame.turn == currentGame.mark then sendGameAction(4, battleshipCursor) end
end

local function handleTrail(key, enter, character)
    if not currentGame.trail then
        if currentGame.isHost and character and character:lower() == "s" then sendGameAction(4, 10) end
        return
    end
    if currentGame.trail.result then
        if character and character:lower() == "r" then sendGameAction(6) end
        return
    end
    trailChoice = math.min(trailChoice, #currentGame.choices)
    if key == 17 then trailChoice = math.max(1, trailChoice - 1)
    elseif key == 18 then trailChoice = math.min(#currentGame.choices, trailChoice + 1)
    elseif enter and currentGame.canMove then sendGameAction(4, trailChoice) end
end

local function orientChessSquare(square, mark)
    return mark == "O" and 65 - square or square
end

local function moveChessCursor(key)
    local tile = orientChessSquare(chessCursor, currentGame.mark)
    if key == 17 and tile > 8 then tile = tile - 8
    elseif key == 18 and tile <= 56 then tile = tile + 8
    elseif key == 19 and (tile - 1) % 8 > 0 then tile = tile - 1
    elseif key == 20 and tile % 8 > 0 then tile = tile + 1 end
    chessCursor = orientChessSquare(tile, currentGame.mark)
end

local function submitChessMove(promotion)
    if sendGameAction(4, chessFrom * 256 + (chessCursor - 1) * 4 + promotion) then
        chessFrom, chessPromotion = nil, false
        chessHints = {}
    end
end

local function handleChess(key, enter, character)
    if chessPromotion then
        local promotion = character and ("qrbn"):find(character:lower(), 1, true)
        if promotion then submitChessMove(promotion - 1)
        elseif key == 8 or key == 127 then chessFrom, chessPromotion, chessHints = nil, false, {} end
        return
    end
    local moves = currentGame.moves or {}
    if key == 151 then
        chessMoveScroll = math.min(chessMoveScroll + 8, math.max(0, #moves - CHESS_MOVE_ROWS))
    elseif key == 152 then
        chessMoveScroll = math.max(0, chessMoveScroll - 8)
    end
    moveChessCursor(key)
    if not enter or currentGame.guest == "" or currentGame.turn ~= currentGame.mark
        or currentGame.winner ~= "" then return end
    local piece = currentGame.board:sub(chessCursor, chessCursor)
    local own = piece ~= "." and (piece == piece:upper()) == (currentGame.mark == "X")
    if own then
        chessFrom = chessFrom ~= chessCursor and chessCursor or nil
        chessHints = {}
        if chessFrom then sendGameAction(7, chessFrom) end
    elseif chessFrom then
        local moving = currentGame.board:sub(chessFrom, chessFrom):lower()
        if moving == "p" and (chessCursor <= 8 or chessCursor > 56) then chessPromotion = true
        else submitChessMove(0) end
    end
end

local function handleMines(key, enter)
    local field = minefield
    if not field then
        if key == 17 then mineDifficulty = math.max(1, mineDifficulty - 1)
        elseif key == 18 then mineDifficulty = math.min(#MINE_DIFFICULTIES, mineDifficulty + 1)
        elseif key == 32 then mineNoGuess = not mineNoGuess
        elseif enter then minefield = newMinefield() end
        return
    end
    local cell = field.cursor
    if key == 68 or key == 100 then
        minefield = nil
    elseif key == 82 or key == 114 then
        minefield = newMinefield()
    elseif field.generating then
        return
    elseif key == 17 and cell > MINE_SIDE then
        field.cursor = cell - MINE_SIDE
    elseif key == 18 and cell <= MINE_SIDE * (MINE_SIDE - 1) then
        field.cursor = cell + MINE_SIDE
    elseif key == 19 and (cell - 1) % MINE_SIDE > 0 then
        field.cursor = cell - 1
    elseif key == 20 and cell % MINE_SIDE > 0 then
        field.cursor = cell + 1
    elseif enter then
        revealMineCell()
    elseif (key == 70 or key == 102) and not field.result and not field.open[cell] then
        if field.flags[cell] then
            field.flags[cell] = nil
            field.flagCount = field.flagCount - 1
        elseif field.flagCount < field.count then
            field.flags[cell] = true
            field.flagCount = field.flagCount + 1
        end
    end
end

if isOwner then
    local nextWebsiteRefresh, lastWebsiteRequest = 0, nil
    local function refreshWebsite()
        if gameTab ~= "website" and not PHONE.tools.webVisible() then return end
        local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
        local id = page and page.id or 0
        local route = page and page.route or "home"
        local requestKey = id .. ":" .. route
        local now = timer.curtime()
        if (requestKey == lastWebsiteRequest and now < nextWebsiteRefresh) or net.getBytesLeft() < 32 then return end
        lastWebsiteRequest, nextWebsiteRefresh = requestKey, now + 2
        net.start("room_website_request")
        net.writeUInt(id, 16)
        net.writeString(route)
        net.send()
    end

    local nextWebsiteRelay = 0
    local function refreshWebsiteFrame()
        local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
        if (gameTab ~= "website" and not PHONE.tools.webVisible()) or not page or not page.title or page.demo or page.donation then
            websiteFrame, websiteFrameSite = nil, nil
            return
        end
        local processor = entity(page.id)
        if processor and processor:isValid() and processor:getClass() == "starfall_processor" then
            local ok, results = pcall(hook.runRemote, processor, "starfall_website_screen_v1", MAXWELLNET.capabilities, page.route or "home")
            local frame = ok and type(results) == "table" and type(results[1]) == "table" and results[1][1]
            if MAXWELLNET.validFrame(frame) then
                page.frameAt = timer.curtime()
                websiteFrame, websiteFrameSite = frame, page.id
                return
            end
        end
        local now = timer.curtime()
        if now < nextWebsiteRelay or net.getBytesLeft() < 32 then return end
        nextWebsiteRelay = now + 0.25
        net.start("room_website_frame_request")
        net.writeUInt(page.id, 16)
        net.send()
    end

    startWebsitePolling = function()
        nextWebsiteRefresh, lastWebsiteRequest, nextWebsiteRelay = 0, nil, 0
        timer.create("room_website_refresh", 0.5, 0, refreshWebsite)
        timer.create("room_website_screen_refresh", 0.05, 0, refreshWebsiteFrame)
        refreshWebsite()
    end

    net.receive("room_website_sound", function()
        local id, cue = net.readUInt(16), net.readUInt(3)
        local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
        if not poweredOn or (gameTab ~= "website" and not PHONE.tools.webVisible()) or not page or page.id ~= id then return end
        local sounds = {"buttons/button15.wav", "buttons/button9.wav", "buttons/button10.wav",
            "ambient/water/water_splash1.wav", "buttons/button17.wav"}
        if not sounds[cue] then return end
        pcall(function()
            local position = eyePos()
            local source = websiteControls.soundScreen
            if not PHONE.open then
                if not source or not source:isValid() then
                    for _, component in ipairs(chip():getLinkedComponents()) do
                        if component:getClass() == "starfall_screen" then source = component; break end
                    end
                end
                if source and source:isValid() then position = source:getPos() end
            end
            if sound and sound.emitSound then sound.emitSound(sounds[cue], position, 65, 100, 0.45) end
        end)
    end)

    net.receive("room_website_frame", function()
        local id, route = net.readUInt(16), net.readString()
        local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
        if not poweredOn or (gameTab ~= "website" and not PHONE.tools.webVisible()) or not page or page.id ~= id or (page.route or "home") ~= route then return end
        local frame = {}
        local count = net.readUInt(9)
        for i = 1, count do
            local command = {net.readBool() and "text" or "rect"}
            for j = 2, 5 do command[j] = net.readUInt(10) end
            for j = 6, 8 do command[j] = net.readUInt(8) end
            if command[1] == "text" then command[9] = net.readString() end
            frame[i] = command
        end
        if MAXWELLNET.validFrame(frame) then
            page.frameAt = timer.curtime()
            websiteFrame, websiteFrameSite = frame, id
        end
    end)

    net.receive("room_website_donate_result", function()
        local id, message = net.readUInt(16), net.readString()
        local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
        if (gameTab == "website" or PHONE.tools.webVisible()) and page and page.id == id then websiteControls.message = message end
    end)

    net.receive("room_website_error",function()
        local message=net.readString()
        if gameTab=="website" then websiteControls.message=message;websiteControls.discoveryError=true end
    end)

    net.receive("room_website_pages", function()
        if not poweredOn or (gameTab ~= "website" and not PHONE.tools.webVisible()) then return end
        local previous = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
        local count = net.readUInt(6)
        local pages, byId, selection = {}, {}, 1
        for i = 1, count do
            local page = {id = net.readUInt(16), name = net.readString()}
            if previous and page.id == previous.id then
                selection = i
                previous.name = page.name
                page = previous
            end
            pages[i], byId[page.id] = page, page
        end
        if net.readBool() then
            local id = net.readUInt(16)
            local original = byId[id]
            local page = {scroll = original and original.scroll or 0}
            page.title = net.readString()
            page.route = net.readString()
            local mode = net.readUInt(2)
            page.demo, page.donation = mode == 1, mode == 2
            page.face, page.size, page.weight = net.readString(), net.readUInt(7), net.readUInt(10)
            page.height = net.readUInt(10)
            page.scroll = math.min(page.scroll or 0, page.height - 512)
            page.buttons = {}
            local count = net.readUInt(7)
            for i = 1, count do
                local button = {}
                for j = 1, 4 do button[j] = net.readUInt(10) end
                button[5], button[6] = net.readString(), net.readString()
                page.buttons[i] = button
            end
            page.amounts, page.lines = {}, {}
            if page.demo then page.startedAt = timer.curtime() - net.readUInt(32) / 10 end
            if page.donation then
                for i = 1, 3 do page.amounts[i] = net.readUInt(31) end
            end
            local lineCount = net.readUInt(3)
            for i = 1, lineCount do page.lines[i] = net.readString() end
            page.extensions = net.readTable()
            if original and (original.route or "home") == page.route then
                for key, value in pairs(page) do original[key] = value end
                original.loadedAt = original.loadedAt or timer.curtime()
            end
        end
        for _, page in ipairs(pages) do page.loadedAt = page.loadedAt or timer.curtime() end
        websitePages, selectedWebsite, websiteLoaded = pages, selection, true
        if websiteControls.discoveryError or websiteControls.message=="Discovery timed out. Use Retry." then
            websiteControls.message=""
        end
        websiteControls.discoveryError=false
    end)

    net.receive("room_channel_list", function()
        local names = net.readString()
        channelWaiting, channelNames, channelIds = nil, {}, {}
        if not showChannels then return end
        for entry in names:gmatch("[^\n]+") do
            local colon = entry:find(":", 1, true)
            local id = colon and tonumber(entry:sub(1, colon - 1))
            local name = id and entry:sub(colon + 1) or entry
            channelNames[#channelNames + 1] = name
            channelIds[#channelNames] = id or #channelNames
            if name == channelName then selectedChannel = #channelNames end
        end
        selectedChannel = math.max(1, math.min(#channelNames, selectedChannel))
        channelStatus = #channelNames == 0 and "This server did not provide a channel list." or ""
    end)

    net.receive("room_channel_result", function()
        local result = net.readString()
        channelWaiting = nil
        if result == "ok" then
            if channelNames[selectedChannel] == channelName then showChannels = false
            else
                channelWaiting = timer.curtime() + 5
                channelStatus = "Switching channels..."
            end
        else channelStatus = result end
    end)

    net.receive("room_private_state", function()
        local snapshot = net.readTable()
        if room == "" then return end
        local messages = snapshot.messages or {}
        local old = PHONE.previousMessages
        if old and not showPrivate then
            local overlap = math.min(#old, #messages)
            while overlap > 0 do
                local same = true
                for i = 1, overlap do
                    if old[#old - overlap + i] ~= messages[i] then same = false; break end
                end
                if same then break end
                overlap = overlap - 1
            end
            for i = overlap + 1, #messages do
                if messages[i]:sub(1, 5) == "From " then
                    PHONE.tools.notify("Private message",messages[i])
                    PHONE.unread = math.min(PHONE.unread + 1, 99)
                    PHONE.alertUntil = timer.curtime() + 6
                end
            end
            if PHONE.alertUntil > timer.curtime() and not (PHONE.previousAlert == PHONE.alertUntil) and poweredOn then
                playChatSound("mention")
            end
            PHONE.previousAlert = PHONE.alertUntil
        end
        PHONE.previousMessages = messages
        if not showPrivate then return end
        PHONE.unread = 0
        local previous = privateRecipients[privateSelection]
        privateRecipients, privateSelection, privateRows = snapshot.recipients or {}, 0, {}
        for i, entry in ipairs(privateRecipients) do
            if previous and entry.id == previous.id then privateSelection = i end
        end
        for _, message in ipairs(snapshot.messages or {}) do
            for _, line in ipairs(wrapText(message, 460, emojiFont)) do privateRows[#privateRows + 1] = line end
        end
        privateScroll = math.min(privateScroll, math.max(0, #privateRows - 6))
        if snapshot.notice then privateStatus = snapshot.notice
        elseif privateStatus == "No private inbox reply. Retrying..." then privateStatus = "" end
    end)

    net.receive("room_private_result", function()
        local result = net.readString()
        if not privateWaiting then return end
        privateWaiting = nil
        if result == "ok" then privateDraft, privateScroll = "", 0 end
        privateStatus = result == "ok" and "Private message sent." or result
    end)

    net.receive("room_payment_state", function()
        local result = net.readTable()
        if not showPayments then return end
        if result.recipients then
            local selected = paymentRecipients[paymentSelection]
            paymentRecipients, paymentSelection = result.recipients, 1
            for i, entry in ipairs(paymentRecipients) do
                if selected and entry.id == selected.id then paymentSelection = i end
            end
        end
        if result.finished then paymentReview = nil end
        if result.quote then paymentReview = result.quote end
        paymentWaiting = nil
        paymentStatus = result.notice or ""
        if result.finished or result.notice then PHONE.tools.notify("Payment",paymentStatus) end
    end)

    function PHONE.computerClickAt(x, y, right)
        if x and y and PHONE.tools.taskbarClick(x,y,right) then return end
        if x and y and PHONE.tools.handles(gameTab) then
            if x>=369 and y>=8 and y<39 then changeGameTab("menu") else PHONE.tools.click(gameTab,x,y,right) end
            return
        end
        if gameTab=="menu" and x and y and themeConfigured and computerName~="" then
            PHONE.desktopClick(x,y,right)
            return
        end
        if type(gameTab)=="string" and gameTab:sub(1,4)=="mos:" and x and y then
            if x>=369 and y>=8 and y<39 then changeGameTab("menu")
            else PHONE.appHost.click(x,y,right) end
            return
        end
        if gameTab == "maker" and x and y then PHONE.maker.click(x,y); return end
        if gameTab == "files" and x and y then PHONE.files.click(x,y); return end
        if gameTab=="contracts" and x and y then
            if y>=120 and y<153 then PHONE.contracts.key(x<256 and 19 or 20,false)
            elseif y>=155 and y<185 then PHONE.contracts.key(112,false)
            elseif y>=230 and y<410 then
                PHONE.contracts.selected=math.max(1,PHONE.contracts.selected-4)+math.floor((y-230)/36)
            elseif y>=420 and y<450 then PHONE.contracts.key(13,true)
            elseif y>=451 then PHONE.contracts.key(114,false) end
            return
        end
        if not poweredOn or timer.curtime() < startupUntil or not x or not y or x < 16 or x >= 496 then return end
        if themeConfigured then
            if room ~= "" and not gameTab and not showPayments and not showPrivate
                and x >= 265 and x < 365 and y >= 8 and y < 39 then
                toggleChannels()
                return
            end
            if computerName ~= "" and not joining and not versionWarning and not showPrivate and not showChannels then
                if gameTab == "menu" and x >= 236 and x < 365 and y >= 8 and y < 39 then
                    changeGameTab(nil)
                    return
                elseif x >= 369 and y >= 8 and y < 39 then
                    playChatSound("game")
                    toggleLastApp()
                    return
                elseif gameTab == "calculator" then calculatorClick(x, y); return
                elseif gameTab == "settings" then
                    if y >= 72 and y < 98 then
                        settings.selectPage(x<176 and "color" or (x<336 and "name" or "wallpaper"))
                    elseif settings.page == "color" then
                        themes.selectAt(y, x)
                    elseif settings.page == "wallpaper" then
                        PHONE.wallpaper.click(x,y)
                    elseif y >= 226 and y < 268 then
                        settings.saveName()
                    end
                    return
                elseif gameTab == "paint" then PHONE.paint.click(x, y); return
                elseif PHONE.extras[gameTab] and (gameTab == "credits" or gameTab == "music") then
                    PHONE.extras.click(gameTab, x, y); return
                elseif gameTab == "changelog" then
                    if y >= 414 and y < 454 then
                        if x < 136 then changelogPage = math.min(#CHANGELOG, changelogPage + 1)
                        elseif x >= 376 then changelogPage = math.max(1, changelogPage - 1) end
                    end
                    return
                elseif gameTab == "website" then
                    if websiteControls.clickChrome(x,y) then return end
                    if y >= 84 and y < 118 then
                        if x >= 16 and x < 58 then stepWebsite(-1)
                        elseif x >= 454 and x < 496 then stepWebsite(1) end
                    elseif x >= 426 and y >= 132 and y < 202 then
                        websiteControls.scroll(y < 167 and -96 or 96)
                    else
                        local px, py = (x - 96) / 0.625, (y - 132) / 0.625
                        websiteControls.activateButton(px, py)
                        local slot = math.floor((py - 154) / 74)
                        if px >= 48 and px < 464 and slot >= 0 and slot < 3 and (py - 154) % 74 < 56 then
                            requestWebsiteDonation(slot + 1)
                        end
                    end
                    return
                elseif gameTab == "hud" then
                    if y >= 134 and y < 193 then showChatHUD = not showChatHUD end
                    if y >= 426 and y < 470 then changeGameTab("menu") end
                    return
                elseif gameTab == "menu" then
                    PHONE.desktopClick(x,y,right)
                    return
                elseif gameTab then return end
            end
            return
        end
        themes.selectAt(y, x)
    end

    function PHONE.screenClick(x, y, right)
        if not x or not y or x < 0 or x >= 512 or y < 0 or y >= 512
            or not poweredOn or timer.curtime() < startupUntil then return end
        PHONE.computerClickAt(x, y, right)
    end

    hook.add("StarfallUsed", "chatroom_theme", function(user, screen)
        if user ~= player() or not screen or not screen:isValid()
            or screen:getClass() ~= "starfall_screen" then return end
        local x, y = render.cursorPos(user, screen)
        if not x or not y or not poweredOn or timer.curtime() < startupUntil then return end
        websiteControls.soundScreen = screen
        PHONE.mouseScreen = screen
        if not PHONE.controlsLocked and not keyboardActive and not PHONE.open then
            PHONE.setControlsLocked(true, true)
        end
        PHONE.screenClick(x, y)
    end)

    function PHONE.screenMouseActive()
        return not PHONE.open and poweredOn and timer.curtime() >= startupUntil
            and (PHONE.controlsLocked or keyboardActive) and hasPermission("input")
            and PHONE.mouseScreen and PHONE.mouseScreen:isValid()
    end

    hook.add("inputpressed", "chatroom_screen_mouse", function(button)
        if button ~= MOUSE.LEFT and button ~= MOUSE.RIGHT then return end
        if not PHONE.screenMouseActive() then return end
        local user, screen = player(), PHONE.mouseScreen
        if user:getEyeTrace().Entity ~= screen then return end
        local x,y=render.cursorPos(user,screen)
        PHONE.screenClick(x,y,button==MOUSE.RIGHT)
    end)

    hook.add("think","maxwellos_paint_drag",function()
        local paint=PHONE.paint
        if not isOwner or gameTab~="paint" or not poweredOn or timer.curtime()<startupUntil
            or not hasPermission("input") then paint.drag(nil,nil,false);return end
        local held=input.isMouseDown(MOUSE.LEFT)
        if not held then paint.drag(nil,nil,false);return end
        local x,y
        if PHONE.open and hasPermission("input.enablecursor") then
            local mx,my=input.getCursorPos()
            local px,py,scale,face=PHONE.geometry()
            x,y=(mx-px-14)/scale,(my-py-46)/scale
            if x<0 or x>=512 or y<0 or y>=512 then x,y=nil,nil end
        elseif PHONE.screenMouseActive() then
            local user,screen=player(),PHONE.mouseScreen
            if user:getEyeTrace().Entity==screen then x,y=render.cursorPos(user,screen) end
        end
        paint.drag(x,y,held and x~=nil)
    end)

    -- Continuous website coordinates use the same viewport as click controls.
    local nextPointer, pointerWasHeld = 0, false
    hook.add("think", "room_website_pointer", function()
        if not isOwner or not poweredOn or gameTab ~= "website" then return end
        if timer.curtime() < nextPointer then return end
        local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
        if not page or not page.title or page.demo or page.donation or websiteControls.showBookmarks
            or websiteControls.focus then return end
        local user = player()
        local screen = PHONE.mouseScreen
        if not screen or not screen:isValid() or user:getEyeTrace().Entity ~= screen then return end
        local x, y = render.cursorPos(user, screen)
        if not x or not y then return end
        local px, py = (x - 96) / 0.625, (y - 132) / 0.625
        local held = user:keyDown(IN_KEY.USE) or user:keyDown(IN_KEY.ATTACK)
        local right = user:keyDown(IN_KEY.ATTACK2)
        if hasPermission("input") then
            held = held or input.isKeyDown(KEY.E) or input.isMouseDown(MOUSE.LEFT)
            right = right or input.isMouseDown(MOUSE.RIGHT)
        end
        if px < 0 or px >= 512 or py < 0 or py >= 512 then held, right = false, false end
        if not held and not right and not pointerWasHeld then return end
        if net.getBytesLeft() < 48 then return end
        pointerWasHeld = held or right
        nextPointer = timer.curtime() + 0.1
        net.start("room_website_pointer")
        net.writeUInt(page.id, 16)
        net.writeUInt(math.max(0, math.min(511, math.floor(px))), 10)
        net.writeUInt(math.max(0, math.min(1023, math.floor(py + (page.scroll or 0)))), 10)
        net.writeBool(held)
        net.writeBool(right)
        net.send()
    end)

    net.receive("room_games_state", function()
        local snapshot = net.readTable()
        if snapshot.variant ~= gameWatchTab then return end
        if snapshot.current or snapshot.notice ~= "" then gameEntryUntil = nil end
        if snapshot.notice ~= "" then PHONE.tools.notify("Game: "..tostring(snapshot.variant),snapshot.notice);gameStatus = snapshot.notice
        elseif snapshot.current or snapshot.clearNotice then gameStatus = "" end
        if not snapshot.changed then return end
        local incoming = snapshot.current
        if poweredOn and gameTab == gameWatchTab and currentGame and incoming and currentGame.id == incoming.id then
            if gameTab == "chess" and incoming.board ~= currentGame.board and (incoming.ply or 0) > (currentGame.ply or 0) then
                playChatSound("chess")
            elseif gameTab == "tic" and incoming.board ~= currentGame.board and incoming.board ~= "........." then
                playChatSound("tic")
            elseif gameTab == "battleship" and incoming.lastShot ~= ""
                and (incoming.target ~= currentGame.target or incoming.fleet ~= currentGame.fleet) then
                local hit = incoming.lastShotHit
                if hit == nil then hit = incoming.lastShot:find(" hit ", 1, true) ~= nil end
                playChatSound(hit and "explosion" or "splash")
            end
        end
        local selection = matches[selectedMatch]
        if currentGame and not snapshot.current and snapshot.notice == "" then gameStatus = "Match closed." end
        if gameWatchTab == "chess" then
            if not currentGame or not incoming or incoming.id ~= currentGame.id
                or (incoming.ply or 0) < (currentGame.ply or 0) then
                chessMoveScroll = 0
                chessCursor = incoming and incoming.mark == "O" and 5 or 61
            end
            if not currentGame or not incoming or incoming.id ~= currentGame.id or incoming.board ~= currentGame.board then
                chessFrom, chessPromotion, chessHints = nil, false, {}
            end
        end
        matches, currentGame = snapshot.matches, snapshot.current
        selectedMatch = 1
        for i, match in ipairs(matches) do
            if selection and selection.id == match.id then selectedMatch = i end
        end
    end)

    net.receive("room_chess_hints", function()
        local result = net.readTable()
        if gameTab ~= "chess" or not currentGame or result.id ~= currentGame.id
            or result.board ~= currentGame.board or result.from ~= chessFrom then return end
        chessHints = {}
        for _, square in ipairs(result.hints) do chessHints[square] = true end
    end)

    net.receive("room_directory", function()
        directoryReceived = true
        directoryDetail = net.readString()
        local selectedId = directory[selected] and directory[selected].id
        directory = {}
        selected = 1
        for i = 1, net.readUInt(8) do
            local id, name = net.readUInt(16), net.readString()
            local passwordRequired, version = net.readBool(), net.readString()
            directory[i] = {id = id, name = name, passwordRequired = passwordRequired, version = version}
            if id == selectedId then selected = i end
        end
        if versionWarning then
            local entry = directory[selected]
            if not entry or entry.id ~= versionWarning.id or entry.version ~= versionWarning.version then
                versionWarning = nil
            end
        end
    end)

    net.receive("room_rename_result", function()
        local result, name = net.readString(), net.readString()
        settings.waiting = nil
        if result == "ok" then
            computerName, settings.name, settings.message = name, name, "Name saved."
            settings.saveProfile()
        else
            settings.message = result
        end
    end)

    net.receive("room_join_result", function()
        local result = net.readString()
        if not joinUntil then return end
        joinUntil = nil
        if result == "ok" then
            joining, status = nil, ""
        else
            if not keyboardActive then joining = nil end
            status = result
        end
    end)

    net.receive("room_painting_result", function()
        local message, pixels, channel = net.readString(), net.readString(), net.readString()
        if room == "" or channel ~= channelName then return end
        if pixels == "" then
            if gameTab == "paint" then PHONE.paint.message = message else status = message end
            return
        end
        local _, runs = PHONE.paint.decode(pixels)
        if runs and not gameTab and not showChannels and not showPrivate then
            PHONE.paint.preview = {author = message, runs = runs}
        end
    end)

    net.receive("room_history", function()
        local newRoom = net.readString()
        roomVersion = net.readString()
        if newRoom ~= "" then directoryReceived = true end
        if newRoom ~= room then
            PHONE.paint.preview = nil
            scroll, draft, status, waitingUntil = 0, "", "", nil
            joining, password, joinUntil, versionWarning = nil, "", nil, nil
            lastSequence, activeAlert = nil, nil
            showPayments, paymentReview, paymentWaiting = false, nil, nil
            showPrivate, privateWaiting, privateDraft, privateStatus = false, nil, "", ""
            showChannels, channelWaiting, channelNames, selectedChannel = false, nil, {}, 1
            channelIds = {}
            privateRecipients, privateRows, privateSelection, privateScroll = {}, {}, 0, 0
            paymentRecipients, paymentSelection, paymentAmount, paymentStatus = {}, 1, "", ""
            if MULTIPLAYER_TABS[gameTab] or gameTab == "payments" or gameTab == "contracts" then gameTab = nil end
            matches, currentGame, selectedMatch, gameStatus = {}, nil, 1, ""
            gameWatchTab, gameEntryUntil = nil, nil
        end
        if room ~= newRoom then
            PHONE.previousMessages, PHONE.unread, PHONE.alertUntil = nil, 0, 0
            PHONE.watchInbox = newRoom ~= ""
        end
        room = newRoom
        rows = {}
        local count = net.readUInt(5)
        local alerts = {}
        local mentions = {}
        local notificationMessages={}
        for i = 1, count do
            local message = net.readString()
            notificationMessages[i]=message
            local isAlert = net.readBool()
            local mentioned = not isAlert and messageMentions(message, computerName)
            if isAlert then alerts[i] = message end
            if mentioned then mentions[i] = message end
            local prefix = message:sub(1, -66)
            local thumbnail = message:sub(-64)
            if #thumbnail == 64 and prefix:find(": [Painting] F7 opens latest", 1, true)
                and message:sub(-65, -65) == "|" and not thumbnail:find("[^0-8A-Z]") then
                rows[#rows + 1] = {text = prefix, face = emojiFont, thumbnail = thumbnail}
            else
                for _, line in ipairs(wrapText(message, 468, emojiFont)) do
                    rows[#rows + 1] = {text = line, alert = isAlert, mention = mentioned, face = emojiFont}
                end
            end
        end
        local notice = net.readString()
        if notice ~= "" then status = notice end
        local newSequence = net.readUInt(32)
        local newChannel = net.readString()
        PHONE.typingNames = net.readString()
        if newChannel ~= channelName then
            PHONE.paint.preview = nil
            channelName = newChannel
            lastSequence, activeAlert, scroll, draft = nil, nil, 0, ""
            showChannels, channelWaiting, status = false, nil, ""
        end
        if lastSequence and newSequence > lastSequence then
            local firstNew = math.max(1, count - (newSequence - lastSequence) + 1)
            for i=firstNew,count do
                local message=notificationMessages[i]
                if message:find(" lobby (#",1,true) then PHONE.tools.notify("Game invitation",message)
                elseif not alerts[i] and not mentions[i] and (gameTab or (not PHONE.open and not PHONE.controlsLocked)) then
                    PHONE.tools.notify("Chat: "..newChannel,message)
                end
            end
            for i = count, firstNew, -1 do
                if alerts[i] then
                    PHONE.tools.notify("Room alert",alerts[i])
                    activeAlert = wrapText(alerts[i]:sub(9), 340, smallFont)[1]
                    alertUntil = timer.curtime() + 6
                    break
                end
            end
            for i = count, firstNew, -1 do
                if mentions[i] then
                    PHONE.tools.notify("Mention",mentions[i])
                    activeAlert = "Mention from " .. mentions[i]:sub(1, mentions[i]:find(": ", 1, true) - 1)
                    alertUntil = timer.curtime() + 6
                    playChatSound("mention")
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        lastSequence = newSequence
        scroll = math.min(scroll, math.max(0, #rows - ROWS_VISIBLE))
    end)

    timer.create("chatroom_directory_request", 1, 0, function()
        if directoryReceived then
            timer.remove("chatroom_directory_request")
            return
        end
        if net.getBytesLeft() < 40 then return end
        net.start("room_directory_request")
        net.send()
    end)

    net.receive("room_keyboard", function()
        keyboardActive = net.readBool()
        local powered = net.readBool()
        if powered ~= poweredOn then
            if not powered then PHONE.extras.stop() end
            poweredOn = powered
            if not powered then PHONE.setOpen(false) end
            startupUntil = powered and timer.curtime() + themes.startupDuration or 0
            PHONE.syncSoloGame()
            if gameTab == "website" then
                if poweredOn then startWebsitePolling()
                else
                    timer.remove("room_website_refresh")
                    timer.remove("room_website_screen_refresh")
                    websiteFrame, websiteFrameSite = nil, nil
                end
            elseif poweredOn and MULTIPLAYER_TABS[gameTab] then
                sendGameAction(1, MULTIPLAYER_TABS[gameTab])
            end
        end
        if keyboardActive then return end
        versionWarning = nil
        password = ""
        if not joinUntil then joining = nil end
    end)

    net.receive("room_result", function()
        if not waitingUntil then return end
        waitingUntil = nil
        local result = net.readString()
        if result == "ok" then
            draft, status, scroll = "", "", 0
        else
            status = result
        end
    end)

    hook.add("think", "chatroom_focus", function()
        if channelWaiting and timer.curtime() >= channelWaiting then
            channelWaiting, channelStatus = nil, "No reply. Press R to refresh."
        end
        if privateWaiting and timer.curtime() >= privateWaiting then
            privateWaiting, privateStatus = nil, "Delivery not confirmed."
        end
        if paymentWaiting and timer.curtime() >= paymentWaiting then
            local confirming = paymentReview ~= nil
            paymentWaiting, paymentReview = nil, nil
            paymentStatus = confirming and "No response. Check for an in-game payment prompt."
                or "Payment review timed out. Try again."
        end
        if PHONE.watchInbox and sendPrivateAction(1) then PHONE.watchInbox = false end
        if settings.waiting and timer.curtime() >= settings.waiting then
            settings.waiting, settings.message = nil, "No name change confirmation. Try again."
        end
        if waitingUntil and timer.curtime() >= waitingUntil then
            waitingUntil = nil
            status = "Delivery was not confirmed."
        end
        if joinUntil and timer.curtime() >= joinUntil then
            joinUntil = nil
            if not keyboardActive then joining = nil end
            status = "Join request timed out."
        end
    end)
    local function submitJoin(entry, value)
        if net.getBytesLeft() < #value + #computerName + 32 then
            status = "Connection busy. Try again shortly."
            return
        end
        joinUntil = timer.curtime() + 5
        status = entry.passwordRequired and "Checking password..." or "Joining..."
        net.start("room_join")
        net.writeUInt(entry.id, 16)
        net.writeString(value)
        net.writeString(computerName)
        password = ""
        net.send()
    end

    local function joinSelected(entry)
        if entry.passwordRequired then
            joining, password, status = entry, "", ""
        else
            submitJoin(entry, "")
        end
    end

    local function handleGameKey(key, enter, character)
        if PHONE.tools.handles(gameTab) then PHONE.tools.key(gameTab,key,enter,character);return end
        if gameTab=="menu" and PHONE.tools.desktopKey(key,enter,character) then return end
        if type(gameTab)=="string" and gameTab:sub(1,4)=="mos:" then
            PHONE.appHost.key(key,enter,character)
            return
        end
        if gameTab == "maker" then PHONE.maker.key(key,enter,character); return end
        if gameTab == "files" then PHONE.files.key(key,enter,character); return end
        if gameTab == "calculator" then calculatorKey(key, enter); return end
        if gameTab == "settings" then
            if key == 19 or key == 20 then
                local pages={"color","name","wallpaper"}
                local current=settings.page=="color" and 1 or (settings.page=="name" and 2 or 3)
                settings.selectPage(pages[(current-1+(key==19 and -1 or 1))%3+1])
            elseif settings.page == "wallpaper" then
                PHONE.wallpaper.key(key,enter)
            elseif settings.page == "color" then
                if key == 17 then themes.move(-1)
                elseif key == 18 then themes.move(1)
                elseif key == 151 then themes.move(-9)
                elseif key == 152 then themes.move(9)
                elseif enter then applyTheme() end
            elseif not settings.waiting then
                if enter then settings.saveName()
                elseif key == 8 or key == 127 then settings.name = settings.name:sub(1, -2)
                elseif character and #settings.name < 24 and character:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_-]$") then
                    settings.name = settings.name .. character
                end
            end
            return
        end
        if gameTab == "paint" then PHONE.paint.key(key, enter); return end
        if gameTab == "credits" or gameTab == "music" then PHONE.extras.key(gameTab, key, enter); return end
        if gameTab == "changelog" then
            if key == 19 or key == 151 then changelogPage = math.min(#CHANGELOG, changelogPage + 1)
            elseif key == 20 or key == 152 then changelogPage = math.max(1, changelogPage - 1)
            elseif key == 8 or key == 127 then changeGameTab("menu") end
            return
        end
        if gameTab == "website" then
            if websiteControls.key(key, enter, character) then return end
            local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
            local scrollable = page and page.height and page.height > 512
            if key == 19 or (key == 17 and not scrollable) then stepWebsite(-1)
            elseif key == 20 or (key == 18 and not scrollable) then stepWebsite(1)
            elseif key == 17 then websiteControls.scroll(-64)
            elseif key == 18 then websiteControls.scroll(64)
            elseif key == 151 then websiteControls.scroll(-320)
            elseif key == 152 then websiteControls.scroll(320)
            elseif enter then websiteControls.activateButton()
            elseif key >= 49 and key <= 51 then requestWebsiteDonation(key - 48)
            elseif key == 8 or key == 127 then changeGameTab("menu") end
            return
        end
        if gameTab == "hud" then
            if enter then showChatHUD = not showChatHUD
            elseif key == 8 or key == 127 then changeGameTab("menu") end
            return
        end
        if gameTab == "contracts" then PHONE.contracts.key(key,enter); return end
        if gameTab == "payments" then
            if key == 168 then changeGameTab("menu")
            else handlePayment(key, enter) end
            return
        end
        if gameTab == "menu" then
            if key==114 or key==82 then PHONE.appHost.scan(true)
            elseif key==9 then PHONE.appHost.startOpen=not PHONE.appHost.startOpen
            elseif key==151 or key==152 then
                local step=PHONE.desktopColumns*PHONE.desktopRows
                selectedGame=math.max(1,math.min(#menuOptions,selectedGame+(key==151 and -step or step)))
            elseif key==19 then selectedGame=math.max(1,selectedGame-PHONE.desktopRows)
            elseif key==20 then selectedGame=math.min(#menuOptions,selectedGame+PHONE.desktopRows)
            elseif key==17 then selectedGame=math.max(1,selectedGame-1)
            elseif key==18 then selectedGame=math.min(#menuOptions,selectedGame+1)
            elseif enter then enterSelectedGame() end
            PHONE.desktopScroll(0,true)
            return
        end
        if gameTab == "squadron" then PHONE.squadron.key(key, enter); return end
        if gameTab == "mines" then
            if key == 8 or key == 127 then changeGameTab("menu")
            else handleMines(key, enter) end
            return
        end
        if MULTIPLAYER_TABS[gameTab] then
            if currentGame then
                if (key == 8 or key == 127) and not chessPromotion then
                    sendGameAction(5)
                elseif character and character:lower() == "r" and currentGame.winner ~= "" then
                    sendGameAction(6)
                elseif gameTab == "chess" then
                    handleChess(key, enter, character)
                elseif gameTab == "battleship" then
                    handleBattleship(key, enter)
                elseif gameTab == "pong" then
                    if key == 17 or key == 18 then
                        PHONE.pongKeyboardUntil = timer.curtime() + 0.25
                        sendGameAction(4, key == 17 and 1 or 2)
                    end
                elseif gameTab == "trail" then
                    handleTrail(key, enter, character)
                elseif key >= 49 and key <= 57 then
                    sendGameAction(4, key - 48)
                end
            elseif key == 8 or key == 127 then
                changeGameTab("menu")
            elseif key == 17 then
                selectedMatch = math.max(1, selectedMatch - 1)
            elseif key == 18 then
                selectedMatch = math.max(1, math.min(#matches, selectedMatch + 1))
            elseif character and character:lower() == "c" then
                if sendGameAction(2) then gameEntryUntil = timer.curtime() + 5 end
            elseif enter and matches[selectedMatch] then
                if sendGameAction(3, matches[selectedMatch].id) then gameEntryUntil = timer.curtime() + 5 end
            end
            return
        end
    end

    local function handleKey(key)
        local enter = key == 10 or key == 13 or key == 142
        if not themeConfigured then
            if key == 17 then
                themes.move(-1)
            elseif key == 18 then
                themes.move(1)
            elseif key == 151 or key == 152 then
                themes.move(key == 151 and -9 or 9)
            elseif enter then
                applyTheme()
            end
            return
        end
        local character
        if key >= 32 and key <= 126 then character = string.char(key) end
        if computerName == "" then
            if enter then
                if nameDraft ~= "" then
                    computerName, status = nameDraft, ""
                    settings.saveProfile()
                else
                    status = "Enter a computer name first."
                end
            elseif key == 127 or key == 8 then
                nameDraft = nameDraft:sub(1, -2)
            elseif character and character:match("^[A-Za-z0-9_-]$") and #nameDraft < 24 then
                nameDraft = nameDraft .. character
            end
            return
        end
        if key == 165 then
            if gameTab and not joinUntil then changeGameTab(nil) end
            return
        end
        if key == 158 or key == 159 then
            toggleLastApp()
            return
        end
        if key == 168 and room ~= "" and not gameTab then
            if net.getBytesLeft() >= 32 then
                net.start("room_leave")
                net.send()
            end
            return
        end
        if not joining and not versionWarning and not showPrivate and not showChannels then
            if gameTab then handleGameKey(key, enter, character); return end
        end
        if room == "" then
            if joinUntil then return end
            if versionWarning then
                if key == 164 then
                    versionWarning = nil
                elseif enter then
                    local entry = directory[selected]
                    versionWarning = nil
                    joinSelected(entry)
                end
            elseif joining then
                if key == 164 then
                    joining, password, status = nil, "", ""
                elseif enter and password ~= "" then
                    submitJoin(joining, password)
                elseif key == 127 or key == 8 then
                    password = password:sub(1, -2)
                else
                    if character and #password < 64 then password = password .. character end
                end
            elseif key == 17 then
                selected = math.max(1, selected - 1)
                status = ""
            elseif key == 18 then
                selected = math.max(1, math.min(#directory, selected + 1))
                status = ""
            elseif enter and directory[selected] then
                local entry = directory[selected]
                local _, mismatch = versionStatus(entry.version)
                if mismatch then
                    versionWarning, status = entry, ""
                else
                    joinSelected(entry)
                end
            end
            return
        end
        if not gameTab and PHONE.paint.preview then
            if key == 8 or key == 127 or key == 169 then PHONE.paint.preview = nil end
            return
        end
        if key == 169 and not gameTab and not showChannels and not showPrivate then
            PHONE.paint.action("view")
            return
        end
        if key == 164 and not gameTab and not showPayments and not showPrivate then
            toggleChannels()
            return
        end
        if showChannels then
            if channelWaiting then return end
            if key == 17 then selectedChannel = math.max(1, selectedChannel - 1)
            elseif key == 18 then selectedChannel = math.max(1, math.min(#channelNames, selectedChannel + 1))
            elseif key == 82 or key == 114 then sendChannelAction(0)
            elseif enter and channelIds[selectedChannel] then sendChannelAction(channelIds[selectedChannel]) end
            return
        end
        if key == 167 and not gameTab and not showPayments and not privateWaiting then
            local opening = not showPrivate
            if not opening or sendPrivateAction(1) then showPrivate = opening end
            return
        end
        if showPrivate then
            handlePrivate(key, enter, character)
            return
        end
        if key == 151 then
            scroll = math.min(scroll + 7, math.max(0, #rows - ROWS_VISIBLE))
        elseif key == 152 then
            scroll = math.max(0, scroll - 7)
        elseif waitingUntil then
            return
        elseif enter then
            if draft:find("%S") then
                if net.getBytesLeft() < #draft + 32 then
                    status = "Connection busy. Try again shortly."
                    return
                end
                waitingUntil = timer.curtime() + 5
                status = "Sending..."
                net.start("room_post")
                net.writeString(draft)
                net.send()
            end
        elseif key == 127 or key == 8 then
            local previous = draft
            draft = draft:sub(1, -2)
            if previous ~= draft then PHONE.reportTyping(draft ~= "") end
        else
            if character and #draft < 160 then
                draft = draft .. character
                PHONE.reportTyping(true)
            end
        end
    end

    local nextArrowSound = 0
    net.receive("room_keys", function()
        for i = 1, net.readUInt(9) do
            local key = net.readUInt(8)
            if keyboardActive and poweredOn and not PHONE.controlsLocked and timer.curtime() >= startupUntil then
                local enter = key == 10 or key == 13 or key == 142
                local quiet = gameTab == "pong" or gameTab == "squadron"
                local cue = enter and "enter" or (gameTab and "game" or "key")
                handleKey(key)
                if not quiet then
                    if key < 17 or key > 20 then
                        playChatSound(cue)
                    else
                        local now = timer.curtime()
                        if now >= nextArrowSound then
                            nextArrowSound = now + 0.05
                            playChatSound(cue)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    setupPermissionRequest({"enablehud", "render.hud", "input", "input.enablecursor", "input.lockcontrols", "http.get", "sound.create", "file.read", "file.write", "file.exists", "file.find", "file.findInGame", "material.imagecreate", "hologram.create", "material.create", "render.offscreen"},
        "Computer keyboard, local apps, files, and wallpaper access", true)

    function PHONE.activateHud()
        if hasPermission("enablehud") and hasPermission("render.hud") then
            pcall(enableHud, player(), true)
        end
    end
    function PHONE.setControlsLocked(enabled, screenFocus)
        if not enabled then
            -- Only undo a lock that this phone created, not another chip's lock.
            if PHONE.controlsLocked and hasPermission("input.lockcontrols") then
                pcall(input.lockControls, false)
            end
            PHONE.controlsLocked = false
            PHONE.lockNotice = ""
            return
        end
        if (not PHONE.open and not screenFocus) or not poweredOn then return end
        if not hasPermission("input.lockcontrols") then
            PHONE.lockNotice = "Grant Lock Controls permission"
            PHONE.lockNoticeUntil = timer.curtime() + 4
            return
        end
        -- Starfall enforces a cooldown between locking attempts. Check before
        -- calling so a failed attempt does not throw an error in the chip.
        local canCheck, canLock = pcall(input.canLockControls)
        if not canCheck or not canLock then
            PHONE.lockNotice = "Relock cooldown (about 10 seconds)"
            PHONE.lockNoticeUntil = timer.curtime() + 3
            return
        end
        local ok = pcall(input.lockControls, true)
        if ok then
            PHONE.controlsLocked = true
            PHONE.lockNotice = ""
        else
            PHONE.lockNotice = "Unable to lock controls; try again"
            PHONE.lockNoticeUntil = timer.curtime() + 3
        end
    end
    function PHONE.setOpen(enabled)
        if not enabled then PHONE.setControlsLocked(false) end
        PHONE.open = enabled
        if enabled then PHONE.activateHud() end
        if hasPermission("input.enablecursor") then
            pcall(input.enableCursor, enabled)
        end
    end
    function PHONE.openInbox()
        if gameTab then changeGameTab(nil) end
        if gameTab then return end -- A payment review or live game may prevent switching.
        if room ~= "" then
            if not showPrivate and sendPrivateAction(1) then showPrivate = true end
            if showPrivate then PHONE.unread = 0 end
        end
    end
    hook.add("permissionrequest", "chatroom_phone_permissions", function()
        PHONE.activateHud()
        if PHONE.open and hasPermission("input.enablecursor") then
            pcall(input.enableCursor, true)
        end
    end)
    PHONE.keys = {
        [KEY.ENTER] = 13, [KEY.PAD_ENTER] = 13,
        [KEY.BACKSPACE] = 8, [KEY.SPACE] = 32,
        [KEY.UP] = 17, [KEY.DOWN] = 18, [KEY.LEFT] = 19, [KEY.RIGHT] = 20,
        [KEY.PAGEUP] = 151, [KEY.PAGEDOWN] = 152,
        [KEY.TAB] = 9, [KEY.ESCAPE] = 27, [KEY.F1] = 163, [KEY.F2] = 164, [KEY.F3] = 165,
        [KEY.F5] = 167, [KEY.F6] = 168, [KEY.F7] = 169,
        [KEY.SLASH] = 47, [KEY.PERIOD] = 46, [KEY.COMMA] = 44,
        [KEY.MINUS] = 45, [KEY.EQUAL] = 61,
        [KEY.SEMICOLON] = 59, [KEY.APOSTROPHE] = 39,
        [KEY.LBRACKET] = 91, [KEY.RBRACKET] = 93,
        [KEY.BACKSLASH] = 92, [KEY.BACKQUOTE] = 96,
        [KEY.PAD_PLUS] = 43, [KEY.PAD_MINUS] = 45,
        [KEY.PAD_MULTIPLY] = 42, [KEY.PAD_DIVIDE] = 47,
        [KEY.PAD_DECIMAL] = 46
    }
    if KEY.LCONTROL then PHONE.keys[KEY.LCONTROL]=158 end
    if KEY.RCONTROL then PHONE.keys[KEY.RCONTROL]=159 end
    for number = 0, 9 do
        PHONE.keys[KEY[tostring(number)]] = 48 + number
        PHONE.keys[KEY["PAD_" .. number]] = 48 + number
    end
    for i = 0, 25 do PHONE.keys[KEY.A + i] = 97 + i end
    PHONE.shiftKeys = {
        [48] = 41, [49] = 33, [50] = 64, [51] = 35,
        [52] = 36, [53] = 37, [54] = 94, [55] = 38,
        [56] = 42, [57] = 40, [61] = 43, [45] = 95,
        [47] = 63, [46] = 62, [44] = 60, [59] = 58,
        [39] = 34, [91] = 123, [93] = 125,
        [92] = 124, [96] = 126
    }

    PHONE.tools.dispatchKey=function(win,key)
