        local previous=gameTab;gameTab=win.tab or nil
        local ok,err=pcall(handleKey,key)
        if gameTab=="menu" then win.closeRequested=true elseif gameTab~=win.tab and gameTab~="windows" then win.tab=gameTab or false end
        gameTab=previous;if not ok then error(err) end
    end

    function PHONE.typeKey(button)
        local key = PHONE.keys[button]
        if not key then return end
        if input.isShiftDown() then
            if key >= 97 and key <= 122 then key = key - 32 end
            key = PHONE.shiftKeys[key] or key
        end
        handleKey(key)
    end

    hook.add("inputpressed", "chatroom_phone_hotkey", function(button)
        if not hasPermission("input") then return end
        -- When typing is locked, Shift+P is an uppercase P, not the phone hotkey.
        -- Unlock with Alt or the on-screen button before toggling the phone.
        if button == KEY.P and input.isShiftDown() and not PHONE.controlsLocked then
            PHONE.setOpen(not PHONE.open)
            return
        end
        if (not PHONE.open and not PHONE.controlsLocked) or not poweredOn or timer.curtime() < startupUntil then return end
        if button == KEY.ESCAPE then
            if gameTab == "website" and (websiteControls.focus or websiteControls.showBookmarks) then
                websiteControls.focus, websiteControls.showBookmarks = nil, false
                return
            end
            PHONE.setOpen(false)
            return
        end
        if button == KEY.LALT or button == KEY.RALT then
            if PHONE.controlsLocked then PHONE.setControlsLocked(false) end
            return
        end
        if PHONE.open and button == KEY.F5 and room ~= "" then
            PHONE.openInbox()
            return
        end
        if button==KEY.LCONTROL or button==KEY.RCONTROL then
            -- A connected Wire keyboard supplies its own Ctrl event when unlocked.
            if PHONE.controlsLocked or not keyboardActive then handleKey(158) end
            return
        end
        -- Only consume physical keyboard typing while game controls are locked.
        -- Otherwise the phone remains clickable while you play normally.
        if not PHONE.controlsLocked then return end
        local key = PHONE.keys[button]
        if key then
            PHONE.typeKey(button)
            PHONE.repeatButtons=PHONE.repeatButtons or {}
            local arcade = (gameTab == "squadron" and (key == 19 or key == 20))
                or (gameTab == "pong" and (key == 17 or key == 18))
            if repeatsKey(key) then PHONE.repeatButtons[button]=timer.curtime()+(arcade and 0.07 or 0.38) end
        end
    end)

    hook.add("inputreleased","maxwellos_key_rollover",function(button)
        if PHONE.repeatButtons then PHONE.repeatButtons[button]=nil end
    end)

    PHONE.mousePreviouslyDown = false
    hook.add("think", "chatroom_phone_mouse", function()
        -- Starfall always allows Alt to release its control lock. Reflect that
        -- emergency unlock in the button state before accepting more typing.
        if PHONE.controlsLocked then
            local ok, actual = pcall(input.isControlLocked)
            if ok and not actual then PHONE.controlsLocked = false end
        end
        PHONE.repeatButtons=PHONE.repeatButtons or {}
        for button,due in pairs(PHONE.repeatButtons) do
            if not PHONE.controlsLocked or not poweredOn or not hasPermission("input") or not input.isKeyDown(button) then
                PHONE.repeatButtons[button]=nil
            elseif timer.curtime()>=startupUntil and timer.curtime()>=due then
                PHONE.repeatButtons[button]=timer.curtime()+0.07
                PHONE.typeKey(button)
            end
        end
        if not PHONE.open or not hasPermission("input") or not hasPermission("input.enablecursor") then
            PHONE.mousePreviouslyDown = false
            return
        end
        local down = input.isMouseDown(MOUSE.LEFT)
        local rightDown=input.isMouseDown(MOUSE.RIGHT)
        if (down and not PHONE.mousePreviouslyDown) or (rightDown and not PHONE.mousePreviouslyRight) then
            local mx, my = input.getCursorPos()
            local x, y, scale, face, fw = PHONE.geometry()
            if mx >= x + 14 and mx < x + fw - 14
                and my >= y + face + 82 and my < y + face + 112 then
                PHONE.setControlsLocked(not PHONE.controlsLocked)
            elseif mx >= x + fw - 43 and mx < x + fw - 17 and my >= y + 11 and my < y + 37 then
                PHONE.setOpen(false)
            elseif mx >= x + fw - 120 and mx < x + fw - 13
                and my >= y + face + 46 and my < y + face + 81 then
                PHONE.openInbox()
            elseif mx >= x + 14 and mx < x + 14 + face
                and my >= y + 46 and my < y + 46 + face then
                PHONE.computerClickAt((mx - x - 14) / scale, (my - y - 46) / scale,rightDown)
            end
        end
        PHONE.mousePreviouslyDown = down
        PHONE.mousePreviouslyRight=rightDown
    end)
end

local function renderThemeSetup()
    if not themeConfigured then label(22, 10, "Computer setup", ink, titleFont) end
    label(22, 47, "Choose a color preset", muted, smallFont)
    for i = settings.themeTop, math.min(#themes, settings.themeTop + 8) do
        local theme = themes[i]
        local y = THEME_TOP + (i - settings.themeTop) * THEME_STEP
        local active = i == settings.theme
        render.setColor(theme.panel)
        render.drawRect(16, y, 480, THEME_HEIGHT)
        render.setColor(theme.accent)
        render.drawRect(16, y, active and 8 or 3, THEME_HEIGHT)
        render.drawRect(438, y + 7, 34, 20)
        label(33, y + 6, theme.name, active and theme.accent or (theme.ink or themes.defaultInk))
        if active then label(315, y + 8, "Selected", theme.accent, smallFont) end
    end
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(16, 419, 480, 47)
    label(26, 433, "Use " .. themes[settings.theme].name, accent)
    label(399, 433, "<", accent)
    label(466, 433, ">", accent)
    label(22, 483, themeConfigured and "Left/Right tabs  |  Up/Down colors  |  Enter applies"
        or "Aim and press E, or use Up/Down and Enter.", muted, smallFont)
end



function PHONE.desktopIcon(entry,x,y,shortcut)
    local color=accent
    if entry.tab=="files" then
        render.setColor(Color(219,164,47))
        render.drawRect(x,y+7,32,22)
        render.drawRect(x+2,y+2,14,8)
        render.setColor(Color(255,211,90))
        render.drawRect(x,y+12,32,17)
    elseif entry.tab=="chatrooms" then
        render.setColor(Color(62,166,235))
        render.drawRect(x,y+3,32,23)
        render.drawRect(x+5,y+24,7,7)
        render.setColor(Color(230,246,255))
        for line=0,2 do render.drawRect(x+6,y+8+line*5,20-line*3,2) end
    elseif entry.tab=="music" then
        render.setColor(Color(171,123,234))
        render.drawRect(x+9,y+2,4,23)
        render.drawRect(x+24,y+2,4,23)
        render.drawRect(x+9,y+2,19,5)
        render.drawRect(x+3,y+23,10,7)
        render.drawRect(x+18,y+23,10,7)
    elseif entry.tab=="website" then
        render.setColor(Color(57,163,203))
        render.drawRect(x,y+2,32,28)
        render.setColor(Color(193,237,251))
        render.drawRectOutline(x+5,y+6,22,20,1)
        render.drawLine(x+16,y+6,x+16,y+26)
        render.drawLine(x+5,y+16,x+27,y+16)
    elseif MULTIPLAYER_TABS[entry.tab] or entry.tab=="squadron" or entry.tab=="mines" then
        render.setColor(Color(224,131,77))
        render.drawRect(x,y+8,32,19)
        render.setColor(Color(50,43,48))
        render.drawRect(x+6,y+13,3,9)
        render.drawRect(x+3,y+16,9,3)
        render.drawRect(x+22,y+13,4,4)
        render.drawRect(x+26,y+18,4,4)
    else
        render.setColor(color)
        render.drawRect(x+2,y+2,28,29)
        render.setColor(Color(238,246,251))
        render.drawRect(x+5,y+5,22,4)
        render.drawRect(x+6,y+13,8,13)
        render.drawRect(x+17,y+13,8,5)
        render.drawRect(x+17,y+21,8,5)
    end
    if shortcut~=false then
    render.setColor(Color(244,248,255))
    render.drawRect(x-1,y+24,10,9)
    render.setColor(Color(50,116,197))
    render.drawLine(x+1,y+30,x+6,y+26)
    render.drawLine(x+3,y+26,x+6,y+26)
    render.drawLine(x+6,y+26,x+6,y+29)
    end
end

function PHONE.desktopClick(x,y,right)
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    if not host.startOpen and PHONE.tools.desktopClick(x,y,right) then return end
    if y>=474 then
        if x>=424 and #menuOptions>PHONE.desktopColumns*PHONE.desktopRows then
            PHONE.desktopScroll(x<464 and -6 or 6,false);return
        end
        if x<80 then
            if #menuOptions>PHONE.desktopColumns*PHONE.desktopRows then
                PHONE.desktopScroll(x<40 and -PHONE.desktopColumns or PHONE.desktopColumns,false)
            end
        elseif x>=152 and x<194 then host.startOpen=not host.startOpen
        elseif x>=194 and x<236 then changeGameTab(nil)
        elseif x>=236 and x<278 then changeGameTab("files")
        elseif x>=278 and x<320 then
            if lastApp~=false then changeGameTab(lastApp) end
        elseif x>=320 and x<362 then host.scan(true)
        elseif x>=362 and x<404 then changeGameTab("settings") end
        return
    end
    if host.startOpen then
        if x>=100 and x<420 and y>=142 and y<450 then
            local first=math.max(1,selectedGame-10)
            local index=first+math.floor((y-142)/28)
            if menuOptions[index] then selectedGame=index; enterSelectedGame() end
        else host.startOpen=false end
        return
    end
    local column=math.floor((x-8)/82)
    local row=math.floor((y-LAUNCHER_TOP)/62)
    if column<0 or column>=PHONE.desktopColumns or row<0 or row>=PHONE.desktopRows then return end
    if (x-8)%82>=TILE_WIDTH or (y-LAUNCHER_TOP)%62>=TILE_HEIGHT then return end
    local index=((PHONE.desktopTop or 0)+column)*PHONE.desktopRows+row+1
    if menuOptions[index] then selectedGame=index; enterSelectedGame() end
end

local function renderGameMenu()
    local host=PHONE.appHost
    if not PHONE.wallpaper.drawImage(0,0,512,474) then
        for band=0,11 do
            render.setColor(Color(8+band,17+band,32+band*2))
            render.drawRect(0,band*40,512,40)
        end
        -- Soft geometric glow keeps the desktop readable behind small shortcuts.
        for step=0,7 do
            render.setColor(Color(24+step*2,48+step*2,80+step*3,55))
            render.drawRect(274+step*9,74+step*13,180-step*14,316-step*23)
        end
    end
    local first=(PHONE.desktopTop or 0)*PHONE.desktopRows
    for index=first+1,math.min(first+PHONE.desktopColumns*PHONE.desktopRows,#menuOptions) do
        local entry=menuOptions[index]
        local slot=index-first-1
        local x=8+math.floor(slot/PHONE.desktopRows)*82
        local y=LAUNCHER_TOP+(slot%PHONE.desktopRows)*62
        local locked=room=="" and (MULTIPLAYER_TABS[entry.tab] or entry.needsRoom)
        if index==selectedGame then
            render.setColor(Color(105,160,211,65))
            render.drawRect(x,y,TILE_WIDTH,TILE_HEIGHT)
            render.setColor(Color(165,211,249,150))
            render.drawRectOutline(x,y,TILE_WIDTH,TILE_HEIGHT,1)
        end
        PHONE.desktopIcon(entry,x+22,y+3)
        local lines=wrapText(entry.name,72,PHONE.desktopFont)
        for line=1,math.min(2,#lines) do
            local text=lines[line]
            render.setFont(PHONE.desktopFont)
            local width=render.getTextSize(text)
            local tx,ty=x+(TILE_WIDTH-width)/2,y+36+(line-1)*13
            label(tx+1,ty+1,text,Color(0,0,0,220),PHONE.desktopFont)
            label(tx,ty,text,locked and Color(169,182,195) or Color(245,248,255),PHONE.desktopFont)
        end
    end
    if updates.latest then label(268,451,"GitHub update v"..updates.latest,accent,PHONE.desktopFont) end
    if host.startOpen then
        render.setColor(Color(27,32,42,248))
        render.drawRect(100,100,320,368)
        label(118,116,"All programs",ink,smallFont)
        local firstItem=math.max(1,selectedGame-10)
        for index=firstItem,math.min(#menuOptions,firstItem+10) do
            local y=142+(index-firstItem)*28
            if index==selectedGame then
                render.setColor(Color(65,80,99))
                render.drawRect(108,y,304,27)
            end
            label(120,y+5,menuOptions[index].name,Color(239,245,250),smallFont)
        end
    end
    PHONE.tools.drawDesktop()
end

local function renderMines()
    local field = minefield
    if not field then
        label(22, 85, "Choose a difficulty", accent)
        for i, option in ipairs(MINE_DIFFICULTIES) do
            local y = 123 + (i - 1) * 57
            render.setColor(panel)
            render.drawRect(16, y, 480, 46)
            label(29, y + 13, (i == mineDifficulty and "> " or "  ") .. option.name
                .. "  |  " .. option.count .. " mines", i == mineDifficulty and accent or ink)
        end
        label(22, 311, "No Guess: " .. (mineNoGuess and "On" or "Off"), mineNoGuess and accent or ink)
        label(22, 343, "No Guess checks for a solution using deductions.", muted, smallFont)
        label(22, 453, "Up/Down selects  |  Space toggles No Guess", ink, smallFont)
        label(22, 484, "Enter starts  |  Backspace / Ctrl Desktop", muted, smallFont)
        return
    end
    local banner = field.result
    if field.generating then banner = "Finding a No Guess board..."
    elseif field.searchFailed then banner = "No board found. Press Enter to search again." end
    label(22, 82, banner or ("Flags remaining: " .. (field.count - field.flagCount)
        .. (field.noGuess and "  |  No Guess" or "")), accent, smallFont)
    for cell = 1, MINE_CELLS do
        local x = 96 + (cell - 1) % MINE_SIDE * 40
        local y = 111 + math.floor((cell - 1) / MINE_SIDE) * 40
        local number = field.open[cell]
        local text, color = "", ink
        if field.result and field.mines[cell] then text, color = "*", alertColor
        elseif field.flags[cell] then text, color = "F", alertColor
        elseif number and number > 0 then text, color = tostring(number), accent end
        if cell == field.cursor then
            render.setColor(accent)
            render.drawRect(x, y, 38, 38)
        end
        render.setColor(number and background or panel)
        render.drawRect(x + 2, y + 2, 34, 34)
        label(x + 14, y + 10, text, color)
    end
    label(22, 453, "Arrows move  |  Enter reveals  |  F flags", ink, smallFont)
    label(22, 484, "R restarts  |  D difficulty  |  Backspace / Ctrl Desktop", muted, smallFont)
end

local function renderChessBoard(game)
    local side = game.mark == "X" and "White" or "Black"
    local state = "Waiting for another player..."
    if game.winner == "draw" then state = "Stalemate."
    elseif game.winner ~= "" then state = game.winner == game.mark and "Checkmate. You won!" or "Checkmate. You lost."
    elseif game.guest ~= "" then
        state = (game.turn == game.mark and "Your turn" or "Opponent's turn") .. (game.check and "  |  Check" or "")
    end
    label(22, 81, "Match #" .. game.id .. "  |  You are " .. side .. "  |  " .. state, accent, smallFont)
    for tile = 1, 64 do
        local square = orientChessSquare(tile, game.mark)
        local column = (tile - 1) % 8
        local row = math.floor((tile - 1) / 8)
        local x, y = 34 + column * 36, 110 + row * 38
        local piece = game.board:sub(square, square)
        if square == chessCursor or square == chessFrom then
            render.setColor(square == chessFrom and alertColor or accent)
            render.drawRect(x, y, 35, 37)
        end
        render.setColor((column + row) % 2 == 0 and panel or background)
        render.drawRect(x + 2, y + 2, 31, 33)
        if chessHints[square] then
            render.setColor(accent)
            render.drawRect(x + 12, y + 27, 11, 5)
        end
        if piece ~= "." then
            local white = piece == piece:upper()
            label(x + 9, y + 11, (white and "w" or "b") .. piece:upper(), white and ink or alertColor)
        end
        if column == 0 then label(17, y + 10, tostring(8 - math.floor((square - 1) / 8)), muted, smallFont) end
        if row == 7 then label(x + 14, y + 38, string.char(97 + (square - 1) % 8), muted, smallFont) end
    end
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(330, 110, 166, 306)
    label(340, 117, "Moves", accent, smallFont)
    local moves = game.moves or {}
    local last = #moves - chessMoveScroll
    local first = math.max(1, last - CHESS_MOVE_ROWS + 1)
    for i = first, last do
        local ply = (game.ply or #moves) - #moves + i
        local number = math.floor((ply + 1) / 2)
        local notation = number .. (ply % 2 == 1 and ". " or "... ") .. moves[i]
        label(340, 138 + (i - first) * 18, notation, ink, smallFont)
    end
    if #moves == 0 then label(340, 138, "No moves yet", muted, smallFont) end
    if #moves > CHESS_MOVE_ROWS then label(330, 424, "PgUp/PgDn scroll", muted, smallFont) end
    local controls = "Arrows move  |  Enter picks or moves  |  Backspace leaves"
    if chessPromotion then controls = "Promote to Q, R, B, or N. Backspace cancels."
    elseif game.rematchRequested then controls = "Rematch requested. Waiting for your opponent."
    elseif game.opponentRequested then controls = "Opponent requested a rematch. Press R to accept."
    elseif game.winner ~= "" then controls = "R requests a rematch  |  Backspace leaves" end
    label(22, 453, controls, ink, smallFont)
end

local function renderFleetGrid(board, x, showCursor, preview, previewColor)
    for cell = 1, 100 do
        local col, row = (cell - 1) % 10, math.floor((cell - 1) / 10)
        local left, top = x + col * 21, 153 + row * 21
        local tile = board:sub(cell, cell)
        render.setColor(preview and preview[cell] and previewColor or panel)
        render.drawRect(left, top, 19, 19)
        if showCursor and cell == battleshipCursor then
            render.setColor(accent)
            render.drawRect(left, top, 19, 2)
            render.drawRect(left, top + 17, 19, 2)
            render.drawRect(left, top, 2, 19)
            render.drawRect(left + 17, top, 2, 19)
        end
        if tile == "S" then label(left + 5, top + 1, "S", accent, smallFont)
        elseif tile == "H" then label(left + 5, top + 1, "X", alertColor, smallFont)
        elseif tile == "M" then label(left + 5, top + 1, "o", muted, smallFont) end
        if row == 0 then label(left + 5, 132, string.char(65 + col), muted, smallFont) end
        if col == 0 then label(x - 19, top + 1, tostring(row + 1), muted, smallFont) end
    end
end

local function renderBattleship(game)
    local state = "Waiting for another player..."
    local placing = game.ready == false
    if game.winner ~= "" then state = game.winner == game.mark and "You won!" or "Your fleet was sunk."
    elseif placing then
        if game.nextShip > 0 then state = "Place your fleet  |  " .. game.placed .. "/5"
        elseif game.guest == "" then state = "Fleet ready. Waiting for another player."
        else state = "Fleet ready. Waiting for your opponent." end
    elseif game.guest ~= "" then state = game.turn == game.mark and "Your turn" or "Opponent's turn" end
    label(22, 81, "Match #" .. game.id .. "  |  " .. state, accent, smallFont)
    label(39, 108, "Your fleet", ink, smallFont)
    label(285, 108, "Target grid", ink, smallFont)
    local preview, previewColor
    if placing and game.nextShip > 0 then
        preview = {}
        local step = battleshipVertical and 10 or 1
        local edge = battleshipVertical and math.floor((battleshipCursor - 1) / 10) or (battleshipCursor - 1) % 10
        local valid = edge + game.nextShip <= 10
        for i = 0, math.min(game.nextShip, 10 - edge) - 1 do
            local square = battleshipCursor + i * step
            preview[square] = true
            if game.fleet:sub(square, square) == "S" then valid = false end
        end
        previewColor = valid and accent or alertColor
    end
    renderFleetGrid(game.fleet, 37, placing, preview, previewColor)
    renderFleetGrid(game.target, 279, not placing)
    label(22, 382, "S ship  |  X hit  |  o miss", muted, smallFont)
    if placing then
        if game.nextShip > 0 then
            label(22, 409, "Next ship: " .. game.nextShip .. " cells  |  "
                .. (battleshipVertical and "Vertical" or "Horizontal"), ink, smallFont)
        end
    else
        local tracker = "Hits: " .. game.hits .. "/17"
        if game.sunk ~= nil then tracker = tracker .. "  |  Sunk: " .. game.sunk .. "/5" end
        label(289, 382, tracker, accent, smallFont)
        label(22, 409, game.lastShot, ink, smallFont)
    end
    local controls = "Arrows aim  |  Enter fires  |  Backspace leaves"
    if placing then controls = "Arrows move  |  Enter places  |  R rotates  |  U undoes"
    elseif game.rematchRequested then controls = "Rematch requested. Waiting for your opponent."
    elseif game.opponentRequested then controls = "Opponent requested a rematch. Press R to accept."
    elseif game.winner ~= "" then controls = "R requests a rematch  |  Backspace leaves" end
    label(22, 453, controls, ink, smallFont)
end

local function renderTrailGame(game)
    local state = game.trail
    if not state then
        label(22, 85, "Expedition #" .. game.id .. "  |  " .. #game.party .. "/4 players", accent)
        for i, name in ipairs(game.party) do label(22, 122 + (i - 1) * 29, name, ink) end
        label(22, 453, game.isHost and "S starts the expedition  |  Backspace leaves"
            or "Waiting for the host to start  |  Backspace leaves", muted, smallFont)
        return
    end
    local turn = game.canMove and "Your turn" or (game.controller .. "'s turn")
    label(22, 84, "Day " .. state.day .. "  |  " .. game.destination .. "  |  " .. turn, accent, smallFont)
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(16, 111, 480, 66)
    label(26, 119, state.miles .. " / 2,000 miles", accent)
    label(26, 146, "Food " .. state.food .. "  |  Health " .. math.max(0, state.health)
        .. "  |  Wagon " .. math.max(0, state.wagon) .. "  |  $" .. state.money, ink, smallFont)
    for i, line in ipairs(wrapText(state.event, 454, font)) do label(22, 193 + (i - 1) * 20, line, ink) end
    if state.result then
        label(22, 288, state.result, accent, titleFont)
        label(22, 453, game.voted and ("Rematch votes: " .. game.votes .. "/" .. #game.party)
            or "R votes for a new expedition  |  Backspace leaves", ink, smallFont)
        return
    end
    for i, choice in ipairs(game.choices) do
        local y = 266 + (i - 1) * 39
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(16, y, 480, 32)
        local selected = i == math.min(trailChoice, #game.choices)
        label(26, y + 7, (selected and "> " or "  ") .. choice, selected and accent or ink)
    end
    label(22, 453, "Up/Down selects  |  Enter acts on your turn  |  Backspace leaves", ink, smallFont)
end

local function renderMatchTab()
    if not currentGame then
        label(22, 86, "Matches", accent)
        if #matches == 0 then label(22, 124, "No matches yet.", muted) end
        local first = math.max(1, selectedMatch - 9)
        for i = first, math.min(#matches, first + 9) do
            local match = matches[i]
            local y = 117 + (i - first) * 31
            if i == selectedMatch then
                render.setColor(panel)
                render.drawRect(16, y - 2, 480, 27)
            end
            label(22, y, "#" .. match.id .. "  " .. match.host, i == selectedMatch and accent or ink)
            local state = match.open and "Open" or "In use"
            if match.players then state = match.players .. "/4  " .. state end
            label(379, y, state, muted, smallFont)
        end
        label(22, 453, "C creates a match  |  Up/Down selects  |  Enter joins", ink, smallFont)
    elseif gameTab == "chess" then
        renderChessBoard(currentGame)
    elseif gameTab == "battleship" then
        renderBattleship(currentGame)
    elseif gameTab == "trail" then
        renderTrailGame(currentGame)
    elseif gameTab == "pong" and currentGame.pong then
        local game, ball = currentGame, currentGame.pong
        local now = timer.curtime()
        local view = PHONE.pongView
        if not view or view.id ~= game.id or now - view.time > 0.5 then
            view = {id = game.id, time = now, left = ball.left, right = ball.right}
            PHONE.pongView = view
        end
        local blend = math.min(1, math.max(0, now - view.time) * 18)
        view.left = view.left + (ball.left - view.left) * blend
        view.right = view.right + (ball.right - view.right) * blend
        view.time = now
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(36, 120, 440, 300)
        render.setColor(muted)
        render.drawRect(255, 120, 2, 300)
        render.setColor(accent)
        render.drawRect(46, 120 + view.left - 32, 8, 64)
        render.drawRect(458, 120 + view.right - 32, 8, 64)
        render.setColor(ink)
        render.drawRect(36 + ball.x - 4, 120 + ball.y - 4, 8, 8)
        label(36, 87, game.host .. " " .. ball.a .. "  |  " .. ball.b .. " " .. game.guest, ink)
        local hint = game.guest == "" and "Waiting for another player"
            or (game.winner ~= "" and (game.winner == game.mark and "You won!  R rematch" or "You lost.  R rematch")
            or "Aim or use Up/Down  |  First to 7")
        label(36, 448, hint, accent)
    else
        local game = currentGame
        local opponent = game.mark == "X" and game.guest or game.host
        local turn = "Waiting for another player..."
        if game.winner == "draw" then turn = "Draw."
        elseif game.winner ~= "" then turn = game.winner == game.mark and "You won!" or "You lost."
        elseif opponent ~= "" then turn = game.turn == game.mark and "Your turn" or ("Waiting for " .. opponent) end
        label(22, 82, "Match #" .. game.id .. "  |  You are " .. game.mark, ink, smallFont)
        label(22, 107, turn, accent)
        for square = 1, 9 do
            local x = 121 + ((square - 1) % 3) * 91
            local y = 142 + math.floor((square - 1) / 3) * 91
            local mark = game.board:sub(square, square)
            render.setColor(panel)
            render.drawRect(x, y, 87, 87)
            label(x + 33, y + 24, mark == "." and tostring(square) or mark,
                mark == "." and muted or accent, titleFont)
        end
        local controls = "1–9 places a mark  |  Backspace leaves the match"
        if game.rematchRequested then controls = "Rematch requested. Waiting for your opponent."
        elseif game.opponentRequested then controls = "Opponent requested a rematch. Press R to accept."
        elseif game.winner ~= "" then controls = "R requests a rematch  |  Backspace leaves the match" end
        label(22, 453, controls, ink, smallFont)
    end
    local hint = currentGame and "Ctrl Desktop" or "Backspace / Ctrl Desktop"
    label(22, 484, gameStatus ~= "" and gameStatus or hint, muted, smallFont)
end

    label(22, 481, channelStatus ~= "" and channelStatus or "F2 closes Channels  |  Ctrl Desktop", muted, smallFont)
end

end

local function renderCalculator()
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(16, 85, 480, 52)
    local state = calculator
    local value = state.left and (string.format("%.10g", state.left) .. " " .. state.operator .. " " .. state.entry)
        or (state.entry ~= "" and state.entry or "0")
    label(26, 100, state.error or value, state.error and alertColor or ink)
    label(380, 151, "Clear  C", accent, smallFont)
    for i, key in ipairs(CALCULATOR_KEYS) do
        local x, y = 16 + (i - 1) % 6 * 80, 180 + math.floor((i - 1) / 6) * 47
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(x, y, 74, 41)
        local text = key == "Angle" and (state.radians and "RAD" or "DEG") or key

    end
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(369, 426, 127, 44)
    label(381, 438, "Desktop  Ctrl", accent, smallFont)
    local hint = calculator.streamGreeting and "Calc (short for calculator for anyone new to the stream)"
        or "Press E on buttons or use the Wiremod Keyboard."
    label(22, 483, hint, muted, smallFont)
end

local websiteFonts, websiteFontCount = {}, 0
local function websiteFont(page)
    if page.face == "" then return smallFont end
    local size = math.max(8, math.floor(page.size * 0.625 + 0.5))
    local key = page.face .. "|" .. size .. "|" .. page.weight
    if websiteFonts[key] then return websiteFonts[key] end
    if websiteFontCount >= 16 then return smallFont end
    local ok, face = pcall(render.createFont, page.face, size, page.weight)
    websiteFontCount = websiteFontCount + 1
    websiteFonts[key] = ok and face or smallFont
    return websiteFonts[key]
end

local function drawWebsiteFrame(frame, face, scroll)
    scroll = scroll or 0
    local scale = 0.625
    local left, top = 96, 132
    for _, command in ipairs(frame) do
        if type(command) == "table" then
            local kind, x, y, w, h, r, g, b, value = unpack(command, 1, 9)
            if type(x) == "number" and type(y) == "number" and type(w) == "number" and type(h) == "number"
                and x >= 0 and y >= 0 and w >= 0 and h >= 0 and x + w <= 512 and y + h <= 1023
                and type(r) == "number" and r >= 0 and r <= 255
                and type(g) == "number" and g >= 0 and g <= 255
                and type(b) == "number" and b >= 0 and b <= 255 then
                y = y - scroll
                local color = Color(r, g, b)
                if kind == "rect" then
                    local first, last = math.max(0, y), math.min(512, y + h)
                    if last > first then
                        render.setColor(color)
                        render.drawRect(left + x * scale, top + first * scale, w * scale, (last - first) * scale)
                    end
                elseif kind == "text" and y >= 0 and y + h <= 512 and type(value) == "string" and #value <= 96 then
                    for _, line in ipairs(wrapText(value, w * scale, face)) do
                        label(left + x * scale, top + y * scale, line, color, face)
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local function websiteDemoFrame(page, now)
    local progress = (now % 4) / 4
    local frame = {
        {"rect", 0, 0, 512, 512, 19, 27, 42},
        {"rect", 0, 0, 512, 82, 33, 54, 77},
        {"text", 24, 24, 464, 36, 234, 245, 255, page.title},
        {"rect", 24, 330, 464, 102, 33, 54, 77},
        {"rect", 40, 391, 432, 15, 19, 27, 42},
        {"rect", 40, 391, 432 * progress, 15, 56, 200, 223},
        {"rect", 40 + 400 * progress, 372, 32, 12, 255, 187, 98},
        {"text", 40, 340, 432, 30, 198, 222, 239, "Live animation"},
        {"text", 24, 462, 464, 32, 198, 222, 239, "Uptime: " .. math.floor(now) .. "s"}
    }
    for i, line in ipairs(page.lines) do
        frame[#frame + 1] = {"text", 24, 105 + (i - 1) * 40, 464, 36, 198, 222, 239, line}
    end
    return frame
end

local function websiteDonationFrame(page)
    local frame = {
        {"rect", 0, 0, 512, 512, 19, 27, 42},
        {"rect", 0, 0, 512, 82, 33, 54, 77},
        {"text", 24, 24, 464, 36, 234, 245, 255, page.title},
        {"text", 48, 105, 416, 32, 198, 222, 239, page.lines[1] or ""},
        {"text", 48, 401, 416, 32, 198, 222, 239, "Press E on an amount to donate."},
        {"text", 48, 440, 416, 32, 198, 222, 239, "Confirm in the in-game payment prompt."}
    }
    for i, amount in ipairs(page.amounts) do
        if amount > 0 then
            local y = 154 + (i - 1) * 74
            frame[#frame + 1] = {"rect", 48, y, 416, 56, 29, 100, 98}
            frame[#frame + 1] = {"text", 64, y + 14, 380, 32, 234, 245, 255, "Donate $" .. amount}
        end
    end
    return frame
end


function websiteControls.clickChrome(x,y)
    if x >= 16 and x < 90 and y >= 132 and y < 268 then
        local row=math.floor((y-132)/34)
        local page=websitePages[selectedWebsite]
        if row==0 then websiteControls.back()
        elseif row==1 then websiteControls.bookmark()
        elseif row==2 then
            websiteControls.loadBookmarks()
            websiteControls.showBookmarks=not websiteControls.showBookmarks
        else websiteControls.retry() end
        return true
    end
    if websiteControls.showBookmarks then
        if x>=96 and x<416 and y>=167 and y<439 then
            local first=math.max(1,websiteControls.bookmarkSelection-7)
            local entry=websiteControls.bookmarks[first+math.floor((y-167)/34)]
            if entry then websiteControls.go(entry.name,entry.route,true,entry.id) end
        end
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function websiteControls.draw(page)
    websiteControls.loadBookmarks()
    websiteControls.startedAt = websiteControls.startedAt or timer.curtime()
    if websiteControls.waitUntil and timer.curtime() >= websiteControls.waitUntil then
        websiteControls.waitUntil = nil
        websiteControls.message = "No action response. Check the site before trying again."
    end
    if not page and not websiteLoaded and not websiteControls.discoveryError and timer.curtime()-websiteControls.startedAt>10 then
        websiteControls.message = "Discovery timed out. Use Retry."
    end
    for i, text in ipairs({"Back","Save","Favorites","Retry"}) do
        local y=132+(i-1)*34
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(16,y,74,28)
        label(21,y+6,text,accent,smallFont)
    end
    if page and page.route then label(22,61,"/"..page.route,muted,smallFont) end
    if page and page.extensions and not websiteControls.showBookmarks then
        local values=websiteControls.values(page)
        for _, field in ipairs(page.extensions.inputs) do
            local y=field[2]-(page.scroll or 0)
            if y>=0 and y+field[4]<=512 then
                local x,top,w,h=96+field[1]*0.625,132+y*0.625,field[3]*0.625,field[4]*0.625
                render.setColor(websiteControls.focus==field[6] and accent or panel)
                render.drawRect(x,top,w,h)
                render.setColor(background)
                render.drawRect(x+2,top+2,math.max(0,w-4),math.max(0,h-4))
                local text=values[field[6]]
                if text=="" then text=field[5] end
                render.setFont(smallFont)
                while #text>0 and render.getTextSize(text)>w-10 do text=text:sub(2) end
                label(x+5,top+math.max(0,(h-16)/2),text,ink,smallFont)
            end
        end
        for _, link in ipairs(page.extensions.links) do
            local y=link[2]-(page.scroll or 0)
            if y>=0 and y+link[4]<=512 then
                render.setColor(panel)
                render.drawRect(96+link[1]*0.625,132+y*0.625,link[3]*0.625,link[4]*0.625)
                label(100+link[1]*0.625,136+y*0.625,link[5],accent,smallFont)
            end
        end
        if #page.extensions.missing>0 then
            websiteControls.message="Unsupported features: "..table.concat(page.extensions.missing,", ")
        end
    end
    if websiteControls.showBookmarks then
        render.setColor(background)
        render.drawRect(96,132,320,320)
        label(108,140,"Favorites (K closes)",accent,smallFont)
        local first=math.max(1,websiteControls.bookmarkSelection-7)
        for i=first,math.min(#websiteControls.bookmarks,first+7) do
            local entry=websiteControls.bookmarks[i]
            label(104,173+(i-first)*34,(i==websiteControls.bookmarkSelection and "> " or "")..
                entry.name.."/"..entry.route,ink,smallFont)
        end
        if #websiteControls.bookmarks==0 then label(108,190,"No saved sites yet.",muted,smallFont) end
    end
    if page and not page.demo and not page.donation and not websiteControls.showBookmarks then
        local elapsed=timer.curtime()-(page.frameAt or page.loadedAt or timer.curtime())
        if elapsed>5 then
            label(104,416,"Site is not responding. Use Retry.",alertColor,smallFont)
        elseif not page.frameAt then
            label(104,416,"Waiting for site graphics...",muted,smallFont)
        end
    end
end

local function renderWebsite()
    local page = websitePages[selectedWebsite]
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(16, 84, 480, 34)
    label(28, 91, "<", #websitePages > 1 and accent or muted)
    label(465, 91, ">", #websitePages > 1 and accent or muted)
    local siteLabel = websiteLoaded and "No website chips found" or "Looking for website chips..."
    if page then
        siteLabel = page.name .. "  #" .. page.id .. "  (" .. selectedWebsite .. "/" .. #websitePages .. ")"
    end
    label(64, 93, siteLabel, ink, smallFont)
    if page and page.donation then
        drawWebsiteFrame(websiteDonationFrame(page), websiteFont(page))
    elseif page and page.demo then
        drawWebsiteFrame(websiteDemoFrame(page, math.max(0, timer.curtime() - page.startedAt)), websiteFont(page))
    elseif page and websiteFrameSite == page.id and websiteFrame then
        drawWebsiteFrame(websiteFrame, websiteFont(page), page.scroll)
    elseif page and page.title then
        render.setColor(Color(19, 27, 42))
        render.drawRect(40, 143, 432, 270)
        render.setColor(Color(33, 54, 77))
        render.drawRect(40, 143, 432, 55)
        for i, line in ipairs(wrapText(page.title, 396, websiteFont(page))) do
            label(58, 149 + (i - 1) * 21, line, Color(234, 245, 255), websiteFont(page))
        end
        for i, line in ipairs(page.lines) do
            for j, part in ipairs(wrapText(line, 396, websiteFont(page))) do
                label(58, 208 + (i - 1) * 38 + (j - 1) * 17, part, Color(198, 222, 239), websiteFont(page))
            end
        end
    end
    websiteControls.draw(page)
    label(22, 460, "animations and other features might be laggy and or not work", alertColor, smallFont)
    if page and not page.title then label(64, 162, "Loading selected site...", muted, smallFont) end
    local hint = websiteControls.focus and "Typing | Enter submits | Tab next field | Esc stops"
        or "I/Tab field | H Back | B Save | K Favorites | R Retry"
    if page and page.height and page.height > 512 then
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(426, 132, 54, 34)
        render.drawRect(426, 168, 54, 34)
        label(438, 140, "Up", accent, smallFont)
        label(434, 176, "Down", accent, smallFont)
        hint = "Up/Down scrolls  |  Left/Right switches sites"
    elseif page and page.donation then
        hint = "Press E on an amount or use 1-3."
    end
    if websiteControls.message ~= "" then hint = websiteControls.message end
    label(22, 483, hint, websiteControls.resultStatus == "error" and alertColor or muted, smallFont)
end

local function renderChangelog()
    if updates.latest then
        label(22, 61, "Update: github.com/Mooseyboi30/MaxwellOS", accent, smallFont)
    end
    local entry = CHANGELOG[changelogPage]
    if renderedChangelogPage ~= changelogPage then
        changelogRows = wrapText(entry[2], 440, font)
        renderedChangelogPage = changelogPage
    end
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(16, 86, 480, 310)
    label(32, 105, entry[1], accent, titleFont)
    for i, line in ipairs(changelogRows) do
        label(32, 161 + (i - 1) * 22, line, ink)
    end
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(16, 414, 120, 40)
    render.drawRect(376, 414, 120, 40)
    label(30, 425, "< Older", changelogPage < #CHANGELOG and accent or muted)
    label(390, 425, "Newer >", changelogPage > 1 and accent or muted)
    label(220, 426, changelogPage .. " / " .. #CHANGELOG, muted, smallFont)
    label(22, 483, "Left/Right or PgUp/PgDn changes release  |  Ctrl Desktop", muted, smallFont)
end

local function renderAppsPanel()
    if MULTIPLAYER_TABS[gameTab] and activeAlert and timer.curtime() < alertUntil then
        label(22, 46, activeAlert, alertColor, smallFont)
    end
    if PHONE.tools.handles(gameTab) then PHONE.tools.draw(gameTab)
    elseif gameTab == "menu" then renderGameMenu()
    elseif gameTab == "calculator" then renderCalculator()
    elseif gameTab == "website" then renderWebsite()
    elseif type(gameTab)=="string" and gameTab:sub(1,4)=="mos:" then PHONE.appHost.draw()
    elseif gameTab == "maker" then PHONE.maker.draw()
    elseif gameTab == "files" then PHONE.files.draw()
    elseif gameTab == "paint" then PHONE.paint.draw()
    elseif gameTab == "credits" or gameTab == "music" then PHONE.extras.draw(gameTab)
    elseif gameTab == "changelog" then renderChangelog()
    elseif gameTab == "settings" then
        render.setColor(panel)
        for i,page in ipairs({"color","name","wallpaper"}) do
            local x=16+(i-1)*160
            render.drawRect(x,72,152,26)
            label(x+10,77,page=="color" and "Color" or (page=="name" and "Computer name" or "Wallpaper"),
                settings.page==page and accent or muted,smallFont)
            render.setColor(panel)
        end
        if settings.page == "wallpaper" then
            PHONE.wallpaper.drawSettings()
        elseif settings.page == "color" then
            renderThemeSetup()
        else
            label(22, 122, "Current name: " .. computerName, ink)
            render.setColor(panel)
            render.drawRect(16, 161, 480, 46)
            render.drawRect(16, 226, 160, 42)
            label(28, 174, settings.name .. "_", accent)
            label(28, 236, "Save name", accent)
            label(22, 285, settings.storageError and "Local profile could not be saved." or settings.message, muted, smallFont)
            label(22, 483, "Left/Right tabs  |  Type a name  |  Enter saves", muted, smallFont)
        end
    elseif gameTab == "hud" then
        label(22, 85, "Chat overlay", accent, titleFont)
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(16, 134, 480, 59)
        label(28, 154, showChatHUD and "HUD on" or "HUD off", accent)
        label(22, 217, "Connect to the linked Starfall HUD to see room chat.", muted, smallFont)
        label(22, 244, "Press Enter or aim at the toggle and press E.", muted, smallFont)
        label(381, 438, "Back to Apps", accent, smallFont)
        label(22, 483, "Backspace returns to Apps.", muted, smallFont)
    elseif gameTab == "contracts" then PHONE.contracts.draw()
    elseif gameTab == "payments" then renderPayments()
    elseif gameTab == "squadron" then PHONE.squadron.draw()
    elseif gameTab == "mines" then renderMines()
    else renderMatchTab() end
end

function PHONE.drawComputerScreen()
    if not poweredOn then
        render.setColor(Color(9, 13, 19))
        render.drawRect(0, 0, 512, 512)
        label(22, 449, "Computer off", muted)
        label(22, 479, "Set the Wiremod Power input to 1.", muted, smallFont)
        return
    end
    local now = timer.curtime()
    local crashRemaining = startupUntil - now - themes.startupDuration
    if crashRemaining > 0 then
        render.setColor(Color(7, 15, 25))
        render.drawRect(0, 0, 512, 512)
        label(28, 114, "SYSTEM CRASH", Color(255, 120, 125), titleFont)
        local detail = "Calculator exception: division by zero."
        local visible = math.floor(#detail * math.min(1, (2.5 - crashRemaining) / 1.2))
        label(28, 178, detail:sub(1, visible), Color(230, 241, 250), smallFont)
        label(28, 224, "Restarting in " .. math.ceil(crashRemaining) .. "s", Color(155, 176, 198), smallFont)
        return
    end
    if now < startupUntil then
        local progress = 1 - (startupUntil - now) / themes.startupDuration
        render.setColor(background)
        render.drawRect(0, 0, 512, 512)
        label(72, 200, "Starting computer", ink, titleFont)
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(72, 260, 368, 10)
        if progress > 0 then
            render.setColor(accent)
            render.drawRect(72, 260, 368 * progress, 10)
        end
        return
    end
    render.clear(background)
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(0, 0, 512, 72)
    if isOwner and not themeConfigured then
        renderThemeSetup()
        return
    end
    if isOwner and computerName == "" then
        label(22, 10, "Computer setup", ink, titleFont)
        label(22, 47, "Choose a computer name", muted, smallFont)
        label(22, 111, "Use 1–24 letters, numbers, hyphens or underscores.", ink, smallFont)
        render.setColor(panel)
        render.drawRect(16, 147, 480, 50)
        label(26, 163, nameDraft .. "_", accent)
        label(22, 483, status ~= "" and status or
            ((keyboardActive or PHONE.open or PHONE.controlsLocked) and "Type a name and press Enter." or "Look at the Wiremod Keyboard and press E."), muted, smallFont)
        return
    end
    if gameTab=="menu" and isOwner then renderGameMenu(); return end
    if room == "" and not gameTab then
        render.drawRect(16, 423, 480, 48)
    elseif not gameTab and not showPayments and not showPrivate and not showChannels then
        render.drawRect(16, 372, 480, 102)
    end
    local title = room ~= "" and room:sub(1, 15) or "Chatroom"
    if type(gameTab)=="string" and gameTab:sub(1,4)=="mos:" then
        title=PHONE.appHost.active and PHONE.appHost.active.name or "Application"
    elseif gameTab == "menu" then title = "Desktop"
    elseif gameTab == "calculator" then title = "Calculator"
    elseif gameTab == "website" then title = "Website Viewer"
    elseif gameTab == "files" then title = "Files"
    elseif gameTab == "maker" then title = "Website Maker"
    elseif gameTab == "contracts" then title = "Contracts"
    elseif gameTab == "paint" then title = "Paint"
    elseif gameTab == "credits" then title = "Credits"
    elseif gameTab == "music" then title = "HL2 Music Player"
    elseif gameTab == "changelog" then title = "Changelog"
    elseif PHONE.tools.handles(gameTab) then title=({manager="App Manager",notifications="Notifications",profile="Profile Transfer",dashboard="Wire Dashboard",windows="Windows"})[gameTab]
    elseif gameTab == "settings" then title = "Settings"
    elseif gameTab == "hud" then title = "HUD"
    elseif gameTab == "tic" then title = "Tic-Tac-Toe"
    elseif gameTab == "squadron" then title = "Star Squadron"
    elseif gameTab == "mines" then title = "Minesweeper"
    elseif gameTab == "chess" then title = "Chess"
    elseif gameTab == "battleship" then title = "Battleship"
    elseif gameTab == "pong" then title = "Pong"
    elseif gameTab == "trail" then title = "Oregon Trail"
    elseif showPayments then title = "Payments"
    elseif showPrivate then title = "Private messages"
    elseif showChannels then title = "Channels" end
    label(22, 10, title, ink, titleFont)
    if PHONE.controlsLocked and not PHONE.open then
        label(22, 28, "Alt releases keyboard", muted, smallFont)
    end

    if not isOwner then
        label(22, 46, "This terminal belongs to another player.", muted, smallFont)
        return
    end
    if updates.latest then
        local notice = "Go to GitHub for v" .. updates.latest
        render.setFont(smallFont)
        label(496 - render.getTextSize(notice), 46, notice, alertColor, smallFont)
    else
        label(376, 46, "Computer v" .. CHIP_VERSION, muted, smallFont)
    end
    if room ~= "" then
        if not gameTab and not showPayments and not showPrivate then
            render.setColor(background)
            render.drawRect(265, 8, 100, 31)
            label(276, 15, "F2 Channels", accent, smallFont)
        end
        if showChannels then
            renderChannels()
            return
        end
        if showPrivate then
            renderPrivate()
            return
        end
    end
    if not joining and not versionWarning then
        render.setColor(background)
        render.drawRect(369, 8, 127, 31)
        if gameTab == "menu" then
            render.drawRect(236, 8, 129, 31)
            label(247, 15, "F3 Chatrooms", accent, smallFont)
        end
        local shortcut = gameTab == "menu" and "Ctrl Last app" or "Ctrl Desktop"
        label(380, 15, shortcut, accent, smallFont)
        if gameTab then
            renderAppsPanel()
            return
        end
    end
    local focused = keyboardActive or PHONE.open or PHONE.controlsLocked
    if room == "" then
        if versionWarning then
            local warning = versionStatus(versionWarning.version)
            label(22, 46, "Version mismatch", muted, smallFont)
            label(22, 90, versionWarning.name .. "  #" .. versionWarning.id, ink)
            label(22, 119, warning, accent, smallFont)
            label(22, 148, "Some features may behave differently.", muted, smallFont)
            label(22, 189, "Get matching versions:", ink, smallFont)
            label(22, 214, "https://github.com/Mooseyboi30/MaxwellOS", accent, smallFont)
            label(26, 439, "Enter continues  |  F2 goes back", ink)
            return
        end
        label(22, 46, joining and "Enter server password" or "Select a server", muted, smallFont)
        if joining then
            label(22, 90, joining.name .. "  #" .. joining.id, ink)
            local version = versionStatus(joining.version)
            label(22, 119, version, muted, smallFont)
            label(26, 439, "Password  " .. string.rep("*", math.min(32, #password)), accent)
        else
            if #directory == 0 then
                label(22, 90, directoryReceived and "No matching chatroom servers found." or "Looking for chatroom servers...", muted)
                if directoryReceived then
                    label(22, 119, directoryDetail, muted, smallFont)
                end
            end
            local first = math.max(1, selected - PICKER_ROWS + 1)
            for i = first, math.min(first + PICKER_ROWS - 1, #directory) do
                local entry = directory[i]
                local active = i == selected
                local y = 88 + (i - first) * 26
                if active then
                    render.setColor(panel)
                    render.drawRect(16, y - 3, 480, 24)
                end
                label(22, y, (active and "> " or "  ") .. entry.name:sub(1, 22) .. "  #" .. entry.id,
                    active and accent or ink)
                local version = versionStatus(entry.version)
                label(338, y, version, muted, smallFont)
            end
            label(26, 439, focused and "Up/Down selects   Enter opens" or "Use the wired keyboard to select", muted, smallFont)
        end
        local hint = joining and "Enter joins  |  F2 goes back  |  Alt exits" or "Ctrl Desktop  |  Alt exits"
        label(22, 483, status ~= "" and status or (focused and hint or "Look at the Wiremod Keyboard and press E"), muted, smallFont)
        return
    end
    if activeAlert and timer.curtime() < alertUntil then
        label(22, 46, activeAlert, alertColor, smallFont)
    else
        label(22, 46, "# " .. channelName .. "  |  " .. versionStatus(roomVersion), accent, smallFont)
    end

    if PHONE.paint.preview then
        local preview = PHONE.paint.preview
        render.setColor(PHONE.paint.palette[8])
        render.drawRect(81, 92, 350, 350)
        for y, runs in pairs(preview.runs) do
            for _, run in ipairs(runs) do
                render.setColor(PHONE.paint.palette[run[3]])
                render.drawRect(81 + (run[1] - 1) * 7, 92 + (y - 1) * 7, run[2] * 7, 7)
            end
        end
        label(22, 464, preview.author .. " | F7 or Backspace to close", ink)
        return
    end
    if #rows == 0 then label(22, 91, "No messages yet.", muted) end
    local last = #rows - scroll
    local first = math.max(1, last - ROWS_VISIBLE + 1)
    for i = first, last do
        local row = rows[i]
        local y = 88 + (i - first) * 22
        if row.thumbnail then
            for j = 0, 63 do
                local color = PHONE.paint.colorIndex(row.thumbnail:byte(j + 1))
                render.setColor(PHONE.paint.palette[color == 0 and 8 or color])
                render.drawRect(22 + j % 8 * 2, y + math.floor(j / 8) * 2, 2, 2)
            end
        end
        label(row.thumbnail and 46 or 22, y, row.text, row.alert and alertColor or (row.mention and accent or ink), row.face)
    end

    if PHONE.typingNames and PHONE.typingNames ~= "" then
        local names = PHONE.typingNames
        local firstName, others = names:match("^([^ ]+) %+(%d+)$")
        local who = firstName and (firstName .. " and " .. others .. " more are") or (names .. " is")
        local line = wrapText(who .. " typing in #" .. channelName, 468, smallFont)[1]
        label(22, 64, line, muted, smallFont)
    end
    local prompt = focused and ("> " .. draft .. "_")
        or (draft ~= "" and draft or "Use the wired keyboard to type")
    if prompt ~= lastPrompt then
        promptRows = wrapText(prompt, 330, emojiFont)
        lastPrompt = prompt
    end
    local firstPrompt = math.max(1, #promptRows - COMPOSER_ROWS + 1)
    for i = firstPrompt, #promptRows do
        label(26, 378 + (i - firstPrompt) * 18, promptRows[i], focused and accent or muted, emojiFont)
    end
    local hint = focused and "Ctrl Desktop  |  F2 Channels  |  F5 PM  |  F6 Leave"
        or "Look at the Wiremod Keyboard and press E"
    label(22, 483, status ~= "" and status or hint, muted, smallFont)
end
PHONE.baseDrawComputerScreen=PHONE.drawComputerScreen
function PHONE.drawComputerScreen()
    PHONE.baseDrawComputerScreen()
    if PHONE.tools then
        if not PHONE.tools.inWindowRender and poweredOn and timer.curtime()>=startupUntil and themeConfigured and computerName~="" and (gameTab=="menu" or gameTab=="windows") then PHONE.tools.drawTaskbar() end
        PHONE.tools.drawToast()
    end
end

hook.add("render", "chatroom_screen", function()
    if isOwner and poweredOn and timer.curtime() >= startupUntil and gameTab == "pong"
        and currentGame and currentGame.pong and currentGame.guest ~= "" and currentGame.winner == ""
        and timer.curtime() >= (PHONE.pongKeyboardUntil or 0)
        and (tonumber(roomVersion) or 0) >= 35.7 then
        local now = timer.curtime()
        if now >= (PHONE.pongAimAt or 0) then
            PHONE.pongAimAt = now + 0.10
            local _, y = render.cursorPos()
            if y then
                local target = math.max(32, math.min(268, math.floor(y - 120 + 0.5)))
                local ball = currentGame.pong
                local paddle = currentGame.mark == "X" and ball.left or ball.right
                if math.abs(target - paddle) >= 2 then sendGameAction(4, 100 + target) end
            end
        end
    end
    PHONE.drawComputerScreen()
    if isOwner and PHONE.screenMouseActive() and player():getEyeTrace().Entity == PHONE.mouseScreen then
        local x, y = render.cursorPos()
        if x and y and x >= 0 and x < 512 and y >= 0 and y < 512 then
            render.setColor(Color(0, 0, 0))
            render.drawRect(x - 2, y - 9, 5, 19)
            render.drawRect(x - 9, y - 2, 19, 5)
            render.setColor(Color(255, 255, 255))
            render.drawRect(x, y - 8, 1, 17)
            render.drawRect(x - 8, y, 17, 1)
        end
    end
end)

hook.add("drawhud", "chatroom_phone", function()
    if not isOwner or not PHONE.open then return end
    if not render.renderTargetExists("maxwellos_phone") then render.createRenderTarget("maxwellos_phone") end
    local x, y, scale, face, fw, fh = PHONE.geometry()
    render.selectRenderTarget("maxwellos_phone")
    render.clear(background)
    PHONE.drawComputerScreen()
    render.selectRenderTarget()

    render.setColor(Color(6, 9, 14, 245))
    render.drawRect(x, y, fw, fh)
    render.setColor(panel)
    render.drawRect(x + 5, y + 5, fw - 10, fh - 10)
    render.setColor(accent)
    render.drawRect(x + 12, y + 41, face, 3)
    label(x + 17, y + 15, "POCKET COMPUTER", ink, smallFont)
    render.setColor(Color(115, 46, 49))
    render.drawRect(x + fw - 43, y + 11, 26, 26)
    label(x + fw - 35, y + 14, "X", ink, smallFont)

    render.setColor(Color(255, 255, 255))
    render.setRenderTargetTexture("maxwellos_phone")
    render.drawTexturedRectUV(x + 14, y + 46, face, face, 0, 0, 0.5, 0.5)
    render.setColor(accent)
    render.drawRect(x + fw / 2 - 26, y + fh - 9, 52, 3)
    if PHONE.lockNotice ~= "" and timer.curtime() < PHONE.lockNoticeUntil then
        label(x + 18, y + face + 64, PHONE.lockNotice, alertColor, smallFont)
    else
        label(x + 18, y + face + 64,
            PHONE.controlsLocked and "Alt unlocks | X closes" or "Shift+P to close", muted, smallFont)
        label(x + fw - 108, y + face + 64,
            PHONE.unread > 0 and ("PM " .. PHONE.unread .. "  OPEN") or "PM  OPEN",
            PHONE.unread > 0 and alertColor or accent, smallFont)
    end
    -- Toggle is deliberately separate from the screen so it stays accessible
    -- even while other apps (including chat and private messages) are open.
    render.setColor(PHONE.controlsLocked and Color(27, 92, 72) or Color(46, 59, 73))
    render.drawRect(x + 14, y + face + 82, fw - 28, 30)
    label(x + 22, y + face + 88,
        PHONE.controlsLocked and "LOCK CONTROLS: ON" or "LOCK CONTROLS: OFF",
        PHONE.controlsLocked and accent or ink, smallFont)
end)

hook.add("drawhud", "chatroom_current_chat", function()
    if not poweredOn or timer.curtime() < startupUntil or not isOwner or not showChatHUD or room == "" then return end
    local title = "Room: " .. room .. "  |  # " .. channelName
    local first = math.max(1, #rows - 5)
    local count = math.max(0, #rows - first + 1)
    render.setColor(background)
    render.drawRect(16, 16, 500, 34 + count * 19)
    render.setColor(accent)
    render.drawRect(16, 16, 3, 34 + count * 19)
    label(28, 24, title, accent, smallFont)
    for i = first, #rows do
        local row = rows[i]
        label(28, 47 + (i - first) * 19, row.text, row.alert and alertColor or (row.mention and accent or ink), row.face)
    end
end)


hook.add("drawhud", "maxwellos_phone_pm", function()
    if not isOwner or not poweredOn or PHONE.open or timer.curtime() >= PHONE.alertUntil or PHONE.tools.dnd() then return end
    label(22, 155, "New private message  |  Shift+P opens phone", accent, smallFont)
end)
